# Parity Gaps — ranked by gameplay impact

_Generated 2026-11-19. 597 registry leads with an open or unknown dimension, highest score first._

Score weights logic & liveness highest, then values/timing, then presentation/audio, scaled by category. These are recorded assessments. Recheck the current caller before treating a lead as a defect. For a mixed row, only the explicitly open dimensions contribute to its score. Design decisions and unscored caveats follow the table.

| # | score | unit | feature | worst | gaps |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## Evidence to reconcile

These rows record design decisions, mismatched constants, or caveats outside the ranked table. Check each claim against the pinned source, the current caller, and the decision rationale before assigning work.

- `assault.objective.decrease_and_score` — QC centerprints the destroyed objective's .message to all players (if set); the port has no objective .message broadcast (tracked under assault.objective.advance_chain). The PlayHitsound forced-hitsound for a wall victim is now its own row (assault.notify.play_hitsound, dead).
- `assault.objective.advance_chain` — QC centerprints the destroyed objective's .message to all players (if set); the port has no objective .message broadcast. Unchanged.
- `assault.objective.waypoint_sprite` — The prior audit's 'no client render path' gap is CLOSED (Wave 17, 2f0bfdb) — it had grepped src/VortexArena.Net (no net code there; the net layer is game/net/). The visible result is rendered by a faithful QC port of Draw_WaypointSprite but was NOT runtime-observed in this code-read re-audit (game not launched); a live visual check would be the only remaining upgrade.
- `assault.mapobject.assault_wall` — Constant think_interval (0.2 s (nextthink = time + 0.2) / per-frame (DriveWalls runs every tick from DriveFrame))
- `assault.config.warmup_incompatible` — ReadyRestart_Deny (the QC round-2 gate) is structurally unneeded in the port (round 2 is driven by the in-Assault SecondRoundPending timer, not a ready-restart), so it is not modeled — but its functional effect (no manual ready-restart mid-Assault) is achieved by sv_ready_restart_after_countdown=0. The QC assault_turrets_teamswap_forbidden flag (set in ReadyRestart_Deny under campaign) is mirrored by the campaign guard in AssaultTurretRoundstart.
- `assault.notify.play_hitsound` — Minor (parity-neutral): the hook's RETURN VALUE is deliberately discarded at the firing site (DamageSystem.cs:351-353) — the port's HITSOUND_DAMAGE_DEALT_TOTAL accrual (line 353) already runs for ANY non-self victim a player hits, so a func_assault_destructible already produces a hitsound regardless of the hook. In Base the PlayHitsound hook is ORed into the hitsound-firing condition (which otherwise gates on player/turret/monster victims); the port's stat path is more permissive, so the observable effect (the wall beeps) matches Base, but the hook is consulted for reachability/parity rather than being load-bearing. Wiring the return into a victim-type gate would make it strictly load-bearing as Base.
- `bot-ai.population.namemodel` — Only the two fire-relevant skill-modifier columns (argv11 bot_aggresskill, argv13 bot_aimskill) are read; the other 10 of bots.txt's 12 skill columns are folded into the single BotSkill knob (tracked under bot-ai.skill.modifiers).
- `bot-ai.think.throttle` — Constant skill-based ping (bound(0,0.07-0.005*skill+rand*0.01,0.65) / not simulated (INTENDED DIVERGENCE — see bot-ai.aim.latency))
- `bot-ai.aim.lead` — Constant trajectory search (10 traces, pitch += 0.1, tracetoss onto faked target / closed-form 0.5*g*t^2 drop, 45-deg range guard)
- `bot-ai.weapon.choose` — No bot_rangepreference shift, so per-bot 'sniper vs spammer' weapon-range personalities are absent (the close/far distance thresholds are the same 300/850 for every bot). (UNCHANGED.) Constant bot_rangepreference (2^pref distance shift) (per-bot / not implemented)
- `bot-ai.weapon.wr_aim` — Only the ChosenWeapon's wr_aim is consulted — there is no MAX_WEAPONSLOTS per-slot loop (Base runs wr_aim for every owned weapon slot each think). This is now the SOLE remaining wr_aim gap. (UNCHANGED.) lastfiredweapon combo bookkeeping is not modeled; combos that depend on the previously-fired weapon don't trigger (the weapon.choose combo-hold covers the common splash-follow-up case). Constant weapon slots driven (MAX_WEAPONSLOTS (all), each wr_aim / 1 (the ChosenWeapon's wr_aim hooks only))
- `bot-ai.roles.goalrating` — rangebias: the generic DM role still passes flat 2000 for items/enemies/roam rather than QC's per-rating-call bias (objective roles like CTS now do vary it, e.g. 5000). Minor scaling divergence only — the path-cost metric itself is now faithful. (NARROWED 2026-06-28 fourth pass.) (CLOSED 2026-07-11 follow-up) enemyplayers Strength/Shield aggression nudges — WIRED via StatusEffectsCatalog.GetTime (skill>3, ±0.5/±0.2/-0.4 per QC roles.qc:203-210). (CLOSED 2026-07-11 follow-up) per-weapon bot_pickupbasevalue — every Weapon def now carries the QC 'rating' ATTRIB value (Weapon.BotPickupBaseValue) with the arsenal discount (c = 1 - Σowned/20000·0.5) and the full ammo_pickupevalfunc (per-ammo m_botvalue × need + 10% weapon base; ammo for unowned weapons rates 0). (CLOSED 2026-07-11 follow-up) underwater no-goal shore-waypoint push — BotBrain.TryPushShoreWaypoint (QC havocbot_ai:68-100), run in the token block for a SWIMMING goal-less bot. Constant rangebias (items/enemies/roam) (per-rating-call (e.g. items 2000, enemies BOT_RATING_ENEMY) / objective roles vary it (CTS 5000); the GENERIC DM role still passes flat 2000 for items/enemies/roam)
- `bot-ai.combat.dodge` — Constant bot_dodge producer coverage (20 files / 31 setters (projectiles, beams, monsters, nades, hook, turret) / 15 files (Arc/Blaster/Crylink/Devastator/Electro/Fireball/Hagar/Hlac/Minelayer/Mortar/OkRpc/Porto/Seeker/Vaporizer projectiles + PhaserTurret beam); all weapon producers, missing monsters/napalm/hook/generic-turret)
- `bot-ai.special.jetpack_rocketjump` — Constant bot_ai_navigation_jetpack (0 / n/a (jetpack NAV still unported)) Constant bot_ai_navigation_jetpack_mindistance (3500 / n/a)
- `bot-ai.move.keyboard` — QC stores the latched keyboard move × sv_maxspeed; the port latches the normalized -1..1 move and applies maxspeed in the Steer caller — equivalent result, slightly different intermediate representation (cosmetic, not behavioral).
- `bot-ai.skill.modifiers` — Recorded divergence — The port deliberately folds the bulk of QC's 12 randomized per-bot skill modifier columns into a single BotSkill knob, carrying only the facets it can apply faithfully: bot_moveskill (bunnyhop/keyboard gate), bot_aggresskill (argv11, fire decision) and bot_aimskill (argv13, fire-cone factor). This keeps the same overall skill axis without simulating every per-facet variance; a population is more homogeneous than stock but skill scaling of aim/reaction/aggression is preserved. 8 of the 12 per-bot skill-modifier columns remain folded into the single knob (the intended divergence); MoveSkill, AggresSkill (argv11) and AimSkill (argv13) are the three carried separately. autoskill uses ScoreFrags (net SCORE) rather than QC's totalfrags (gross kills) as the frag metric — a minor difference in what 'frags gained' means per window; logic/timing/band are otherwise faithful. Constant per-bot skill modifiers (12 randomized columns (move/dodge/ping/weapon/aggres/range/aim/offset/mouse/think/ai/keyboard) / 4 facets carried: single BotSkill knob + MoveSkill + AggresSkill (argv11) + AimSkill (argv13); other 8 folded) Constant autoskill metric (totalfrags (gross kills) / ScoreFrags (net SCORE))
- `bot-ai.aim.latency` — Recorded divergence — Vortex Arena deliberately does not simulate bot network latency. Base gives every bot a skill-scaled fake ping (bot.qc:96-106, bound(0, 0.07 - 0.005*(skill+bot_pingskill) + rand*0.01, 0.65)) and a bot_aimlatency shot-lead delay, then feeds that ping to antilag — it makes a low-skill bot worse by pretending it is on a bad connection rather than by making it play worse. That conflates two different axes, and it expresses bot difficulty partly through the netcode path, which this port keeps reserved for real clients (our antilag/rewind story is a reimplementation and synthetic rewind traffic in it is not wanted). Skill is instead expressed purely through aim error, turn rate, reaction interval and decision cadence, all of which are ported faithfully. CONSEQUENCE, accepted: bots below SUPERBOT lead and react slightly sharper than stock at the same skill number, so our skill values are not numerically interchangeable with the upstream ones. Revisit only if bot difficulty tuning proves it cannot reach a stock-like low-skill feel without the latency term. Decided 2026-07-27; see planning/superbots-parity-2026-07-27.md. INTENDED DIVERGENCE (2026-07-27), not an oversight: bots have zero simulated latency — no skill-based ping and no bot_aimlatency delay folded into the shot lead. BotAim.ShotLead's shotDelay parameter defaults to 0 and no live caller (BotBrain.AimAndDecideFire) passes a latency. Low-skill bots lead/react sharper than stock, so skill numbers are not numerically interchangeable with the upstream ones. See divergence_rationale. (Cross-ref: bot-ai.think.throttle owns the ping half; this row owns the aim.qh-side latency term.) Constant lead shotdelay term (this.bot_aimlatency / ShotLead shotDelay parameter exists but is 0 on the live path)
- `bot-ai.scripting.moveto_personal_waypoint` — Recorded divergence — Script destinations use the native navigator and shared waypoint graph rather than spawning and linking a personal waypoint entity. The native navigator retries stalled progress. QuakeC personal-waypoint link staging and teleport classification are absent.

- `bot-ai.roles.item_sharing` — Constant bot_ai_friends_aware_pickup_radius (500 / 500 (Cvars.cs:321 declared, no reader))
- `bot-waypoints.graph.node_model` — File-load link path (LoadLinks/LoadHardwiredLinks) appends unsorted with no 32-link cap; Base keeps wp00..wp31 sorted by cost and drops the furthest when full. A* tolerates this; route results unaffected unless a node exceeds 32 cache links. Box move_out_of_solid / setsize-to-zero collapse of point waypoints (waypoint_spawn) not replicated; the port keeps the parsed mins/maxs verbatim (m1==m2 -> IsBox false). Behaviorally equivalent for navigation. Constant max_links_per_wp (32 (wp00..wp31) / 32 (AutoLink cap; uncapped on file load))
- `bot-waypoints.graph.link_cost` — FAITHFUL (logic): the 7-arg TravelCost (Waypoint.cs:205-236) is a line-for-line port of waypoint_gettravelcost (waypoints.qc:1028-1063): both-submerged and both-crouched early-returns, the JUMP-source variant jumpheight_time+sqrt((height+jh)/(g/2)) vs plain sqrt(height/(g/2)), c=LinearCost(xy)+max(heightcost), and the single-submerged/single-crouched (c+slow)/2 half-path averages all match. FAITHFUL (values, skill cost): the skill>=7 bunnyhop 1.25x speedup branch in LinearCost is LIVE — WaypointNetwork.Skill is assigned in ForMap (Waypoint.cs:757) from the skill arg, which GameWorld.LoadWaypointNetwork (GameWorld.cs:4632-4634) feeds with (int)Cvars.Skill before any link cost bakes; the network is built once at BotPopulation.cs:178 on the live bot-population path. A mid-match skill change is not re-costed (documented BotPopulation.cs:135-138).
- `bot-waypoints.file.load_waypoints` — Symmetry header parsed but unused (no editor to mirror with); WAYPOINT_VERSION parsed but not gated/warned on (Base discards/relinks when WAYPOINT_VERSION < waypoint_version_loaded, waypoints.qc:570). Constant WAYPOINT_VERSION (1.04 / parsed (not enforced))
- `bot-waypoints.file.load_links` — No version/time gating against the .waypoints file (Base discards a stale cache and triggers schedulerelinkall); the port loads whatever cache it's given. Cache links get cost from Link()->TravelCost without per-endpoint water/crouch flags (defaults false), so the new water/crouch half-path math is not applied to cache-loaded links (they were authored with it baked in, so this is usually moot).
- `bot-waypoints.file.load_hardwired` — Hardwired links are marked with the CustomJp flag as a stand-in (no dedicated wphwXX mirror / hardwired concept), so links cannot be identified by a precise per-link hardwired identity for save/round-trip. Behaviour on the read path is faithful; the marker imprecision only matters for re-export, which is dead (see file.save).
- `bot-waypoints.path.markroutes` — Recorded divergence — Port uses A* (single-target, multi-seed entry) instead of Base's single-source Dijkstra flood with per-edict scratch state. Both yield an optimal least-cost path over the cost terms both implement; the port re-plans per goal so it doesn't need Base's all-pairs flood, and keeping cost/back-pointer state out of the immutable node lets one WaypointNetwork be shared across all bots. The algorithm-shape change is intentional and now uses Base's reachability-seeded multi-seed entry (NearestSeeds), so route results align with Base. CLOSED (Wave 18, this session): the expanding multi-seed nearest search is now LIVE. SetGoal (BotNavigation.cs:247-248) gathers net.NearestSeeds(origin, onGround) and runs the multi-seed FindPath(seeds, goalWp); the single net.Nearest(origin) + single-source FindPath is now only a fallback for when no seed is reachable (no collision world in tests / fully boxed-in bot). ComputeRouteCosts (Waypoint.cs:819, via BotRoles.SeedRoute) seeds the goal-rating route-cost field from NearestSeeds too. Both consumers now match Base's reachability-seeded multi-seed entry rather than forcing the single geometrically-nearest node. Minor residual (fidelity, not a reopen): BotBrain.cs:335 calls SeedRoute(Network, bot.Origin) with the default onGround=true, so the goal-rating cost field always uses the on-ground 750/50000 seed-radius growth even while the bot is airborne. SetGoal's route build itself passes bot.OnGround live (BotBrain.cs:346), so the bot's actual route uses the correct air 500/1500 growth; only the candidate-rating cost field is slightly off mid-air.

The stock symmetry-mirroring workflow, editor waypoint models and link beams remain open. The stock player-driven autowaypoint mode and useless-waypoint pruning remain open.

Without an AppearanceProvider (pure demo / no NetGame context) the override is inert and the entity keeps its server colormap. The forcePlayerColorsEnabled branch returns 1024+localnum+1 instead of Base's bare slot index (intended, for ModelTint contract-safety). - `cl-csqcmodel.glowmod.deathfade_ghost` — Recorded divergence — isdead is derived from networked DeadState/Health<=0 rather than Base's IS_DEAD_FRAME(frame)=(frame==0||frame==1), because the port doesn't network the MD3 0/1 death frame and a literal port would false-positive living idle players. death_time is captured client-side the first frame the entity is seen dead (Base networks it via ReadApproxPastTime BIT(5)); under packet loss the fade origin differs slightly. Both documented at ClientWorld.IsDeadModel. Death-fade origin (death_time) is client-observed, not server-networked, so the fade can start a frame or two late under packet loss. Constant isdead source (IS_DEAD_FRAME(frame)=(frame==0||frame==1) / DeadState/Health<=0) Constant death_time source (networked ReadApproxPastTime (BIT(5)) / client-observed first-dead-frame instant)
- `cl-csqcmodel.lod.distance_swap` — Recorded divergence — INTENDED only for the two unported renderer globals: view_quality (a renderer LOD-quality global) and current_viewzoom have no port equivalent, both pinned to 1 — adaptive-quality / zoom LOD scaling is deliberately absent. The missing _lodN SWAP is NOT an intended divergence (see gaps): it is deferred render work; stock player + monster models DO ship _lodN. REAL REGRESSION (STILL OPEN, re-verified 2026-06-27): stock player models erebus/gak/gakmasked AND monsters golem/spider/nanomage/wyvern ship _lod1/_lod2 in Base/data; with default cl_playerdetailreduction 4, Base downshifts distant players/monsters to lod1/lod2, but the port discards the computed index (ClientWorld.cs:1324 `_ = CsqcModelLod.SelectLodIndex(...)`, immediately-following comment 'keep lod0 (the QC fexists-miss path)') and ALWAYS renders lod0. So distant detail never reduces for default content (perf + visible-detail parity gap). view_quality and current_viewzoom are pinned to 1 (no port equivalent), so adaptive-quality / zoom LOD scaling is absent. (Intended.) Constant f = (distance*viewzoom + 100)*detailreduction / bound(0.01,view_quality,1) (yes / view_quality=1, viewzoom=1) Constant actual model swap to _lodN (yes (fexists + precache + modelindex swap) / no-op (always lod0))
- `cl-csqcmodel.skeleton.upper_lower_aim` — Aim pitch reads Entity.Angles.X instead of a separately-networked v_angle_x (BIT(10)); for remote players these can differ slightly (the visible head/torso bend may lag or use the body yaw-pitch rather than the true view pitch). Constant v_angle source for aim pitch (networked v_angle_x (BIT(10), ReadAngle) / Entity.Angles.X (no separate v_angle network))
- `cl-csqcmodel.animdecide.state_machine` — Recorded divergence — The port decides the upper-body action SERVER-side at the producer site (weapon-fire commit / damage / draw / taunt) and networks the EXPIRY-RESOLVED (action, start) as a stable contract, whereas Base runs animdecide_getupperanim per-frame on the CLIENT from the networked anim_upper_action. The networked AnimActionTime is a raw float start time (the death_time-class client-observed divergence): under packet loss the torso-action phase can start a frame or two off. The 8-direction LOCOMOTION is inferred client-side from velocity+angles, which is also how Base's animdecide_setimplicitstate derives it (no extra wire needed). AnimActionTime is a raw float start time (death_time-class): under packet loss the torso-action play phase can begin slightly off vs the server (the action-window expiry is still resolved server-side so the action never over/under-runs by more than the network jitter). The CSQC-side implicit jump detection (ground->air transition sets ANIMACTION_JUMP) is approximated by the inAir->Jump/DuckJump clip pick; no explicit end-of-jump-anim freeze. Constant networked anim inputs (anim_state BIT(7), anim_time, anim_lower_action/time BIT(8), anim_upper_action/time BIT(9) / UpperAction (byte) + AnimActionTime (raw float) networked via EntityField.AnimAction bit (1<<18); locomotion needs no wire (inferred from velocity/angles, as Base does))

Remote/spectated players — the case where the loop is audible to others — fire.
- `cl-csqcmodel.networking.csqcmodel_contract` — Constant colormap BIT(0) (networked (ReadShort) / Entity.Team (team id only, reconstructed to 1024+17*team)) Constant modelflags BIT(2) (networked (ReadByte) / NOT networked as a field, but MF_ROCKET re-derived client-side from the UsingJetpack flag (1<<8); other MF_* still 0) Constant traileffect BIT(2) (networked (ReadByte) / NOT networked (Entity has no TrailEffect)) Constant death_time BIT(5) ReadApproxPastTime (networked / NOT networked — client-observed first-dead-frame instant (see deathfade row, intended)) Constant non-player glowmod BIT(8) scaled (networked for !isplayer / NOT networked (computed from colormap for player models only)) Constant colormod BIT(8) scaled 16/0/255 (ALL entities) (networked for every entity / NOT networked (ModelTint colormod stays white; only the frozen-tint override touches it)) Constant anim_state BIT(7) + anim_time (locomotion) (networked (player) / NOT networked — locomotion inferred client-side from velocity/angles (Base's animdecide_setimplicitstate does the same, see animdecide row)) Constant anim_lower_action/time BIT(8) (non-local) (networked / NOT networked (lower body is the velocity-derived locomotion)) Constant v_angle_x BIT(10) ReadAngle (networked / NOT networked (aim pitch reads Entity.Angles.X)) Constant scale BIT(12) ReadCoord (networked / NOT networked (no per-entity model scale)) Constant tag_networkentity BIT(7,!player) / tag_networkviewloc BIT(11) (networked / NOT networked as a generic attach graph (held weapon attaches via marker + the wepent block, see tagindex row)) Constant CSQCPLAYER_FORCE_UPDATES (per s) (4 / n/a (port net model differs))
- `cl-csqcmodel.anim.animstate_set_update` — Constant start shifted -0.1*frametime into the past (float-hiccup guard) (anim.qc:25 / none (no shared animstate; client self-times each group)) Constant 1-frame ZYM restart via EF_RESTARTANIM_BIT XOR (anim.qc:18 / bit defined (CsqcModelEffectFlags.cs:43) + set at spawn (SpawnSystem.cs:868) but client anim restart is a documented no-op (ServerNet.cs:2163 comment: stateless procedural anim)) Constant anim_update frame advance bound(0,(time-start)*fps,numframes-1) (anim.qc:47-48 / server networks only the group START frame (Entity.Frame); the client ModelAnimator advances within the group by its own clock)
- `cl-csqcmodel.animdecide.frame_registry` — Constant ~31 registered player framegroups (die1..duckwalkbackleft) (animdecide.qh:52-104 / BuildClipTable resolves the same locomotion set (21 leg clips) + the 8 upper-body action clips; dead1/dead2 static-corpse groups collapse into one Dead pick (death/dead/die)) Constant multi-synonym framename lookup (e.g. 'duckwalkstrafeleft duckstrafeleft groupified_25_anim') (FOREACH_WORD over m_framenames + frameforname / Pick() Contains-match over the PRIMARY name only; legacy synonyms (duckstrafeleft, dieone/dietwo) and the groupified_N_anim ZYM names are not probed)
- `cl-csqcmodel.animdecide.explicit_setstate` — Constant ANIMSTATE_DEAD1 vs DEAD2 (die1/die2 base frames) (BIT(0)/BIT(1) / single Dead flag (DeadFlag.Dead) for the legs; the die1/die2 VARIANT reaches the client only via the torso UpperAction (Die1/Die2)) Constant ANIMSTATE_FROZEN -> force idle (BIT(3) / FrozenHold: holds the CURRENT skeletal pose static while encased (ClientWorld.cs:1090-1092), not a forced idle clip) Constant ANIMSTATE_FOLLOW -> force idle (BIT(4) / not implemented)
- `cl-csqcmodel.skeleton.bone_class_contract` — Constant MAX_AIM_BONES fixed-array cap (MAX_AIM_BONES (common/util.qh) / unbounded List (superset; model-parameter files supply few aim bones in practice))
- `cl-csqcmodel.prediction.csqcplayer_contract` — Constant cl_movement default (prediction on) (true (cl_player.qh:24) / prediction ALWAYS on — the port has no unpredicted (engine-interpolated local player) fallback; 'cl_movement' is read only as a menu dependency gate (DialogSettingsMisc.cs:38)) Constant csqcplayer_status tri-state (UNPREDICTED/FROMSERVER/PREDICTED) (cl_player.qh:27-30 / no explicit tri-state; the Reconciler holds server-state + replayed-prediction internally) Constant pmove_flags PMF_JUMP_HELD read-only contract (qh:35-36 / jump-held tracked inside the shared movement sim (not exposed as a pmove_flags field))
- `cl-damageeffects.net.sv_broadcast` — Recorded divergence — Deliberate architecture swap: the port keeps gameplay headless and networks effects server-side over the EFF_NET effect channel (EffectEmitter -> ServerNet.EffectNetSink, see fx-effectinfo.emit.send_effect) instead of sending one compact DamageInfo entity for the client to expand. DamageInfoNetwork exists as a named no-op with ZERO call sites (not even wired into WeaponSplash.RadiusDamage — the doc comment's 'call site can be wired later' is aspirational). The FEATURES the Base entity enabled (attached damage effects, client radius push, species blood, team tint, vehicle/turret impact switches) are scored in their own rows — this row only covers the wire mechanism itself. No compact per-blast event exists client-side, so every downstream consumer (rows below) that keyed off ENT_CLIENT_DAMAGEINFO had to be re-sourced server-side or is missing. Bandwidth/precision profile differs (Base: ~16 bytes quantized per blast; port: one EFF_NET message per effect emission with float32 vectors). Constant silent flag (deathtype |= 0x8000 when !sound_allowed(MSG_BROADCAST, dmgowner) / no wire bit; SoundSystem.SoundAllowedGate gates bot_sound_monopoly at server emission instead) Constant force encoding (dir 3x WriteChar rint(v*127) + length byte dmg_force/4 (max 1020) / n/a (no wire entity; full-precision vectors on the EFF_NET channel)) Constant dmg/radius/edge bytes (bound(1,dmg,255) / bound(0,rad,255) / bound(1,edge,255) / n/a) Constant species bits (bloodtype & BITS(4), BIT(7) = negative radius / n/a) Constant colormap (teamplay ? dmgowner.team : dmgowner.clientcolors / n/a)
- `cl-damageeffects.net.cl_radius_apply` — Constant MIN_DAMAGEEXTRARADIUS (2 / none) Constant MAX_DAMAGEEXTRARADIUS (16 / 16 exists server-side (WeaponSplash.cs:26) but that is damage-pipeline's row) Constant falloff (thisdmg = dmg + (edge-dmg) * (dist-extraradius)/rad; nearest-point-on-box distance / none client-side) Constant push (velocity += damage_explosion_calcpush(damageforcescale*force, vel, damagepush_speedfactor); UNSET_ONGROUND / none client-side) Constant negative-radius forcemul (BIT(7) of species -> forcemul -1 (attractive blast) / none)
- `cl-damageeffects.impact.weapon_dispatch` — Recorded divergence — Architecture: Base runs each weapon's wr_impacteffect CLIENT-side, keyed off the DamageInfo deathtype; the port emits the same named effect + impact sound SERVER-side at the explosion/impact call site and networks it over the effect channel. Equivalent visible result when the gates agree; the gate mismatches below are unintended gaps, not part of the divergence. Sky-surface hits still show the impact particle puff (Base suppresses all impact FX on Q3SURFACEFLAG_SKY; port machinegun-family weapons only silence the ric, and Shotgun.cs:246 passes no silent flag at all so even the ric can play on sky). A hitscan shot that stopped in a player still emits the wall impact behind them — Base's `if(!hitplayer || rad)` suppression is absent (port traces WorldOnly past the victim). The Weapon_ImpactEffect mutator hook (damageeffects.qc:458) has no port dispatch seam. w_issilent per-recipient muting: Base flags the whole broadcast silent when sound_allowed fails; the port gates at SoundSystem emission (bot_sound_monopoly) — same audible outcome for the only Base trigger, different mechanism. Constant backoff source (traceline(w_org - dir*16, w_org + dir*16, MOVE_NOMONSTERS); startsolid retry from -dir*40; else -force_dir / per-weapon impact trace PlaneNormal, fallback -shot.Dir (Machinegun.cs:403); no startsolid retry needed (trace starts at gun)) Constant sky gate (Q3SURFACEFLAG_SKY suppresses the WHOLE wr_impacteffect (particles+sound+decal) / machinegun/rifle/ok* only mute the ricochet on sky (Machinegun.cs:407-408, Rifle.cs:354); Shotgun.cs:246 passes NO silent flag (even the ric plays on sky); only Vaporizer.cs:259 gates the full impact FX on sky+noimpact) Constant hitscan player gate (!hitplayer || rad — no ground impact when a hitscan shot hit a player / not implemented: WorldOnly impact trace paints the wall behind the victim)
- `cl-damageeffects.impact.team_tint` — Constant teamplay tcolor ((dmg_colors - 1) * 0x11, else 0x00; | BIT(10) RENDER_COLORMAPPED / none) Constant palette (particles_colormin = colormapPaletteColor(floor(tcolor/16), false); colormax = colormapPaletteColor(tcolor%16, true) / none)
- `cl-damageeffects.fx.attached_damage` — Recorded divergence — Attach to authoritative direct or splash damage targets in each recipient's native snapshot. Stock also sweeps visual models near the broadcast impact and estimates damage and falloff on the client. The native record is optional within the existing framed effect bundle. Original, Mixed and Modern continue to use their existing asset selections.
- `cl-damageeffects.fx.attached_lifecycle` — Recorded divergence — The native camera identifies the actual first-person body, including a followed player. A persistent spawn flag in the existing snapshot flags clears stale effects when a death snapshot is missed or a match restart spawns a living player directly into a new life.
- `cl-damageeffects.impact.vehicle_fx` — Constant raptor fragment burst (3x RaptorCBShellfragToss at 120° roll steps, vel = normalize + randomvec()*128; + RAPTOR_BOMB_SPREAD + SND_ROCKET_IMPACT / shellfrags + spread ported (client reacts to the emission); the rocket-impact crack sound is NOT played) Constant spiderbot minigun (SND_RIC_RANDOM + EFFECT_SPIDERBOT_MINIGUN_IMPACT / none) Constant racer gun / rocket (SND_LASERIMPACT + RACER_IMPACT / SND_ROCKET_IMPACT + RACER_ROCKET_EXPLODE / none) Constant raptor cannon / bomb (SND_LASERIMPACT + RAPTOR_CANNON_IMPACT / SND_ROCKET_IMPACT + RAPTOR_BOMB_IMPACT / none) Constant bumblebee gun (SND_VEH_BUMBLEBEE_IMPACT + BIGPLASMA_IMPACT / none) Constant vehicle death FX (DEATH_VH_*_DEATH: SND_ROCKET_IMPACT ATTEN_LOW + EXPLOSION_BIG / vehicles emit EXPLOSION_MEDIUM/BIG at death server-side (VehicleCommon/Racer/Raptor/Bumblebee) — owned by vehicle-* units)
- `cl-damageeffects.impact.turret_fx` — Constant ewheel (SND_LASERIMPACT ATTEN_LOW + EFFECT_BLASTER_IMPACT / none) Constant flac (EFFECT_HAGAR_EXPLODE + SND_FLACEXP_RANDOM / none) Constant mlrs/hk/walker-rocket/hellion (SND_ROCKET_IMPACT ATTEN_LOW + EFFECT_ROCKET_EXPLODE / none) Constant machinegun/walker-gun (SND_RIC_RANDOM + EFFECT_MACHINEGUN_IMPACT / none) Constant plasma (SND_TUR_PLASMA_IMPACT ATTEN_LOW + EFFECT_ELECTRO_IMPACT / none) Constant walker melee (SND_RIC_RANDOM ATTEN_LOW + EFFECT_TE_SPARK / none) Constant tesla (te_smallflash / none)
- `cl-damageeffects.fx.model_gib_limit` — Recorded divergence — The native model pool has a fixed ceiling of 1024 normal gibs to bound allocations. Larger cl_gibs_maxcount values are bounded to 1024. Gentle and particle effect modes are outside this model-limit row. Constant cl_gibs_maxcount (100 default, read at spawn / 100 default, read from the live client store, capped at 1024)
- `cl-hud.engine.registry_and_order` — Constant panel_count (28 panels (REGISTER_HUD_PANEL WEAPONS..CHECKPOINTS, hud.qh:250-277; +4 minigame panels = 32 total registrations) / discovered set (incl. port extras: Fps/Ping/Position/Shownames)) Constant _hud_panelorder reverse draw (order[0] on top / Godot child z-order)
- `cl-hud.engine.loadcvars` — Constant hud_panel_update_interval (2 / dirty-flag + viewport throttle)
- `cl-hud.engine.dock` — shirt/pants colormap dock tint (hud_dock_color shirt/pants) still not implemented (the dock reads its own TeamColor, not HudPanel.LocalShirtColor/LocalPantsColor, so it punts on the colormap literals). hud_dock_color 'team' + rgb + the hud_dock_color_team factor tints ARE implemented.
- `cl-hud.panel.ammo` — nade-bonus display fidelity not runtime-verified.
- `cl-hud.panel.racetimer` — Race medal-flash status (race_SendStatus) and personal-best deltas are derived from a host-side analogue, not the TE_CSQC_RACE wire, so a pure --connect client (no in-process Race state) gets no race timer. Decimal-precision + speed-unit edge formatting not runtime-verified.
- `cl-hud.panel.vote` — Vote state is fed from the in-process listen-server VoteController; a pure --connect client has no vote net channel yet, so the panel stays blank there.
- `cl-hud.panel.chat` — Recorded divergence — There is no DarkPlaces engine chat console to drive (con_chatrect/con_chat*); the port keeps its own scrolling line buffer and renders chat itself with the Xolonium font. Same on-screen result, different mechanism. Constant con_chatsize / con_chattime (8 / 30 / own renderer (not engine rect))
- `cl-hud.panel.engineinfo` — Recorded divergence — Off by default like Base; the port also ships a separate DP-style FpsPanel (cl_showfps) that is the primary FPS readout, so engineinfo is redundant by design.
- `cl-hud.panel.infomessages` — Constant group_time / group_fadetime (6 / 0.4 / unknown)
- `cl-hud.panel.centerprint` — Follow-below-scoreboard/radar repositioning fidelity not all runtime-verified. hud_panel_centerprint_fade_in default is 0.15 (the QC code default); Base's shipped _hud_common.cfg setas it to 0, so messages fade in over 0.15s where the stock HUD pops in instantly. Minor; user-overridable via cvar. Constant _fade_in (0.15 QC code default (autocvar_..._fade_in); _hud_common.cfg:133 setas it to 0 for the shipped HUD / 0.15 (matches the QC code default, not the cfg seta))
- `cl-hud.panel.scoreboard` — Recorded divergence — The networked scoreboard is a standalone _scoreboard placed by a manual Configure rect (fed from ClientNet); _fullHud's discovered Scoreboard is left unfed to avoid a double scoreboard. The cvar pos/size therefore doesn't move the live board. Scoreboard ignores hud_panel_scoreboard_pos/_size (manual rect, intended divergence). The time-since-start accuracy show-delay (hud_panel_scoreboard_accuracy_showdelay) is registered but only the binary warmup gate is wired (the delay timer itself is a documented residual). Constant layout source (hud_panel_scoreboard_pos/_size / hardcoded Configure rect in NetGame)
- `cl-hud.panel.checkpoints` — Listen-server only: the split list is fed from the host-side Race state (Race.RaceState + the per-checkpoint record store), not the TE_CSQC_RACE wire, so a pure --connect client (no in-process Race state) sees no checkpoint splits. The frozen-split label uses 'Checkpoint N' / 'Finish line' rather than the per-map waypoint names.
- `cl-hud.net.hybrid_hud` — Recorded divergence — Godot HUD panels need a local Player actor for STAT(*); a pure --connect client has none. So NetGame runs the full skinned Hud on Layer 4 AND a lightweight NetHud (networked health/armor + crosshair) on Layer 5, suppressing _fullHud's HealthArmor/Crosshair when NetHud owns them to avoid a double draw. On a listen server the local server Player feeds the full panels directly.
- `cl-hud.draw.circle_clipped_pic` — Constant blend (DRAWFLAG_ADDITIVE (crosshair rings) / alpha-blended (no per-draw blend material))
- `cl-hud.draw.borderlines` — Constant geometry (4 filled edge quads, pos outset by thickness (frame drawn OUTSIDE the rect), top/bottom lengthened by 2*thickness / 4 filled edge quads around the rect (MapVotePanel.cs:690))
- `cl-hud.draw.pic_tiled` — Constant consumers (scoreboard.qc:1711+ (gfx/scoreboard/scoreboard_bg table backing, 5 sites) + hud_config.qc:1129-1133 (tiled panel-highlight border art) / none tiled; configure-mode highlight is a plain 2px frame in the same color/alpha)
- `cl-hud.draw.string_aspect` — Constant fit rule (scale text to box preserving aspect, recenter on the shrunk axis (+ horizontal recenter after font-size snapping) / no shared scale-to-box primitive; panels draw at panel-computed font sizes with left/center/right alignment)
- `cl-hud.draw.hud_shift_scale_macros` — Recorded divergence — The macro remap layer itself is engine plumbing Godot replaces wholesale: the shake/follow shift+scale is applied once as a CanvasLayer transform (see cl-hud.engine.dynamic_shake / dynamic_follow) instead of per draw call — same on-screen result for panels that participate. The per-panel hud_panel_<id>_dynamichud opt-out is unconsumed: the cvars are registered across panels but nothing reads them (HudPanel.IsDynamic is a redraw-throttle, not this), so the whole-layer shake/sway also moves panels Base excludes — concretely infomessages, shipped dynamichud 0. drawfontscale-based text scaling under dynamic HUD is subsumed by the layer transform (fine); the stringwidth wrapper's drawfontscale-neutral measurement has no analogue because the port never scales the font globally. Constant mechanism (every panel draw call remapped through HUD_Shift/HUD_Scale macros (per-panel, gated by hud_panel_<id>_dynamichud) / one CanvasLayer Offset/Scale transform applied to the whole HUD by HudManager) Constant hud_panel_infomessages_dynamichud (0 (_hud_common.cfg:66 — the one panel shipped opted out) / not consumed (whole-layer transform hits it too))
- `cl-hud.header.physics` — hud_panel_physics_dynamichud (header default true) is not registered/consumed — moot only because the whole-layer dynamic transform ignores the per-panel gate everywhere (see cl-hud.draw.hud_shift_scale_macros).
- `cl-hud.header.weapons` — Constant orderbyimpulse (1 (_hud_common.cfg:80; .qh code default also true) / 0, and unconsumed (Register only, no reader)) Constant accuracy_color_levels (cvar-driven up-to-10-stop ramp (Accuracy_LoadLevels/Accuracy_GetColor) / fixed red→yellow→green ramp (no accuracy_color* cvar hits in game/+src/))
- `cl-hud.header.chat` — Recorded divergence — Same as cl-hud.panel.chat: there is no DarkPlaces chat console to position, so the con_chatrect/_x/_y/con_chatwidth handshake is replaced by the panel drawing its own buffer; con_chatsize is honored as the line size. con_notify/con_notifysize (the non-chat engine notify area the header also declares) and _con_chat_maximized's full engine semantics are not separately ported; maximized-chat parity is already flagged partial on cl-hud.panel.chat.
- `cl-hud.header.timer` — The HUD_Timer_Color/TimeElapsed/TimeLeft helper triple's line-level fidelity is not re-verified here (covered behaviorally by cl-hud.panel.timer, which still carries a runtime-verification caveat).
- `cl-hud.header.powerups` — Constant strength bar color (hud_progressbar_strength_color '1 0.39 0' (strength.qc:48 passes the CVAR to addPowerupItem) / hardcoded m_color '0 0 1' (blue)) Constant shield bar color (hud_progressbar_shield_color '0.36 1 0.07' / hardcoded m_color '1 0 1' (magenta))
- `cl-hud.header.vote` — Recorded divergence — Approved records.nickname_prompt keeps the native local rankings dialog manual. First Run and Profile expose the preference.
- `cl-hud.header.infomessages` — Constant dynamichud (false (the header's one non-default: infomessages opts OUT of dynamic HUD) / not consumed; whole-layer shake/sway moves this panel too)
- `cl-hud.header.modicons` — The mod_active visibility gate (panel hidden when no gametype handler is active) is handled by the panel's own draw/visibility logic; exact HUD_Main mod_active semantics not line-verified.
- `cl-scoreboard.field.ping` — Recorded divergence — The native ping measurement uses ENet RTT per ADR-0005 and ADR-0011. A row without an RTT gets a neutral dash in the player table.
- `cl-scoreboard.sort.players` — Native slot order mirrors active-row ties. Spectators and connecting peers use a separate native roster rather than the QC spectator/downloader ordering.
- `cl-scoreboard.block.spectators` — Constant hud_panel_scoreboard_spectators_aligned (0 (read) / 0 (cfg only))
- `cl-shownames.distance.fade` — The client and server use independent globals. Common initializes max_shot_distance to 32768, and server/world.qc:731 only changes SVQC. The native client uses the same hard ceiling.
- `cl-shownames.name.decolorize` — Name comes from the scoreboard name slice (ResolveScoreboardName, NetGame.cs:1515 NameResolver), not a dedicated entcs name stream; faithful entcs_GetName stand-in but if the scoreboard row is missing the name is empty and the tag shows no text (status bar still draws). Not a presentation-fidelity defect.
- `cl-shownames.alpha.entcs_getalpha` — Recorded divergence — Native gameplay is compiled C# under ADR-0001 and ADR-0011. ADR-0013 governs native extensions. ADR-0019 runs server-supplied CSQC in the separate legacy mode. The pinned stock tree has no subscriber to this event.
- `cl-strafehud.gate.show_mode` — Out-of-range value 4: Base's gate conditions (==1||==3 observer hide, ==3 hook) all miss, so 4 behaves like 2 (always on); port defines 4 as Race/CTS incl. observing. Cosmetic divergence on a non-default value. Constant mode-4 semantics (4 falls through all Base gates = always on incl. observing / 4 = Race/CTS incl. observing)
- `cl-strafehud.input.wishangle_keys` — Constant non-local wishangle (45 if fwd/back key else 90; sign from left/right keys; 0 if neither / always 45 (no pressed-keys STAT))
- `cl-strafehud.input.phys_cvars` — Constant PHYS_SLICKACCELERATE source (STAT(MOVEVARS_SLICKACCELERATE) <- sv_slickaccelerate (stock physicsX 15) / reads sv_airaccelerate (stock 2)) Constant PHYS_AIRSTOPACCELERATE_FULL source (STAT(MOVEVARS_AIRSTOPACCELERATE_FULL) <- sv_airstopaccelerate_full / reads sv_aircontrol == 0 (self-described 'conservative' guess)) Constant ducked maxspeed_mod (0.5 when IS_DUCKED(csqcplayer) / ducked hardcoded false (mod always 1)) Constant fallback defaults (unregistered store) (stock physicsX: maxspeed 360, accelerate 15, airaccelerate 2, friction 6, airaccel_qw -0.8, aircontrol 100 / 800 / 5.5 / 1 / 5 / 1 / 0 fallbacks (live store normally supplies real values via MoveVarsBlock/PhysicsPreset))
- `cl-strafehud.math.frametime` — Constant hud_panel_strafehud_fps_update (0.5 / 0.5 registered but UNREAD (estimator not ported)) Constant DP frame split threshold (0.05 s (halve frames > 50 ms) / absent)
- `cl-strafehud.math.friction_replica` — Constant PHYS_FRICTION_REPLICA_DT (0.00390625 (1/256, common/physics/player.qh:331) / 1/60 (StrafeHudPanel.cs:407))
- `cl-strafehud.bar.zones_render` — Recorded divergence — STYLE_GRADIENT (the default) is rendered through the per-segment soft-gradient loop instead of Base's accelerated two-polygon vertex gradient (R_BeginPolygon), with projection applied up-front — self-documented as visually equivalent at HUD sizes (StrafeHudPanel.cs:1157-1160, 1207-1209). STYLE_PROGRESSBAR falls back to a flat fill because the skin's progressbar art is unavailable in the HUD layer (1182). STYLE_PROGRESSBAR (style 1) loses the progressbar texture: color/alpha faithful but the skin art is not drawn. Not-moving neutral full-bar draw collapses the drawfill/progressbar distinction into one DrawRect (draw_core.qc 14-35 has separate paths).
- `cl-strafehud.bar.projection_range` — Constant VF_FOVX source (range -1 mode) (live getproperty(VF_FOVX) / FovX property, default 90, never fed)
- `cl-strafehud.indicators.angle_markers` — Strafe-ratio + colors are computed from the numerically-tainted zone angles (see upstream rows); the marker plumbing itself is faithful.
- `cl-strafehud.extra.slick_detector` — Constant slickdetector defaults (on, range 200, granularity 1 (bound 0..4), color '0 1 1', alpha 0.5, height 0.125 / identical registered; range/granularity are display-gates only (no trace))
- `cl-strafehud.extra.text_indicators` — Constant unit source (autocvar_hud_speed_unit (live cvar) / SpeedUnit property, default 1, never fed and hud_speed_unit not read)
- `cl-strafehud.coverage.infra` — Recorded divergence — _mod.inc/_mod.qh are genmod.sh build-glue with no behavior (C# has no include-shim analogue); HUD_StrafeHUD_Export is an empty QC stub in Base itself (skin-file export hook) — nothing to port. Cited here so the coverage ledger counts every scoped file. Demo sweep runs only under _hud_configure; the port's HUD-configure editor integration determines whether it is reachable (cl-hud unit owns the editor).
- `cl-teamradar.blip.enumeration` — Port filters the net entity feed to Player/Nameplate kinds; Base walks the dedicated entcs slice (m_entcs_private). Functionally equivalent for players, but no entcs-private subtleties (e.g. spectated/unconnected slots).
- `cl-teamradar.icon.objective` — Tint relies on the server having pre-resolved wp.Color; the client-side spritelookupcolor fallback (per-weapon/item m_color, flag-carrier color) is not modeled client-side.
- `cl-teamradar.cvar.zoomscale` — The native heuristic still supplies a range in the absence of valid map bounds.
- `cl-teamradar.cvar.defaults` — Constant hud_panel_radar_size default (128 (x; y mirrors x when 0) / no analogue (live control size / 2500u range))
- `cl-view.zoom.zoomscript_buttonstatus` — cl_unpress_zoom_on_weapon_switch / cl_unpress_attack_on_weapon_switch and the cl_hook_activeweapon localcmd are not modelled (niche scripting glue). isdemo() zoom-via-__togglezoom fallback is absent. Constant cl_unpress_zoom_on_weapon_switch (1 / none) Constant cl_unpress_attack_on_weapon_switch (1 / none)
- `cl-view.chase.event_death_cam` — Vehicle chase (cl_eventchase_vehicle/_vehicle_distance), spectated-change chase (cl_eventchase_spectated_change), and the game-stop/intermission/viewloc chase triggers in WantEventchase are not ported (death + frozen + host-forced chase only).
- `cl-view.bob.view_bob` — The cl_bob_limit_heightcheck trace path (default 0) is omitted (documented; the default-0 branch is the one that runs).
- `cl-view.smooth.stair_viewheight` — Recorded divergence — Faithful mode (cl_movement_smoothing_faithful, default on) uses the exact bounded stairsmoothz glide + viewheightavg blend; the port-default-mode alternative uses an adaptive render-only stair offset (ClientNet.PredictedStairOffset) to fight the 1/72-quantized server-render-clock jitter (see camera-drift-render-smoothing memory). The stepheight processing is the only intentional divergence in the faithful path.
- `cl-view.screen.postprocess` — Recorded divergence — The damage/content blur, powerup edge-sharpen and blur-test drive engine GLSL shader cvars (r_glsl_postprocess + r_glsl_postprocess_uservec1/2) with no Godot analogue; ViewEffects deliberately ships only the coloured screen reaction (documented in ViewEffects.cs class header). No screen blur when damaged / underwater, no powerup edge-sharpen or blur-test debug effect. Constant hud_postprocessing (1 (master) / none) Constant hud_damage_blur (1 (effect) / none) Constant hud_powerup (0 (sharpen) / none)
- `cl-view.viewmodel.sway` — Sway constants are [Export] fields with hardcoded defaults rather than live cl_*model_* cvar reads, so changing those cvars at runtime has no effect (values still match the shipped defaults). cl_followmodel_velocity_absolute branch (calc_followmodel_ofs:125,152-161) is not ported (default 0, so the view-relative branch — which IS ported — is the default path). UpdateSway does not gate on chase/dead like viewmodel_animate:260 (autocvar_chase_active || HEALTH<=0), but the whole viewmodel node is hidden during chase/dead, so the sway is not observable then anyway.
- `cl-view.demo.free_camera_lockview_ortho` — Constant cl_orthoview (0 / none)
- `cl-view.zoom.unpress_defaults` — Constant cl_unpress_zoom_on_spawn (true (forced -zoom release on own spawn) / none (ZoomHeld re-derived from raw key state; no forced release)) Constant cl_unpress_zoom_on_weapon_switch (true / none)
- `cl-view.demo.camera_cvars` — Constant camera_enable (0 (off; demo playback only) / none)
- `cl-view.spawn.event_effects` — Constant cl_spawn_event_sound (1 (true, spawnpoints.qh:4) / none (sound gated server-side only))
- `cl-view.race.cptimes_defaults` — Constant cl_race_checkpoint_splits_console (1 (print checkpoint splits to console) / none) Constant cl_race_cptimes_onlyself (false (bare) / none (no reader))
- `cl-viewmodel.anim.networked_frames` — Constant wframe states (WFRAME_FIRE1 / WFRAME_FIRE2 / WFRAME_IDLE / WFRAME_RELOAD via networked attackrate+restartanim / WepentView.ViewmodelFrame selector 0 idle/1 fire/2 reload/3 raise/4 drop, networked per-player; SetNetAnimFrame plays a clip once on the rising edge (no per-frame frame= value, no server frame-timing sync))
- `cl-viewmodel.model.skin_colormap_glow_effects` — Constant colormap (256 + entcs colors (team/player palette swap) / mask-driven instance-uniform tint on skin/converted surfaces (DP model: no _shirt/_pants mask -> no tint); approx whole-model albedo tint only on legacy fallback surfaces) Constant skin (m_skin per child (wepskin) / none (base material))
- `cl-viewmodel.alpha.viewmodel_alpha` — Vehicle force-hide (a=-1 when in a vehicle) is handled via the Visible toggle on dead/holster/chase/intermission, not via the continuous alpha=-1 path; functionally equivalent (gun hidden), so no visible defect.
- `cl-viewmodel.align.gunalign_gunoffset` — Constant cl_gunalign (3 (1/2=center vecs.y=0 z-=2; 3=right; 4=left mirror Y) / read live (RefreshCvars); right=authored, left=mirror -2*side, center=-side z-=2)
- `cl-viewmodel.switch.raise_lower_anim` — Recorded divergence — Port does not network the QC wframe temp entity / weapon_switchdelay / weapon_nextthink, so it cannot drive the -90*f*f tip from server-synced state. Instead the gun slides straight down off-screen on a keypress-predicted holster (RunBoundCommand) with a server-confirmed raise (EquipNetworkedWeapon) + auto-recover grace. Documented in ViewModel.PlayHolster/PlayRaise. Weapon-switch animation is a slide-down, not Base's barrel-tip (this.angles_x = -90*f*f), and is not keyed to per-weapon switchdelay_raise/drop or weapon_nextthink (port does not network wframe). Constant angles_x tip (-90 * f * f (barrel swings down out of view) / slide straight down off-screen) Constant switch timing (switchdelay_raise / switchdelay_drop via weapon_nextthink, /WEAPONRATEFACTOR / custom durations, keypress-predicted + server-confirm + auto-recover)
- `cl-viewmodel.sway.followmodel` — cl_followmodel_velocity_absolute (1) branch not ported (port always uses the view-relative path; matches Base default 0). cl_followmodel_speed/limit/lowpass/highpass constants are baked [Export] defaults, not read from cvars (only the cl_followmodel on/off toggle is read live). Constant cl_followmodel_velocity_absolute (0 / not handled (always relative path))
- `cl-viewmodel.sway.leanmodel` — Lean teleport reset (csqcmodel_teleported -> gunangles_prev = view_angles) not ported; gun may snap on teleport/warpzone. cl_leanmodel_speed/limit/highpass/lowpass constants are baked [Export] defaults (only the cl_leanmodel on/off toggle is read live).
- `cl-viewmodel.sway.bobmodel` — cl_bobmodel_side/up/speed constants are baked [Export] defaults (only the cl_bobmodel on/off toggle is read live).
- `cl-viewmodel.muzzle.flash_particle` — Constant chase_active branch (emit at server sv_shotorg / not handled (gun hidden in chase))
- `cl-viewmodel.casings.brass_eject` — Recorded divergence — The native casing pool is bounded at 1024. Separate fixed opaque and fade batches preserve authored model materials. Larger cl_casings_maxcount values are bounded to 1024. Constant cl_casings_maxcount (100 default, read at spawn / 100 default, read from the live client store, capped at 1024)
- `cl-viewmodel.model.wr_viewmodel_override` — Override only resolves on the local player's listen-server Player state (LocalServerPlayer); a pure remote-client (dedicated-server) Tuba instrument is read from server-side player state, so it is correct on the listen-server play path the port runs but unverified on a pure thin client.
- `clanarena.round.timelimit_stalemate` — Minor: CheckWinner reads ca.Handler.RoundEndTime for the timeUp test; that field IS mirrored each frame by MirrorRoundTiming (via _roundSync, GameWorld.cs:2530/2777), so the timelimit branch is reachable. At the stock g_ca_prevent_stalemate 0, a timed-out round yields a stalemate (-2 -> ROUND_OVER), matching QC.
- `clanarena.items.no_pickups` — Recorded divergence — Powerups are matched by classname stand-in (item_strength/shield/invincible) rather than a ported itemdef.instanceOfPowerup registry, because the port has no full item-class registry (the same approximation Mayhem/LMS/NIX/Duel use). Functionally equivalent for the stock powerup classes.
- `clanarena.notify.round_outcome` — The round-win announcer cue is not separately played (QC plays the win sound via the notification; the port Sends the Center+Info notifications but no dedicated Annce cue is wired here).
- `clanarena.round.grace_no_fire` — Minor: the QC ForbidWeaponUse mutator-hook half of weaponUseForbidden is not modeled (RoundFireForbidden only carries the round-grace half). No bundled port mutator forbids weapon use, so functionally complete for CA.
- `clanarena.join.late_join_observer` — Stock CA-specific bot-fill participation accounting remains unported.
- `console-commands.generic.dispatch_infra` — Recorded divergence — QC's per-program command registries (GENERIC_COMMANDS/CLIENT_COMMANDS/... + cfg-generated qc_cmd_* aliases + the sv_cmd/cl_cmd/menu_cmd prefixes) are replaced by two C# tables: the shared ConfigInterpreter registered-command table (client/menu console) and the server Commands table, glued by an unknown-command router (console → local listen world or remote clc_stringcmd). Commands are invoked BARE (no cl_cmd/sv_cmd prefix), matching how the shipped cfg aliases expose them to players.
- `console-commands.generic.rpn` — Recorded divergence — Scope deviation documented in Rpn.cs header (trap R3): the persistent-DB op family (put/get/dbpush/dbpop/dbget/dblen/dbclr/dbsave/dbload/dbins/dbext/dbread/dbat/dbmov/dbgoto) and localtime/gmtime/digest/fexists/fexists_assert/eval are NOT implemented — no QC hashtable-DB/file layer exists; they fall through to the bare-cvar default push (DP's documented unknown-token behavior) with a developer-mode 'unsupported rpn op' log. Every op the shipped cfgs/quickmenu actually use IS ported (arithmetic/stack/logic/compare/bound/when/def/defs/load/set-ops/shuffle/sprintf1s/crc16/rand/time). MAX_RPN_STACK is 128 in the port vs 16 in Base — an rpn program relying on QC's overflow-at-16 diagnostic behaves differently (benign in practice; no shipped cfg pushes >16). db ops / digest / eval / localtime / gmtime / fexists fall to the cvar-push fallback rather than executing (intended, see rationale) — a cfg using them gets a different (non-crashing) result than Base. Constant MAX_RPN_STACK (16 / 128 (Rpn.cs:37 MaxStack))
- `console-commands.generic.nextframe` — On a bare menu/headless console (no world), the console impl runs the command INLINE instead of next frame (documented degenerate in ConsoleCommands.cs:398-401); with a live world it forwards to the server's defer-0 queue (a real next-tick).
- `console-commands.generic.qc_curl` — Recorded divergence — Same class as the ban-sync uri_get divergence (sv-commands-votes.ban.sync_online): the port's core is deliberately HTTP-free; no crypto_uri_postbuf layer exists to build on. No shipped cfg or gameplay path calls qc_curl. No qc_curl: a server admin cannot fetch a URL into a cvar / exec a fetched config from the console.
- `console-commands.generic.runtest` — Recorded divergence — QC's in-VM TEST_* harness is replaced wholesale by the port's xunit suite (tests/VortexArena.Tests, ~2.7k tests) run out-of-process; an in-game runtest command has no equivalent test registry to invoke.
- `console-commands.generic.markup_easter_eggs` — Constant NUM_MARKUPS (41 (&alien..&<| -> \x12..\xff glyphs) / none)
- `console-commands.client.dispatch` — Recorded divergence — The cl_cmd prefix + CLIENT_COMMANDS registry collapse into the shared console table + NetGame's bind-command intercepts (same rationale as generic.dispatch_infra). The '+showaccuracy' legacy no-op commands are intentionally dropped. The demo free-camera console commands (+forward/+back/+moveup/+moveright/+roll_left/… registered by ConsoleCommand_macro_init when isdemo(), driving camera_direction/camera_roll) are not ported — consistent with cl-view's 'demo free camera (CSQC_Demo_Camera) not ported' gap. No `cl_cmd help` catalogue of client commands: the console `help`/`cmdlist` list registered console + server commands, but the QC per-client-command usage texts largely have no equivalent (each missing command below is its own row).
- `console-commands.client.sendcvar_replication` — Recorded divergence — Two deliberate shifts, both documented in code: (1) the client-side W_FixWeaponOrder pre-send fixup moves to the server receive path (same math, one canonical implementation); (2) the deprecated server→client GetCvars f==0 'request' direction (getreplies.qc:392 itself warns it is deprecated in favor of REPLICATE) is not ported — the client watcher pushes changes unprompted, which is the REPLICATE model.
- `console-commands.client.handlevote` — Recorded divergence — Approved records.nickname_prompt keeps First Run and Profile as the prompts for native local rankings. The in-match dialog opens on request. Xonotic can open it automatically.
- `console-commands.replies.records_ladder` — Recorded divergence — The existing console specification calls for up-to-date replies and the server regression requires an explicit empty-ladder message. The paired gameplay run confirmed both visible differences. An empty native ladder retains its existing empty message. The pin renders an empty table because it initializes the header before checking the output string. Native reports read the database and mounted map catalog on demand. The pin uses replies cached at world initialization and refreshed by some record paths. Constant empty ladder (unconditional table header / No ladder on this server!)
- `console-commands.replies.maplists_monsters` — Recorded divergence — The established native rotation has three sources. Showing the effective rotation when the cvar is empty also covers long rotations stored in a file and the installed-map fallback. Maplist page sizing remains part of the existing native command contract. For an empty rotation cvar the native host can report its effective rotation, including the map-list file or the mounted catalog. The pinned reply tests the cvar alone.
- `console-commands.replies.infra` — Recorded divergence — The pin caches replies at world setup. A successful gametype switch refreshes lsmaps, and Race ranking writes or removals refresh rankings. Native builds the requested reply on demand so current settings, installed maps and saved records are reflected immediately.
- `console-commands.server.common_qh_infra` — Recorded divergence — Pure declarations header: the COMMON_COMMAND class/registry/alias macros collapse into the port's flat Commands.Register table (same rationale as generic.dispatch_infra); the autocvars (sv_adminnick/sv_status_privacy/sv_timeout*) and TIMEOUT_*/CLIENT_* constants are declarations whose BEHAVIOR is audited where it runs — sv-commands-votes.timeout.state_machine (timeout consts + sv_timeout* values verified there) and sv-commands-votes.common.info_commands / dispatch rows (adminnick/status_privacy, VerifyClientEntity result codes). Cited here so the coverage ledger counts the file; no behavior is owned by this row.
- `console-commands.local.entity_debug_label` — Recorded divergence — The native listen host projects diagnostics from its rendered camera and reads native entity IDs and fields. Stock broadcasts the temporary QuakeC event to clients. Native diagnostics remain local to the listen host.
- `ctf.flag.spawn_setup` — Constant FLAG_SCALE (0.5625 / n/a (bbox hardcoded))
- `ctf.flag.touch_dispatch` — No FLAG_TOUCHRATE-gated world-touch sound/effect; monster/vehicle touch gating (g_ctf_allow_monster_touch/_vehicle_touch) not modeled.
- `ctf.flag.pickup` — No nade pickup bonus; no g_ctf_flag_stay phantom-flag clone.
- `ctf.flag.return` — No nade return bonus; monster-return notification (CTF_RETURN_MONSTER_<team>) not selected; damage/needkill/speedrun return reasons not distinguished (player-return + timeout auto-return modeled, plus return-when-dropped-near-base).
- `ctf.flag.fckill_score` — effective-HP test approximates QC healtharmor_maxdamage with health+armor (the per-deathtype armor-block percentage is not available at this seam) — a values approximation, not a logic break.
- `ctf.flag.pass` — Recorded divergence — A viable pass completes as an instant transfer to the receiver (the faithful OUTCOME) once the give-up conditions pass; the flight velocity is still computed for the integrating host. The arc-height line-of-sight trace + directional cone are a presentation/aim refinement deferred. A real MoveType.Fly/Toss integrator exists (MoveTypePhysics), so the instant transfer is a deliberate simplification, not a forced one. Pass does not physically arc (no arc-height trace, no pass_directional cone); it transfers instantly when viable. Constant g_ctf_pass_arc / _arc_max / directional_min/max (20 / 200 / 50 / 200 / not ported (straight-line aim))
- `ctf.capture_shield` — The per-viewer ctf_CaptureShield_Customize hide (shield model visible only to enemies, hidden from same-team) is not networked — the shield entity is shown to everyone. Minor; the model/force-push/centerprint are present.
- `ctf.hud.modicons` — Carrying detection uses ReferenceEquals(flag.Carrier, viewer) only; Base also treats the pass_sender as 'carrying' during a FLAG_PASSING state (minor: viewer-is-pass-sender shows 'taken' instead of 'carrying' mid-pass). The Passing state itself IS treated the same as Carried in the pack.
- `ctf.bot_role` — Carrier can't-find-flag watchdog clears route + forces re-rate instead of QC's DEATH_KILL suicide (no bot-layer damage path). Carrier/middle goalentity_lock_timeout (2-3s base-camp goal lock) intentionally omitted — a no-op at the 7s re-rate cadence. Oneflag: carrier routes to OWN base (the port's pinned capture rule, sv_ctf.qc:1186); QC's oneflag find_enemy_flag/enemybase bot call contradicts its own capture rule and is not mirrored.
- `ctf.flag.map_reset` — QC ctf_Reset also throws a carried flag with DROP_RESET (a visible in-place drop) before respawning it; the port collapses the throw+respawn into a direct ResetToBase since the immediately-following respawn makes the in-place drop unobservable (no behavioural difference). flag.enemy clear is a no-op (the port has no flag-health back-link).
- `ctf.ignore_frags` — Mechanism nuance (no observable divergence): QC returns autocvar_g_ctf_ignore_frags so at the default (0) the GiveFrags caller does NOT read back M_ARGV(2) and the original f=1 frag stands; the port ALWAYS returns true and relies on the call site's FragScore defaulting to 1f (Scores.cs:555). Both yield the identical result — default = normal +1 frag, cvar set = 0 frag/score. The unconditional return-true would only matter if a second CTF GiveFragsForKill subscriber existed (none does).
- `ctf.prefer_score_clear` — No-op on stock servers: g_score_resetonjoin defaults to 0, so ClearPlayerOnJoin short-circuits before the veto matters (faithful to Base, which also no-ops at 0). The veto only changes behavior under admin g_score_resetonjoin -1, where CTF then KEEPS the rejoiner's accumulated score.
- `cts.mode.register_latch` — All four MUTATOR_ONADD gameplay clamps are now modeled+live: Activate() forces g_race_qualifying=1 AND _independent_players=1 (Cts.cs:208-209) AND clears fraglimit=0/leadlimit=0 (Cts.cs:218-219, QC GameRules_limit_score/lead(0)), and the ActivateGameType arm asserts timelimit=20 when unset (GameWorld:3157-3158, QC gametype_init default). The independent latch is genuinely read live (DamageSystem.cs:172 independent-block, GameScores independent score columns). Residual (non-gameplay-value): the gametype 'cloaked' listing flag + the m_generate_mapinfo(target_startTimer) mapinfo-support hook are menu/mapinfo-listing concerns, not modeled. Constant cloaked + m_generate_mapinfo target_startTimer (gametype-listing 'cloaked' flag + mapinfo support keyed on target_startTimer / not modeled (menu/mapinfo-listing concern, not a gameplay value))
- `cts.timing.start_finish` — On the live path the run clock is now the frame-accumulated .race_movetime (OnPlayerPhysics, Cts.cs:745-752; split into count + [0,1) frac exactly as QC race.qc), zeroed in StartTimer and read at FinishStage (Cts.cs:326) — deterministic, frame-rate-independent. A wall-clock delta is retained only as a fallback when NO physics frame advanced the accumulator (e.g. the unit harness touching start->stop with no PlayerPhysics), which never happens on the live server path.
- `cts.timer.spawnfuncs` — EXACTTRIGGER exact-brush touch test (vs the placeholder AABB) and the waypoint_spawnforitem_force bot waypoint are not modeled.
- `cts.records.notifications` — RACE_FAIL_* notification token shape is approximated from RaceRecordResult (OldPos/NewPos/OldRecordTime); the QC race_diff color/sign tokens for the 'couldn't break' lines are a best-effort mirror, not byte-verified against every QC branch.
- `cts.physics.force_keyboard` — The race_penalty velocity-freeze branch (Base PlayerPhysics: set velocity=0 + MOVETYPE_NONE while race_penalty active) is not ported (tied to the checkpoint/penalty subsystem CTS omits). cl_movecliptokeyboard 2 is not stuffed to clients (no FixClientCvars hook). The server-side quantization (OnPlayerPhysics) makes this largely redundant for the analog-fairness goal, but the client-side clip is absent. Constant cl_movecliptokeyboard (settemp 2 (stuffcmd, FixClientCvars) / not stuffed (no FixClientCvars hook))
- `cts.weapon.shotgun_only` — g_cts_weapon_stay 2 (weapon-stay behavior for CTS map weapons) is not modeled. The port forces the shotgun via OwnedWeaponSet on spawn rather than the Base WantWeapon start_items filter, but the effect (start loadout = Shotgun only, mid-run map-weapon pickups kept) matches Base. Constant g_cts_weapon_stay (2 / not modeled)
- `cts.weapon.no_drop_throw` — The port folds both QC hooks (ForbidThrowCurrentWeapon + ForbidDropCurrentWeapon) into the single ForbidThrowCurrentWeapon chain — a documented per-port simplification; the effect (no throw, no drop) is correct because the chain is consulted by both IsWeaponThrowable (drop test, WeaponThrowing.cs:157) and ThrowWeapon (WeaponThrowing.cs:193).
- `cts.death.respawn_rules` — g_cts_removeprojectiles (delete the dead player's live projectiles when set) is unmodeled. Default is 0 (off) so the stock path matches; only an explicit g_cts_removeprojectiles 1 diverges (no projectile sweep on death). Constant g_cts_removeprojectiles (0 (off) / not modeled)
- `cts.items.loot_filter` — The QC ITEM_IS_LOOT test is faithful: OnFilterItemDefinition reads the real Entity.ItemIsLoot flag (Cts.cs:665), set by StartItem.SpawnInternal before the hook fires. Only the monster-drop SUB-CASE still keys off a classname tag (id.Contains("monster"), Cts.cs:672) because the port has no .monster_item flag. This branch is inert on stock servers (g_cts_drop_monster_items defaults 0), so it only matters with an explicit g_cts_drop_monster_items 1 — minor residual. QC MonsterDropItem (blanking the dropped item string when !g_cts_drop_monster_items) is folded into the same FilterItem path rather than a separate hook.
- `cts.speedaward` — Persistence rides the in-memory RaceRecords DB + Export/Import (the host must wire the file save), where QC auto-persists via ServerProgsDB db_save/db_load. uid2name consent gates (cl_allow_uidtracking/cl_allow_uid2name) are modeled only as a non-empty PersistentId.
- `cts.hud.cl_panel_gating` — Constant scoreboard rankings header (_("Rankings") in CTS / literal "Rankings:" header (always; not gametype-conditional relabel))
- `cts.lifecycle.prepare_player` — Constant race_checkpoint init (-1 on connect/observe/spawn / set to -1 in OnPlayerSpawn (Cts.cs:719) + OnMakePlayerObserver (Cts.cs:636); NOT on ClientConnect (no hook)) Constant g_allow_checkpoints (AbortSpeedrun practice mode) (0 / not modeled)
- `damage-pipeline.dispatch.front_gate` — QC game_stopped early-out (damage.qc:46/485) is still NOT checked inside Apply — gating lives at the gametype/caller layer; a damage call made while game_stopped would still apply. Spectator killcount==FRAGS_SPECTATOR early-out (damage.qc:485) is still gated at the gametype layer, not in Apply; functionally covered (observers get TakeDamage==No) but not at this seam.
- `damage-pipeline.resource.player_damage` — Pain anim selection (ANIMACTION_PAIN1/2) is host-side and not driven here (presentation); pain/death VOICE volume matches QC (VOL_BASE). The corpse upright/view-from-floor/MOVETYPE_TOSS state IS set here; only the keyframe anim picker is host-side.
- `damage-pipeline.knockback.calcpush` — Recorded divergence — MOVETYPE_PHYSICS path: QC spawns a transient FORCETYPE_FORCEATPOS entity (mass+pos aware, 0.1s) to nudge the rigid body; the headless port has no rigid body, so ApplyKnockback applies a mass-scaled impulse directly to velocity. Equivalent for the headless sim; deliberately not bit-identical for rotation/point-of-application. MOVETYPE_PHYSICS force-at-pos approximated as a direct mass-scaled velocity impulse (covered by divergence_rationale; not a sim-result gap for the headless model).
- `damage-pipeline.radius.splash` — MIN_DAMAGEEXTRARADIUS/damageextraradius per-target shrink (QC damage.qc:767 bound(0.1, damageextraradius, 16) subtracted from dist) is still not applied — targets with a custom damageextraradius (rare) hit at a slightly different range. Unchanged from baseline. RadiusDamage_running recursion guard (damage.qc:715-724) is replaced by per-call method-local target buffers (safe re-entry), so the explicit backtrace-on-recursion warning is gone (intentional, harmless). Constant MIN_DAMAGEEXTRARADIUS per-target dist shrink (bound(0.1, damageextraradius, 16) (damage.qc:767) / not applied)
- `damage-pipeline.heal.dispatcher` — QC game_stopped early-out (damage.qc:951) is NOT checked inside Heal — same pragmatic split as Damage(); the gate lives at the gametype caller layer. Low-risk. The .event_heal callback returns the healed bool; the per-target limit cap is the responsibility of the GtEventHeal sink (GeneratorHeal/IconHeal/HealVehicle clamp to max_health), matching QC where event_heal owns the cap — Heal() itself does not re-clamp.
- `deathmatch.scoring.frag_matrix` — OnDeath writes victim.GtDeaths/GtSuicides which have no readers (dead writes); the scoreboard's Deaths/Suicides come from Scores.GiveFrags read-through. Harmless, not player-visible.
- `deathmatch.scoring.teamkill_punishing` — Constant g_teamkill_punishing (0 (default off) / not read; flat -1 on teamkill) Constant penalty_curve (tk*(tk-1)*0.5 = -1,-2,-4,-7,-11,... / flat -1)
- `deathmatch.winconditions.fraglimit` — Recorded divergence — The port reads a non-Base convenience cvar g_dmlimit before falling back to the engine fraglimit. Base has no g_dmlimit (only TDM has g_tdm_point_limit). With g_dmlimit unset the behavior is identical to Base; it only diverges if an operator sets the invented cvar. Constant g_dmlimit cvar (does not exist in Base / checked before fraglimit)
- `deathmatch.announce.frags_remaining` — The QC suddenDeathEnding branch (checkrules_suddendeathend forcing fragsleft=1) is passed as false from DM (framework-owned, not surfaced). Minor: only affects the final sudden-death-end 'frag-left' cue, not the normal 1/2/3 approach announce.
- `domination.init.mutator_limits` — g_domination_teams_override>=2 (force-N and disable dom_team) handled for team count, but dom_team palette entities (per-team point models/sounds) are not implemented; the port hardcodes the default dom_<color>.md3 palette in DomPointModel.
- `domination.capture.touch` — IS_INDEPENDENT_PLAYER skip not mirrored (port has no independent-player concept here). Capture is instant (no neutral interim state); Base's neutral-during-delay is purely cosmetic (capturetime path is commented out in QC), so functionally equivalent.
- `domination.capture.notification` — Recorded divergence — Base sends a bprint (not the INFO notification) in the round-based variant; the port collapses both paths to the same INFO_DOMINATION_CAPTURE_TIME line. Cosmetic-only, and round-based is non-default. Round-based variant: Base uses bprint("^3%s^3%s") instead of the INFO notification; the port always sends the INFO line (see divergence_rationale).
- `domination.waypoint.sprite` — WaypointSprite_Ping on capture (a one-shot radar ping) is not separately emitted; the port rebuilds the sprite set each tick (pull model) so ownership/color updates flow, but the explicit ping flash on capture is absent.
- `domination.roundbased.player_blocked` — Recorded divergence — QC player_blocked also sets disableclientprediction=1 (a DarkPlaces networking stat that suppresses client-side movement prediction during the freeze). The port has no equivalent stat, so only the observable movement freeze (zeroed input + blocked jump) is reproduced; the prediction-suppression bit is intentionally not mirrored. QC sets/clears a literal per-player player_blocked field (reset_map_players=1, PlayerSpawn=!IsRoundStarted, Domination_RoundStart=0); the port instead reads the round handler's IsRoundStarted directly as the freeze window (RoundMoveForbidden = _ => !domRounds.IsRoundStarted). Equivalent window + observable effect, but no per-player flag is stored. disableclientprediction=1 not mirrored (see divergence_rationale).
- `duel.identity.registration` — leadlimit=0 is not modeled as a cvar seed, but 0 means 'no lead limit' and 1v1 FFA has no lead-limit semantics, so it is inert at the default. Would diverge only if a non-zero leadlimit were ever applied to duel (Base never does). Cosmetic.
- `duel.timing.respawn_delay` — Port does not model the per-gametype g_duel_respawn_delay_* / g_duel_weapon_stay override layer; reads the generic g_respawn_delay_small directly (Duel.RespawnDelay, default 2). Cosmetic at defaults (all duel overrides are 0 => Base falls back to the same generic 2s) but would diverge if an admin set a duel-specific respawn cvar. UNCHANGED.
- `freezetag.mode.registration` — Constant mapinfo defaults (timelimit=20 pointlimit=10 teams=2 leadlimit=6 / fraglimit→10 round-limit)
- `freezetag.revive.score` — Constant nade bonus (nades_GiveBonus(g_nades_bonus_score_low) / not awarded)
- `freezetag.win.roundlimit` — Constant lead limit default (mapinfo leadlimit 6 / leadlimit cvar else 0)
- `freezetag.presentation.ice_model` — Constant ice tint (colormod/glowmod = Team_ColorRGB(team) / team colormod through AttachedCosmeticModel)
- `freezetag.presentation.waypoints` — Frozen→Reviving swap heuristic: FIXED — FrozenState.HasReviver is now set in ReviveTick (n>0, FreezeTag.cs:709) and CollectWaypoints uses st.HasReviver || st.ReviveProgress>=0.95f (FreezeTag.cs:852), matching Base's n>0 || progress>0.95 exactly.
- `freezetag.spawn.late` — Base also handles the dead-player respawn path (freezetag_frozen_timeout <= -2 → freeze with no SPAWN_LATE center line) as a distinct case; the port folds mid-round (re)spawns into the single IsRoundStarted gate. Outcome (a mid-round materializing player ends up frozen) matches; the dead-vs-late distinction is cosmetic.
- `freezetag.notif.alone` — Base freezetag_LastPlayerForTeam_Notify is also invoked from the PlayerSpawn/teamchange paths (where a respawn/team-swap can leave a lone survivor); the port wires it ONLY through OnDeath (the freeze-on-frag path). The dominant freeze case is covered; a survivor created by a teammate's mid-round respawn/teamchange without a freeze would miss the ALONE cue — minor edge.
- `fx-deathtypes.registry.packing` — Recorded divergence — Port carries the deathtype as a string tag (DamageInfo.DeathType) end-to-end instead of a packed int; the bit math (weapon id, HITTYPE bits, special classification) is reproduced via HasHitType/BaseOf/IsWeapon. Behaviorally equivalent for routing; only the wire encoding differs. Documented in DeathTypes.cs. The string scheme now carries the full special set (nade family, cheat, rot, shooting_star, custom, etc.) as named constants. Constant DEATH_WEAPONMASK (BITS(8)=0xFF / weapon NetName via 'weapon/' prefix) Constant DT_FIRST (BIT(14) / bare lowercase special tag (no numeric base)) Constant HITTYPE flags (bits 8-13 (SECONDARY/SPLASH/BOUNCE/ARMORPIERCE/SOUND/SPAM) / '|flag' suffix tokens (same 6))
- `fx-deathtypes.message.weapon_select` — Recorded divergence — Per-weapon wr_killmessage/wr_suicidemessage logic is centralized in DeathMessages (weapon classes are owned by another subsystem and must not carry message logic). Each branch is copied 1:1 with per-line QC citations.
- `fx-deathtypes.name.lookup` — Recorded divergence — The port's DeathType IS a human-readable string tag, so it is its own name; a separate int->name lookup is unnecessary. GameLog/obituary use the raw tag directly.
- `fx-effectinfo.net.wire_protocol` — No encode/decode round-trip unit test; the byte-exact match is established by code read only.
- `fx-effectinfo.parser.tokenize` — Port is LENIENT where DP errors: DP's checkparms() aborts a block on wrong arg count and 'command before effect' breaks parsing; port silently skips. Also DP strtol base-0 treats a leading-0 token as octal; port reads it decimal. None occur in the shipped (generated) file, so no observable divergence. No unit test covers the parser / keyword table / baseline defaults; verified by code read only. (Proposed extraction to an Engine-namespace EffectInfoParser.cs + EffectInfoParserTests.cs has NOT landed in this tree as of 2026-06-28; parse core still lives in game/client/EffectInfo.cs.)
- `fx-effectinfo.parser.keywords` — Recorded divergence — Engine-only render keywords (lightshadow, lightcubemapnum, lightcorona, forcenearest) are parsed-and-ignored: Godot has no analogue for particle coronas, per-light cubemaps, or nearest-filtering. Unknown keywords are silently ignored rather than warned (DP prints a warning). These are deliberate, cosmetic-only omissions. Engine-only keywords (lightcorona/lightcubemapnum/lightshadow/forcenearest) unmodelled — cosmetic dlight/decal detail lost (intended divergence, not a defect).
- `fx-effectinfo.consume.lifetime_fallback` — Recorded divergence — Lifetime()/BuildFromInfo is the LEGACY heuristic GpuParticles3D fallback used only when the faithful/modern backends are not wired or for the warm/trail-sprite query. The default faithful backend (cl_particles_modern 0) computes per-particle life exactly (alpha/alphafade or time, whichever first). The clamp + midpoint is a deliberate approximation in the fallback path only. Fallback BuildFromInfo clamps derived life to [0.05,6]s and uses the alpha/time midpoint rather than per-particle; only hit when the routed backend is unavailable. Constant lifetime clamp (no clamp (per-particle alpha/time) / Clamp(life, 0.05, 6) midpoint)
- `fx-effectinfo.catalog.generator_sources` — Recorded divergence — The QC .inc DEF/SUB blocks (gated by ENABLE_EFFECTINFO) are only the dev-time dumpeffectinfo round-trip tool that REGENERATES effectinfo.txt; stock Xonotic never compiles or runs them at runtime (the DP engine parses the shipped txt directly, as does the port). A regen tool has no runtime behavior to port.
- `fx-notifications.registry.types` — A Send for an unregistered name is a no-op + LastError, and a missing MSG_MULTI sub is silently dropped — so any not-yet-registered cue is absent rather than erroring (vs QC's init-time fatal check). Constant Base all.inc macro invocations (80 ANNCE + 237 INFO + 197 CENTER + 166 MULTI + 13 CHOICE + 82 TEAM/MULTITEAM / n/a (different structuring))
- `fx-notifications.net.protocol` — Recorded divergence — Port sends the server-FORMATTED text plus the raw args and the notification's RegistryId (vs Base sending only id+args and re-formatting on CSQC). The RegistryId lets the client recover the Notification to read icon/cpid/queuetime/durcnt. This is a deliberate consequence of the port doing token expansion server-side (FormatTokens). One real residual consequence: the location/spree display cvars and gentle mode are read SERVER-side, so per-client control of those is lost (see fx-notifications.args.tokens, .gentle). The durcnt-encoded duration is NO LONGER dropped — it is recovered client-side from the notification's Durcnt field via the RegistryId (see fx-notifications.center.durcnt). Per-client display cvars (notification_show_location / _show_sprees*) and gentle mode cannot vary per recipient — the server's value applies to everyone (see fx-notifications.args.tokens, fx-notifications.gentle.variants). Constant wire payload (byte type + short name + strings + longs (re-formatted CSQC-side) / ushort RegistryId + byte type(=WireType) + server-FORMATTED text + str[] + float[])
