The port has no phys_land event/subscription bus; the jumppadcount reset is open-coded at the landing site rather than dispatched through an event. Structural, no observable difference.
- `playerstats-xonstat.gamereport.identity_consent` — Constant uid used only when cvar_cl_allow_uidtracking == 1 (yes (playerstats.qc:86) / NO gate: PersistentId used unconditionally) Constant _netname only when cvar_cl_allow_uid2name == 1 || bot (yes (playerstats.qc:196) / NO gate: NetName always written (PlayerStats.cs:256)) Constant _ranked (r line) from cvar_cl_allow_uidranking (yes, real clients (playerstats.qc:223) / never written) Constant re-AddPlayer on cl_allow_uidtracking change (REPLICATE_APPLYCHANGE (playerstats.qc:26) / absent)
- `playerstats-xonstat.gamereport.event_feeds` — Constant kill-spree milestones (3/5/10/15/20/25/30 / event ids registered (PlayerStats.cs:119) but never incremented) Constant accuracy suffixes (real/hit/fired/cnt-hit/cnt-fired/frags per weapon netname / ids registered (PlayerStats.cs:111-116); WeaponAccuracy holds the values; no bridge)
- `playerstats-xonstat.gamereport.alivetime` — Constant flush points (death + observer transition + finalize / finalize only)
- `playerstats-xonstat.gamereport.avglatency` — Constant latency source (CS(p).latency_sum / latency_cnt (10s PingPLReport sampler, world.qc) / PingProvider default 0 — no sampler exists)
- `playerstats-xonstat.report.payload_v9` — Constant G gametype (PlayerStats_GetGametype() (GetGametype + duel hack when g_maxplayers==2 && <=2 players) / cvar g_playerstats_gametype — set NOWHERE, always blank) Constant D duration (max(0, time - game_starttime) / max(0, now) where now = absolute sim time (GameWorld.cs:4067 passes Time)) Constant R release / I matchid / C purechanges / U port / RP rounds / L ladder / r ranked (all emitted (R via WATERMARK, RP if rounds_played>0, L if g_playerstats_gamereport_ladder set) / none emitted)
- `playerstats-xonstat.submission.http_upload` — Constant SERVERFLAG_PLAYERSTATS / _CUSTOM (BIT(2)/BIT(3), set at Init (requires DP_CRYPTO + DP_QC_URI_POST for the official flag) / bits defined (ServerFlags.cs:29-33) but never set anywhere) Constant DelayMapVote cleared by async CLOSED/ERROR callback (yes (playerstats.qc:508/521) / cleared synchronously in GameReport (intended: vote never blocked))
- `playerstats-xonstat.playerbasic.skill_fetch` — Constant g_playerstats_playerbasic_uri default (https://stats.xonotic.org / same value sits in the live store (shipped xonotic-server.cfg:638, exec'd at boot) but NO code ever reads it) Constant activation gate (uri != "" && (g_balance_teams_skill || sv_showskill) / none) Constant skill mapping (m_skill_var = sigma^2; m_skill_mu = max(0, mu); SP_SKILL = mu + 1 (json '0.game_type_cd' must match gametype); unknown = -1 / none (SKILL column stays at its cleared value)) Constant status codes (PS_B_STATUS ERROR=-2 IDLE=-1 WAITING=0 RECEIVED=1 UPDATING=2 / none)
- `playerstats-xonstat.playerdetail.profile_fetch` — Constant g_playerstats_playerdetail_uri default (https://stats.xonotic.org/player/me / same value sits in the live store (shipped xonotic-server.cfg:639, exec'd at menu boot) but NO code ever reads it) Constant autoupdate interval (g_playerstats_playerdetail_autoupdatetime = 1800 s, also refetch when cl_matchcount increases / 1800 in the store (xonotic-server.cfg:640) but no refetch logic exists to consume it) Constant request body (V 1 + R watermark + l language + n _cl_name + m _cl_playermodel _cl_playerskin / none) Constant response keys (V/R/T/S/P/n/i general+player info; G switches gametype namespace; 'e <event> <data>' -> '<gametype>/<event>' items / none) Constant activation gate (uri != "" && crypto_getmyidstatus(0) > 0 / none)
- `playerstats-xonstat.urllib.http_client` — Constant request slot pool (URI_GET_URLLIB 128..191 (64 concurrent), _urllib_nextslot round-robin persisted as a cvar across map changes / none) Constant URL_READY codes (ERROR=-1 (or -HTTP status), CLOSED=0, CANWRITE=1, CANREAD=2 / none) Constant multi-URI failover (space-separated URI list tried in order; HTTP 422 aborts the chain (urllib.qc:336) / none) Constant POST content type (text/plain via crypto_uri_postbuf (DP_QC_URI_POST) / none)
- `playerstats-xonstat.json.parser` — Recorded divergence — lib/json.qc is a QC-VM workaround for the engine's lack of a JSON facility (hand-rolled recursive descent over a string iterator into a flat bufstr). .NET has System.Text.Json (already used elsewhere in the port, e.g. game/net/CameraTrace.cs:65); re-implementing the QC parser would be pure waste. The only Base consumers relevant to gameplay are the playerbasic skill handler (this unit's skill_fetch row — the '0.game_type_cd'/'0.mu'/'0.sigma' key-path reads map to trivial JsonDocument access) and lib/matrix (out of scope). Cited here so the coverage ledger accounts for the file; the observable behavior is owned by playerstats-xonstat.playerbasic.skill_fetch. Constant key-path flattening (nested keys join with '.', arrays index 0..n + '<ns>.length'; true/false/null -> 1/0/"" / n/a) Constant escape support (\" \\ \n \t \/ (\u unimplemented TODO) / n/a)
- `race.mode.setup_limits` — Constant g_race_laps_limit (default) (-1 (use mapinfo); 0 = unlimited / 7 (DefaultLapLimit fallback)) Constant gametype legacy defaults (timelimit=20 qualifying=5 laplimit=7 teamlaplimit=15 leadlimit=0 / laplimit=7 + timelimit=20 (seeded at activation, GameWorld.cs:3106-3107) when unset; teamlaplimit/qualifying/leadlimit defaults still unseeded)
- `race.checkpoint.entities` — Defrag target_checkpoint / target_startTimer / target_stopTimer chain not modeled for Race (CTS-only no-ops).
- `race.checkpoint.ordered_crossing` — Uses DeathTypes.Void instead of Base's DEATH_HURTTRIGGER for the wrong-way kill (cosmetic deathtype/obituary only; the 10000 damage matches). No SUB_UseTargets / equipment-clear (Portal_ClearAll, porto_forbidden) on crossing. Constant wrong-CP kill damage (spawnflag 4) (10000 (DEATH_HURTTRIGGER) / 10000 (DeathTypes.Void))
- `race.records.notifications` — Notification time tokens are TIME_ENCODE'd ints (matches Base race_diff/race_time HUD tokens); not yet verified visually in a live client, but the dispatch path is the same NotificationSystem.Info used by Ctf/Domination.
- `race.penalty.zones` — disableclientprediction = 2 (Base) is not set during the freeze; the client may mispredict through the freeze (no per-player client-prediction-disable field in the port; documented cross-boundary). RACE_NET_PENALTY_* networking (the on-screen penalty line) is not sent over the wire — penalty HUD line is fed host-side only (LastPenaltySeconds/EventTime/Reason populated, but no net stream for a pure remote client).
- `race.win.condition` — Sudden-death is modeled as a Race-local latch (SuddenDeath) gated on the RaceCompleting state; the shared OverTimeManager deliberately excludes the race/qualifying branch, so the standard overtime-window timer does not gate race end (intentional split, documented in OverTimeManager.cs).
- `race.movement.quantization` — cl_movecliptokeyboard is now SET to 2 at activation (Race.cs:540, the FixClientCvars stuffcmd analogue) but NOTHING reads it — no consumer in src/ or game/ (and it is not in docs/reference/CVARS.md), so the client input clip is still not actually forced. The write is live but its effect is dead; the server-side octant snap remains the only authority. Residual gap unchanged in observable behavior. disableclientprediction=2 during the freeze has no port mechanism (documented cross-boundary). Constant cl_movecliptokeyboard (stuffed to 2 (FixClientCvars) / set to 2 globally at activation (Race.cs:540) — but the cvar has NO reader in src/ or game/ (not in docs/reference/CVARS.md), so the client input clip is still not forced)
- `race.hud.split_timer` — On a pure (remote) client the feed is still absent — there is no TE_CSQC_RACE net message; the feed is host-side (NetGame.UpdateRacePanels reads the live Race gametype's RaceState + per-checkpoint record store). On a listen server the timer/splits/anticipation/speed/medal all render live.
- `race.hud.checkpoint_splits` — Host-side feed only (no TE_CSQC_RACE net path for a pure client). On a listen server StoreSplit is now called per crossing (QC StoreCheckpointSplits) and ClearSplits on a fresh lap, so the panel renders the live split list.
- `race.bot.role` — The raw_touch_check warpzone redirect (QC: a bot within 30qu of an untouchable checkpoint's nearestwaypoint advances to race_NextCheckpoint(cp)) is not modeled — a bot can stall on an unreachable gate (e.g. Stormkeep's warpzone checkpoint). The port falls back to rating ALL checkpoints if the expected gate isn't resolvable, but does not skip a touchable-but-unreachable one.
- `race.speed_awards` — All-time-best persistence keys off PersistentId (non-empty uid) rather than the cl_allow_uid* consent gate Base uses (same uid-gate divergence as the record DB).
- `resources.api.registry` — Resource metadata objects (ammo m_name/m_icon/m_color, fuel m_hidden) are not on a Resource object; ammo HUD coloring/labels are handled in the panel/items layer instead (cosmetic-equivalent). Base networks ammo_fuel as a STAT (_STAT(FUEL)) and the others as legacy fields; the port networks all resources via Entity fields. Behaviorally equivalent for the HUD.
- `resources.api.getlimit` — QC IS_PLAYER(e) is approximated by EntFlags.Client; a non-player entity that happens to carry the Client flag (or vice-versa) would diverge on the limit. No known live case where this matters (only players run the regen tick / get resource caps).
- `resources.cl.readout` — Port does not run a separate CSQC resource module; the HUD reads the networked Entity values directly via Resources.GetResource (behaviorally equivalent for presentation). The CSQC-only SetResource/TakeResource/TakeResourceWithLimit forms (cl_resources.qc, including the known-odd CSQC TakeResourceWithLimit formula) are not ported (no need).
- `resources.api.ammoconsumption` — Port hardcodes per-weapon primary-ammo fallbacks (PrimaryAmmoFallback table) for the unset-cvar case rather than reading the weapon registry's own default; values match the stock g_balance_*_primary_ammo and each weapon's Configure() fallback, so equivalent at stock. A non-stock weapon balance reached via a live cvar is honored (Bal reads the live cvar when set).
- `scoring.rules.scorerules_basics` — SP_SKILL gating (sv_showskill 1/2) is not honored by a live skill column (the column registers but stays empty); cosmetic at the default sv_showskill 0. Constant sv_showskill (0 / not gating a live skill column)
- `scoring.compare.field_player_team` — Final tiebreak differs: QC PlayerScore_Compare ends with t1.owner.playerid - t2.owner.playerid; the port returns 0 (stable-sort order) when all columns tie. Only affects the display order of two players with byte-identical scoreboards — no gameplay effect. Constant compare order (primary, secondary, registration-order others, playerid / primary, secondary, registry-order labeled non-prio, 0)
- `scoring.winningcondition.scores` — The QC 'topscore 0 with ZERO_IS_WORST => +/-999999999' and 'empty server forces equality=0' special cases were not located in the port; an all-zero / empty-server end-of-time tie may resolve differently. Low impact (degenerate cases). Constant empty server forces equality=0 (0:0 still ends) (player_count==0 / unverified)
- `scoring.net.replication` — Recorded divergence — The port replaces QC's three per-entity linked-entity classes (ENT_CLIENT_SCORES_INFO/SCORES/TEAMSCORES with per-field SendFlags) with two snapshot-channel blocks gated by LayoutGeneration/Version (ADR-0007/0011: the port owns the score layout and networks the whole table positionally). Wire-equivalent: positional, int24, change-gated; a remote --connect client applies ScoreInfoBlock so its label-derived NetworkedFields hash matches the server.
- `scoring.report.playerstats` — Recorded divergence — The HTTP upload itself is left to an engine concern: PlayerStats.BuildReport produces the V9 body and GameReport clears DelayMapVote synchronously (QC clears it in the async url callback), so map voting is never blocked. The Godot-free core builds a wire-compatible report; the POST transport is out of the core. Handicap given/taken averaging and avglatency depend on host-wired providers (PingProvider defaults to 0); skill (SP_SKILL) is not fetched from a playerbasic/XonStat endpoint. The report is wire-compatible but some advanced fields are 0 without host wiring.
- `scoring.helper.float2int_decimal_carry` — Powerup/excess edge: effective = FragDamage - max(0, FragDamage - take - save) mirrors QC, but the per-player _scoreDecimal carry is keyed by Player and cleared only on Activate/Deactivate (not per-round); QC's float2int_decimal_fld is a persistent player field too, so this matches, but a player object reused across a full map reset relies on the Activate clear. Low risk.
- `scoring.display.niceprint` — Score_NicePrint's race/cts INTERMISSION broadcast (Base calls Score_NicePrint(NULL) at sv_race.qc:203 / sv_cts.qc:150 when the round ends, broadcasting the standings to all) is not auto-emitted at intermission in the port — only the on-demand `teamstatus` per-caller dump is wired. Low impact: race/cts players still see the networked scoreboard HUD.
- `scoring.display.scorelog` — Recorded divergence — The per-call score-log FILE sink (Base appends each dump to sv_logscores_filename via a fopen/fclose) is omitted; the port's structured event-log file sink (sv_eventlog_files) already covers file logging, so the dedicated scores.log file is not reproduced. The console + eventlog sinks (the parsed-by-tooling outputs) are faithful. The dedicated sv_logscores_filename file output (Base's fopen-append of the score-log to scores.log) is not written; only the console + eventlog sinks are emitted. The sv_logscores_file cvar is registered but does not drive a separate file. Low impact: server-admin file-logging convenience superseded by the event-log file. Constant file sink (sv_logscores_filename append) (per-call file open/close / omitted (port relies on sv_eventlog_files for structured logging))
- `scoring.accuracy.tracking` — QC's dead-target exception (damage in the exact frame the target died still counts: IS_DEAD && time > death_time) is replaced by a plain not-dead test; documented in-code as behaviourally identical because every port credit site evaluates the gate BEFORE Damage() while the target is alive. Holds today; any future post-damage credit site would silently diverge.
- `scoring.accuracy.networking` — Constant sv_accuracy_data_share (1 / cvar absent (CVAR-DIFF.md)) Constant cl_accuracy_data_share / cl_accuracy_data_receive (replicated client cvars / absent)
- `scoring.status.getstatus_strings` — Constant g_full_getstatus_responses (declared server/scores.qh:5 (default 0) / cvar absent)
- `scoring.rules.scorerules_basics_end` — Recorded divergence — The port has no explicit basics_end/ScoreInfo_Init call: the layout re-broadcast is event-driven (LayoutGeneration bumps on every actual label/flag change; the ScoreInfo snapshot block change-gates on the stamp — same ADR-0007/0011 snapshot-channel divergence recorded in scoring.net.replication). The NumTeams/Team_InitTeams side effect is owned by the port's teamplay bootstrap, not the scoring finalize. Constant NumTeams bitmask -> team count -> Team_InitTeams (scores_rules.qc:12-15,27-30 / team init handled by the teamplay path in GameWorld/Teamplay, not by the scoring finalize)
- `survival.round.handler` — Surv_CheckWinner's QC re-Init side effect after a round (round_handler_Init(5, warmup, timelimit) re-arming the next round inside the win check) is handled by the generic RoundHandler.OnRoundReset → ResetMap path rather than an explicit re-Init call; behaviorally equivalent for the next-round cadence but not a literal port of the QC re-Init line.
- `survival.scoring.bank_validkills` — Frag value is a fixed ±1/±2 (hardcoded) rather than carrying an arbitrary GiveFrags multiplier; matches stock 1-per-kill exactly but would diverge under a non-1 frag-value multiplier (no such multiplier ships).
- `survival.scoring.round_end_award` — QC also adds banked kills to totalfrags (it.totalfrags += it.survival_validkills, sv_survival.qc:59); the port's totalfrags equivalent is not updated in UpdateScores (minor — stock scoreboard reads SP_SCORE).
- `survival.win.timeout` — The timeout branch's other QC side effects (nades_RemovePlayer, allowed_to_spawn=false, game_stopped=true, round_handler re-Init, survivalStatuses SendFlags) are not all reproduced in EndRoundTimedOut beyond the score award + RoundResult notify (the re-Init/next-round arming is handled by the generic OnRoundReset path instead).
- `survival.death.eliminate` — QC clears the bot command queue (bot_clearqueue) on a bot's death; the port has no bot command-queue subsystem to clear (noted at Survival.cs:369-370) — behaviorally moot (the bot simply stays eliminated). surv_isEliminated also flags joining players (INGAME_JOINING / FRAGS_PLAYER_OUT_OF_GAME); the port has no joining state, so a late joiner is not marked eliminated until the next AssignRoles.
- `survival.death.punish_teamkill` — The mirror-damage death recurses back into OnDeath (Combat.Damage → Combat.Death → OnDeath(attacker)) within the same call; functionally the attacker is eliminated, but there is no explicit re-entrancy guard and the recursive death is not unit-tested. ITEM_DAMAGE_NEEDKILL maps DEATH_HURTTRIGGER → the port's Void; the precise hurt-trigger ↔ void equivalence is an approximation (DeathTypes.Void stands in for HURTTRIGGER).
- `survival.net.hidden_role_disclosure` — Recorded divergence — QC uses a CSQC linked entity (survivalStatuses) with a maxclients-block bitfield; the port carries the same data in the per-mode GametypeStatusBlock as a net-id list (the port has stable net ids, not maxclients bit slots). Behaviorally equivalent on the disclosure invariant; the wire shape differs by design.
- `survival.presentation.player_colors_and_notifications` — cl_surv DrawScoreboard_Force (show the scoreboard while GAME_STOPPED so players see who the hunter was) is not modeled as a Survival-specific force — the round-end disclosure colors are applied to player models/scoreboard, but the scoreboard is not auto-forced open at GAME_STOPPED.
- `survival.bot.forbid_attack_allies` — Minor: the port restricts the veto to `self is Player && targ is Player` (GameWorld.cs:2927-2929). Base evaluates `targ.survival_status == bot.survival_status` unconditionally — for non-player targets both sides read 0/None and Base would also veto when the bot is prey/None. The port does NOT veto non-player targets. With no monsters in stock Survival this is behaviorally moot, but it is a small narrowing of Base's rule.
- `survival.join.late_join_observe` — Recorded divergence — Base splits the lockout across two QC hooks (ForbidSpawn forbids INGAME players; PutClientInServer force-TRANSMUTEs the rest to Observer + INGAME_STATUS_JOINING + INFO_CA_JOIN_LATE). The port collapses both into a single join-gate outcome — CanJoin refuses every join while the round is live so the would-be joiner stays an observer (the same net state Base leaves it in) until the next round reset re-admits everyone (AssignRoles). The combined behavioral outcome (no client enters the world mid-round) matches; the INFO_CA_JOIN_LATE 'you joined late' info-print and the explicit INGAME_STATUS_JOINING bookkeeping are not separately reproduced (see gaps). The INFO_CA_JOIN_LATE 'you joined too late, sit out this round' info-print and QC's INGAME_STATUS_JOINING marking are not reproduced — the port refuses the join outright rather than admitting an observer with a joining flag. Behaviorally equivalent (the client sits out until the next round) but the player gets no explicit 'join late' message, and surv_isEliminated's INGAME_JOINING branch (the late joiner counted as eliminated this round) has no port analogue (the refused client simply isn't tracked until OnJoin/AssignRoles).
- `sv-anticheat.core.power_mean` — Recorded divergence — Mean uses double internally vs QC float; verdicts are threshold-coarse so precision is immaterial. Deliberate. Constant internal precision (float / double)
- `sv-anticheat.frame.evasion_phase_walk` — Recorded divergence — RNG is a seeded deterministic Random(0x4317) vs Base unseeded random(); the phase walk is intentionally noisy so the exact sequence is irrelevant to verdicts. Deterministic seed chosen for reproducible headless sims.
- `sv-anticheat.frame.fixangle_window` — Recorded divergence — The Base EndFrame FOREACH_CLIENT(.fixangle) catch-all is intentionally not propagated: the port's only two server-side forced-view sources — respawn (InfraClientSpawn -> FixAngle, GameWorld.cs:1214) and teleport (Teleporters.OnPlayerFixAngle -> FixAngle, GameWorld.cs:1004) — both open the window directly at their call site, exactly as Base calls anticheat_fixangle straight from teleporters.qc. The networked Player.FixAngle flag has no server-side per-frame reset in the port (the client predictor/listen host own it), so feeding it through EndFrame would perpetually re-arm the window. Passing no fixAngleClients is the correct port design. STILL OPEN (values only): AntiCheat.PingProvider hardcoded to 0 (GameWorld.cs:997: AntiCheat.PingProvider = _ => 0f) and no net host overrides it, so the suppression window is always 0.2s instead of min(0.4,ping*0.001)+0.2 — too short for laggy remote clients on a dedicated host. Consistent with the rest of the port (PlayerStats.PingProvider is also default 0; there is no server-side real-ping source wired anywhere), and listen-server/bot ping is 0 regardless, so this only bites remote clients on a dedicated host. Constant applied via endframe FOREACH_CLIENT(it.fixangle) (yes (world.qc:2505 -> anticheat_endframe) / intended divergence: the two server-side .fixangle sources (spawn + teleport) call FixAngle directly at their call site (exactly as Base calls anticheat_fixangle straight from teleporters.qc); the networked Player.FixAngle flag has no server-side per-frame reset, so EndFrame(GameWorld.cs:1798) passes no fixAngleClients by design) Constant ping fed to window (real per-client ping / AntiCheat.PingProvider STILL hardcoded 0f (GameWorld.cs:997); no net host override)
- `sv-antilag.history.sample` — An older-than-oldest takeback time returns the oldest recorded sample instead of QC's 'present' (e.origin); only reachable past the 0.4s cap / ~1s buffer depth, so unreachable in normal play. Constant older-than-oldest case (collapses to present (e.origin) / clamps to oldest sample)
- `sv-antilag.mode.client_verified_and_cursor` — Constant g_antilag mode 1 ghost-trace correction (redirect w_shotdir on miss-then-antilag-hit / none) Constant g_antilag mode 3 cursor redirect (redirect w_shotdir toward cursor_trace_ent / none)
- `sv-antilag.hitplot.lifecycle` — Recorded divergence — IPv6 address separators are flattened in the filename for Windows compatibility. Address selection uses the original peer address.
- `sv-campaign.file.parse` — Too-few-fields row is logged + the index slot consumed (parity with QC index advance) rather than QC's error()/abort — benign. Base wraps the campaign filename in language_filename() (localized campaign briefings, e.g. campaignxonoticbeta.de.txt); the port reads the un-localized base name only. Cosmetic for English; non-English campaign text falls back to the base file. Constant filename localization (language_filename(maps/campaign<name>.txt) / maps/campaign<name>.txt (no language_filename prefix))
- `sv-campaign.preinit.abort` — QC _campaign_testrun makes the cheats/unknown-map bailout error() (hard crash, intentional for file verification); the port aborts gracefully (Aborted=true). Minor and testrun-only. QC's cheats bailout calls MapInfo_SwitchGameType(gametype[0]) BEFORE unloading + bailing (so the engine still lands on a sane gametype); the port skips that pre-bailout gametype switch — moot because Aborted falls back to normal play.
- `sv-campaign.transition.next_level` — Recorded divergence — QC CampaignSetup issues disconnect / maxplayers 16 / MapInfo_LoadMap via localcmd; the port reboots the in-process listen server (Shell) preserving campaign mode/index. Equivalent host plumbing for an in-process Godot listen server, not a behavior change. Host plumbing differs (Shell reboot vs QC localcmd disconnect/maxplayers/MapInfo_LoadMap) — intended divergence, not a defect.
- `sv-campaign.warp.command` — QC errors (hard fail 'Sorry, cheater') on an out-of-range warp target; the port logs a benign message instead. Console-only, minor.
- `sv-campaign.mapentity.gametype_switch` — Recorded divergence — QC MapInfo_SwitchGameType(MapInfo_Type_FromString(gametype)) switches the active gametype in-engine; on the in-process listen server the port equivalent sets the live `gametype` cvar so the rebooted/changelevel'd map comes up in that mode (the same mechanism Commands.CmdGameType uses). Host-plumbing equivalence, not a behavior change.
- `sv-chat.flood.per_type_throttle` — Constant wrap_width_px (82.4289758859709 / 82 (int visible chars))
- `sv-chat.format.crosshair_tokens` — %y/%x trace the player's VIEW ANGLES from the eyes (origin+view_ofs out to CurrentMaxShotDistance) rather than Base's CSQC-reported cursor (WarpZone_crosshair_trace_plusvisibletriggers, chat.qc:535); the port does not network the cursor, so an aim that diverges from the view axis (free-look) resolves a different endpoint/entity. Functionally live, faithful approximation.
- `sv-chat.mutator.hooks` — Recorded divergence — No chat mutator bus in the port. Verified these four hooks have NO core subscribers in Base (declared in events.qh, invoked only by chat.qc itself), so omitting them has zero observable effect on stock gameplay. A future custom-mutator path could re-add them.
- `sv-cheats.cmd.usetarget` — Minor logic caveat: the port AddCheats(1) runs even when no target name is given (FireNamedTarget early-returns on empty argv[1]). Base ALSO DID_CHEATs unconditionally and calls SUB_UseTargets with an empty .target (which no-ops), so the cheat-count effect is identical; the only divergence is the port skips the transient-entity spawn on empty input (an unobservable optimization).
- `sv-cheats.cmd.killtarget` — Same minor empty-arg caveat as usetarget: AddCheats(1) fires on an empty target name (FireNamedTarget early-returns before spawning), but Base also DID_CHEATs unconditionally, so the cheat-count effect is identical.
- `sv-cheats.impulse.teleport` — Constant MoveToRandomMapLocation attempts (100 (sv_cheats<2) / 100000 / none (uses a spawnpoint-sampling fallback, no attempt-count tuning))
- `sv-cheats.frame.drag` — Constant initial dragspeed (64 / none) Constant draggee gravity while held (0.00001 / none)
- `sv-client-lifecycle.join.join_allowed` — Constant g_maxping (0 (off) / none) Constant g_maxplayers (0 (off) -> GetPlayerLimit/nJoinAllowed / none)
- `sv-client-lifecycle.dead.respawn_state_machine` — The speedrun/g_race_qualifying personal-respawn early branch and player_anim are not in this path (the ShowRespawnCountdown announcer IS now called each tick).
- `sv-client-lifecycle.dead.respawn_timing` — CalculateRespawnTime mutator hook (a mode overriding the delay) not modeled; GAMETYPE_DEFAULTED_SETTING per-gametype overrides resolve via cvar fallbacks rather than the gametype default table.
- `sv-client-lifecycle.frame.player_powerups` — MF_ROCKET jetpack modelflag (IT_USING_JETPACK) is dropped — the port has no Entity.modelflags render channel (the jetpack fires its own trail effect, so this is cosmetic only).
- `sv-client-lifecycle.frame.idle_kick` — The dedicated-server sv_maxidle_slots free-slot threshold (maxclients - totalClients > sv_maxidle_slots disables the kick) is NOT enforced — the headless core has no engine-level maxclients to read, so the slots gate is a no-op (the cvars are read but skipped). Off by default; only affects a dedicated operator who sets sv_maxidle_slots. Constant sv_maxidle_slots (0 (dedicated free-slot threshold) / read but gate skipped (no engine maxclients))
- `sv-client-lifecycle.frame.spectator_kick` — The port resets the grace clock via the spectator's JoinTime (QC spectatortime) rather than a dedicated spectatortime field; behaviorally equivalent (spectatortime is set to jointime on PutObserverInServer in QC). The dropclient_schedule deferred-drop is realised as a direct ClientDisconnect after the grace, which is functionally the same outcome.
- `sv-client-lifecycle.frame.name_and_version` — QC interleaves ignore_list_send (client.qc:2947) between the name check and GOD MODE block; that is a separate ignore-list feature and remains deferred (tracked under the disconnect/prethink rows). isInvisibleString is an approximation (strip-colors + whitespace/control/nbsp test) rather than the exact QC invisible-glyph table, but catches the common ''/'^9 ' abuse.
- `sv-clientkill.countdown.delay` — Player and clone first thinks include the copy ordinal stagger, sampled on fixed simulation ticks.
- `sv-clientkill.announcer.number` — The spoken kill countdown stays silent by default (NUM_KILL ships disabled — Base N___NEVER), which is faithful; it becomes live the instant the player enables the kill announcer.
- `sv-clientkill.centerprint.teamchange` — The attached digit receives the selected team tint through the snapshot. See indicator.entity. Constant colormod (black '0 0 0' / grey '0.5 0.5 0.5' / Team_ColorRGB / none (digit-entity detail, not networked))
- `sv-clientkill.teamchange.deferred` — The deferred move resolves through KillCountdown.PerformTeamChange (GameWorld.cs:979): -2 → PutObserverInServer; >0 → SetTeam + KillPlayerForTeamChange (+ synchronous bot autobalance for a human) + RemoveExcessPlayers tail. The auto (-1) defer-to-Join when team<=0 and the 'already on a best team' early-out are both handled in CmdSelectTeam (Commands.cs:1620/1635). Residual: lip-stagger first-think offset on the indicator is cosmetic-only (shared with countdown.delay).
- `sv-clientkill.mutator.hooks` — Models the three live gametype hooks (ft block / cts / race) via a single host-wired ClientKillHook; the generic EV_ClientKill_Now mutator pipeline (events.qh:951, beyond these three gametypes) is not a general dispatch — but no other Base mutator subscribes to it, so coverage is complete for the shipped set.
- `sv-clientkill.silent.cts_finish` — ScheduleRetract uses a private _retractAt timer rather than routing the CTS finish through KillCountdown.BeginSilent, but the expiry now runs the real silent DEATH_KILL (not a respawn), honors the kill delay, and handles 0/-1 — behaviorally equivalent to ClientKill_Silent.
- `sv-clientkill.clientkill_now.vehicle_exit` — Sentinel modeled differently: Base sets vehicle_health=-1 then deals 1 DEATH_KILL damage to trigger the death; the port can't set a separate QC float so it deals (health+1) DEATH_KILL to kill the empty vehicle outright. Behaviorally equivalent (vehicle dies). PerformKill always runs the kill-vehicle branch because team changes route through PerformTeamChange, so the QC `if (!killindicator_teamchange)` guard is implicitly always-true here (correct).
- `sv-clientkill.teamchange.excess_removal` — The QueuedPlayersTagIn guard (Base: skip removal when queued players are about to tag in) is not modeled — the port does not implement the join queue, so RemoveExcessPlayers is always called and self-gates on g_balance_teams_remove (default 0). Off by default, no behavioral divergence in stock config.
- `sv-commands-votes.dispatch.server_console` — 8 SERVER_COMMANDS still unimplemented (see sv-commands-votes.debug_tools.missing row); the dispatch shell itself is faithful. (Down from ~12 — effectindexdump now implemented this re-audit, after adminmsg/delrec/printstats, after anticheat.)
- `sv-commands-votes.ban.sync_online` — Recorded divergence — The HTTP cross-server ban propagation (uri_get) is deliberately out of the Godot-free server core; the local ban store + console surface is complete. Documented in Bans.cs class comment. No cross-server ban sync: bans are local-only; banlist providers/trusted-servers cvars are inert. Constant g_ban_sync_uri ("" / none) Constant g_ban_sync_interval (5 (min) / none)
- `sv-commands-votes.common.info_commands` — who: the ping / packetloss columns carry 0 in the Godot-free core (the net host owns live per-client ping/pl; the headless core has no source). The column exists and is formatted; only the value is 0. Documented core limitation, not a logic gap.
- `sv-commands-votes.admin.adminmsg_delrec_printstats` — adminmsg: the headless core has no infobar, so the infobartime branch falls back to the centerprint+chat delivery (value accepted for cfg compatibility). The centerprint/chat lines themselves are faithful.
- `sv-handicap.damage.give_take_scaling` — HandicapTotal still has no explicit HANDICAP_DISABLED() gate of its own, but the residual is moot: the voluntary provider returns 1 in CTS/Race and the only forced setter (the dynamic_handicap mutator) is itself disabled in CTS/Race, so the product stays 1 there. A future non-mutator forced setter that bypassed the gate could still diverge.
- `sv-handicap.voluntary.cl_handicap_cvars` — Base stores handicap_value as int (truncates a fractional cvar like 2.5 to 2 before bound); the port keeps the float, so fractional voluntary handicaps differ slightly. Negligible (cvars are practically integers) but not bit-exact. Constant int truncation of handicap_value (int handicap_value (cvar value truncated to int before bound) / float (no truncation; e.g. cl_handicap_damage_taken 2.5 -> 2.5 not 2))
- `sv-handicap.scoreboard.handicap_icon` — Recorded divergence — The port's scoreboard panel renders icons procedurally and has no gfx/scoreboard/player_handicap texture path, so the handicap marker is drawn as a colored square with the EXACT Base tint ('1 0 0' + '0 1 1' * ((16-lvl)/15)) rather than the bitmap. Same class of presentation divergence as the panel's other extra-icon markers. Marker is a colored square, not the gfx/scoreboard/player_handicap bitmap (the panel has no gfx texture path). The tint, level dependence, and draw condition are exact. Constant icon (gfx/scoreboard/player_handicap / colored 32x32-ish square marker (panel has no gfx texture path))
- `sv-handicap.dynamic.mutator` — PlayerDies fires an EXTRA recompute beyond Base's four hooks (Base covers a kill's frag delta via AddedPlayerScore(SP_SCORE) instead). Harmless — recomputes the same idempotent result a hair earlier; not observable.
- `sv-impulse.dispatch.commands_gate` — Minigame impulses are routed in the QC order (Minigames.Impulse at Commands.cs:2074 precedes the Timeout.IsPaused gate at 2075), but MinigameSessionManager.Impulse has no impulse->discrete-token table — for the P0 games it just re-runs the player's pending Move (returns Invalid otherwise). So a player in a session has the impulse CONSUMED but no meaningful move is produced from a raw weapon-key impulse. The per-game token mapping QC's minigame_event('cmd',...) performs is not modeled. (Detail belongs to a minigames unit; recorded here because the dispatch order/gate now matches.)
- `sv-impulse.weapon.group_cycle` — g_weaponswitch_debug==1 multi-slot loop not modeled (port drives slot 0 only); dead-player impulse latch (re-fire on respawn via this.impulse=imp) not modeled (impulse silently ignored while dead — WeaponImpulses.Handle gates the group branch on !dead).
- `sv-impulse.weapon.priority_cycle` — Constant priority_group_list (cvar_cl_weaponpriorities[number] = cl_weaponpriority0..9 (per-group, PER-CLIENT) / WeaponPriorityForGroup -> server-global cl_weaponpriority<N> cvar facade (PriorityProviderForGroup is NULL, so the per-client replicated value is NOT consulted); main cl_weaponpriority fallback if unset)
- `sv-impulse.use.usekey` — Nexball voluntary drop via the PlayerUseKey hook is not yet a subscriber (the hook is fired and CTF/Keepaway/TeamKeepaway/KeyHunt/Buffs/Powerups subscribe; Nexball's use-key drop, if any, is not wired here). CTF pass-request and Onslaught click-radar both need the live roster/control-point context which DispatchImpulse passes explicitly (Ctf.HandleUseKey / Onslaught.HandleUseKey) — the generic PlayerUseKeyArgs chain carries only the player, so those two are driven outside the hook for the impulse path.
- `sv-impulse.waypoints.sprites` — WarpZone redirection in the crosshair trace is simplified: WaypointResolveLocation does a plain server trace from the eyes along view angles (QC WarpZone_crosshair_trace walks through warpzones). Acceptable simplification — warpzone awareness would need the warpzone spatial index the port doesn't expose from the impulse path.
- `sv-impulse.cheat.impulses` — Teleport's random-location fallback uses BallEntity.RandomMapLocation (spawnpoint-sampling) instead of QC's engine world-bounds scatter (MoveToRandomMapLocation), and the info_autoscreenshot path is faithful (nearest point consumed). Minor: the random fallback flips only the pitch (matching the QC angles_x = -angles.x), but samples from spawnpoints rather than free map points.
- `sv-impulse.cheat.command_impulse` — Constant clone_moving_impulse (140 (CopyBody + v_forward*300 nudge) / not routed) Constant clone_standing_impulse (142 (CopyBody static) / not routed)
- `sv-intermission.client.view_freeze` — Recorded divergence — The QC -2342 RES_HEALTH first-intermission-phase sentinel is intentionally NOT stamped server-side (GameWorld.cs NextLevel comment ~3827): DarkPlaces' SVC_INTERMISSION suppresses the whole gameplay HUD so the value is never shown, but the port has no intermission HUD suppression, so the listen-server HealthArmor panel (bound directly to Player.Health) would render a literal '-2342'. The observable view-freeze effects the sentinel exists to enable (mouse-look lock + viewmodel hide + death-cam suppression) are instead driven client-side off the networked MatchIntermission flag, so the value dimension is na rather than a defect. Constant intermission-phase health sentinel (-2342 / intentionally not stamped server-side)
- `sv-intermission.client.autoscreenshot` — The capture filename uses a wall-clock timestamp instead of Base's networked `matchid` (not networked in the port), so the screenshots/autoscreenshot/<map>-<id>.jpg disambiguator differs cosmetically. The actual capture and opt-in gating are faithful.
- `sv-intermission.audio.cdtrack` — Listen-server only for the cdtrack pick: sv_intermission_cdtrack is read directly from the in-process server cvar (NetGame.cs:2486), which isn't networked, so a pure remote client always sees the empty/kill path. With the stock empty default no switch occurs anyway, so this is invisible on stock config; it would only matter on a server that sets the cvar.
- `sv-ipban.enforce.on_connect` — Base uses Ban_MaybeEnforceBanOnce (a one-shot .ban_checked flag) to avoid re-checking the same client repeatedly; the port omits the flag and instead calls MaybeEnforceBan on every remote client command. This is a strict SUPERSET of Base's behavior (the once-flag is purely an optimization), not a behavioral divergence — no functional gap.
- `sv-ipban.persist` — Load does not spawn the bansyncer think entity (Base Ban_LoadBans creates one); moot here because online sync is absent (intended divergence — see sv-ipban.sync.online).
- `sv-ipban.view_delete` — Ban_Delete's OnlineBanList_SendUnban side-effect is dropped (no online sync); slot deletion itself is faithful.
- `sv-ipban.sync.online` — Recorded divergence — The Bans class is the Godot-free essence of ipban.qc and deliberately excludes the HTTP uri_get cross-server propagation (see the Bans class summary doc-comment). The local store + console command surface is complete; the online provider sync (g_ban_sync_*, the bansyncer think entity, the ban/unban/list URI protocol) is out of scope for the port core. Defaults to disabled in Base anyway (g_ban_sync_uri empty). No cross-server ban propagation: SendBan/SendUnban on insert/delete, the periodic list query, and the trusted-servers verify filter are all absent. The ~-prefixed unauthenticated-banner guard does not exist. Constant g_ban_sync_uri ("" / absent) Constant g_ban_sync_interval (5 (min) / absent) Constant g_ban_sync_timeout (45 (s) / absent) Constant g_ban_sync_trusted_servers ("" / absent) Constant g_ban_sync_trusted_servers_verify (0 / absent) Constant MAX_IPBAN_URIS (16 / absent)
- `sv-mapvoting.lifecycle.think_loop` — Base's MapVote_Think also handles the rescan_pending / fs_rescan path before init; the port has no map-list rescan step (irrelevant to the rotation it builds).
- `sv-mapvoting.ballot.build` — Suggestion seeding is LIVE and carries the suggester name end-to-end: StartMapVoteOrRotation (GameWorld.cs:4193-4196) maps Commands.MapSuggestions (now (Map, Suggester) pairs) into the 4-arg Start, which seeds min(g_maplist_votable_suggestions, nmax) suggested maps first, deduped, then fills from the rotation (matching MapVote_AddVotableMaps' smax seed). The abstain slot append and per-candidate Rng tiebreak (AddVotable) are implemented; abstain is off by default (g_maplist_votable_abstain 0) so the slot isn't added in a stock match. The panel can display up to 30 entries. The authoritative ballot is capped at 20 including the abstain slot. The 'don't care' abstain text/screenshot-dir cells are folded into net.sync.
- `sv-mapvoting.override_chain` — Faithful priority order folded into one method (GameWorld.cs:3948-4014): campaign PostIntermission -> quit_when_empty -> quit_and_redirect -> QueuedNextMap -> samelevel -> lastlevel -> gametype vote -> map vote -> silent rotation. Covers more of DoNextMapOverride than the original baseline noted (quit_when_empty/quit_and_redirect/lastlevel all handled).
- `sv-mapvoting.vote_scoreboard_hide` — Recorded divergence — ADR-0005 assigns native networking to the port. NetGame.UpdateScoreboard directly suppresses the scoreboard while MapVote.Visible, including manual and death scoreboard requests. The legacy health sentinel, SVC_FINALE and impulse reset encode this UI state in DarkPlaces. The scoreboard-hide EFFECT is achieved a DIFFERENT way: UpdateScoreboard models Base's intermission==2 by hiding the scoreboard whenever the MapVote panel is Visible (mapVoteShowing → intermissionShow = MatchIntermission && !mapVoteShowing), so the player does not see the scoreboard during the vote (the visible outcome of Base's SVC_FINALE). Presentation goal met. But Base's actual MECHANISM is absent: it forces RES_HEALTH=2342 on every client (incl. bots) and sends SVC_FINALE "" once. The port models neither (logic/values/timing missing). The -2342 refs in GameWorld/NetGame are the separate INTERMISSION sentinel (FixIntermissionClient), explicitly NOT reproduced. Base also zeroes CS(it).impulse each tick after reading it; the port reads the impulse synchronously in DispatchImpulse (no stored per-tick impulse field), so this specific clear is moot. Constant vote_sentinel_health (2342 / none)
- `sv-mapvoting.cast.abstain` — Abstain() is faithful (clears any map choice, marks abstained, excluded from voters_real, still counts toward 'everyone voted'). Reachable through the live client input: selecting the abstain ballot slot routes the impulse through CastVote -> Abstain. Abstain is off by default (g_maplist_votable_abstain 0) so the slot isn't on a stock ballot, but the cast PATH is live.
- `sv-mapvoting.decide.tally_rank` — Ranking uses a stable descending sort by votes then per-candidate Rng (RankCandidates), faithfully mirroring MapVote_ranked_cmp + the mapvote_rng tiebreak assigned at build. Heapsort vs stable Sort is an implementation detail with identical ordering semantics. The running-gametype 0-vote tiebreak (current gametype index wins among 0-vote options) is modeled FOR the gametype vote via StartGametype giving the current type Rng=1f, but is not modeled for the map vote's current_gametype_index (which is -1 on a map vote, so the branch is inert in Base too).
- `sv-mapvoting.decide.early_finish` — Bit-faithful to Base's `(voters_real - votes_running_total) < votes_recent` unbeatable-leader inequality plus the `voters_real == 0` all-abstained branch. The port ADDS an explicit `allVoted` (ballots >= ExpectedVoters) finish branch that Base does not have as a separate clause; it is functionally subsumed by the unbeatable-leader check in practice and never finishes earlier wrongly, so it is a benign logic addition, not a defect.
- `sv-mapvoting.decide.reduce` — Reduce dimming IS presented on the listen server: NetGame feeds availability each frame, and the mv_top2_alpha reduce-FADE clock (_top2Time) is stamped — UpdateMapVotePanel calls SetAvailability BEFORE SetVotes, and SetAvailability stamps _top2Time on the first option that newly loses availability, so pruned options play the top-2 reveal fade rather than snapping off. FIXED: the :vote:reduce event-log line IS now emitted (MapVoting.cs:474-493 builds the kept|removed-separated line and calls EventLogEcho, wired at GameWorld.cs:1221 gated on sv_eventlog). This was a baseline gap.
- `sv-mapvoting.finish.winner` — FIXED: the :vote:finished event-log line (with per-option tallies + 'didn't vote' count) AND the :vote:suggestion_accepted line for a suggested winner are now emitted (MapVoting.cs:578-599 via EventLogEcho, wired GameWorld.cs:1221). These were baseline gaps. FixClientCvars-on-finish is not modeled (Base FOREACH_CLIENT FixClientCvars at mapvoting.qc:542-544). The gametype-switch-on-finish branch is handled in the dedicated gametype_vote row (ApplyGametypeSwitch); not duplicated here.
- `sv-mapvoting.suggestions` — CmdSuggestMap (Commands.cs:3174-3224) is faithful to MapVote_Suggest: empty-arg usage, the suggestions==0 disable, the map-existence check, the recency gate (Map_IsRecent unless override_mostrecent), the gametype-support gate (unless sv_vote_gametype), the already-suggested dedup, AND it records the suggester's netname. FIXED: the :vote:suggested: event-log line IS now emitted (Commands.cs:3220-3221 GameLog.Echo gated on sv_eventlog). This was a baseline gap. Suggested maps reach the ballot with their suggester: StartMapVoteOrRotation (GameWorld.cs:4193-4196) passes the (Map, Suggester) pairs into MapVote.Start, which seeds them ahead of the rotation (capped by g_maplist_votable_suggestions). show_suggester (default 1) and the panel's 'Suggested by:' line can populate. Minor: the random replacement of a suggestion when the list is full (mapvote_suggestion_ptr >= MAPVOTE_COUNT) is not modeled (the port keeps an unbounded list and caps at seed time).
- `sv-mapvoting.net.sync` — Recorded divergence — The native protocol sends a reliable MapVote control message to remote clients. Listen hosts read GameWorld.MapVote directly. No screenshot transfer (TE_CSQC_PICTURE / mv_getpicture); the panel resolves level shots from the client's content VFS.
- `sv-matrix.chat.bridge` — Recorded divergence — Native dedicated only. Uses bearer headers, JSON serialization and TLS outside loopback. Refuses redirects, bypasses proxies for loopback homeservers, scopes credentials to the configured server and world, runs one receive loop, and retries failed polls after one second with the current cursor.
- `sv-spawnpoints.select.spawn_score` — shortest is seeded with a fixed 1e6 instead of the world diagonal; benign because the prio threshold (>100) and the weight upper-clamp (5000) both swamp it, so the pick is statistically identical. UNCHANGED. Constant shortest seed (vlen(world.maxs - world.mins) (world diagonal) / 1_000_000 (fixed))
- `sv-spawnpoints.select.teamcheck_ladder` — Recorded divergence — DetectTeamSpawns recomputes the team-spawn globals from the live spots on each SelectSpawnPoint call rather than once at link time (Base relocate_spawnpoint); idempotent and O(spots), and it avoids a stale forteams mask across map/gametype changes. Same result. ComputeTeamCheck also auto-derives the team request from a team player when the host never called RequestTeamSpawns explicitly (a tolerant fallback Base doesn't need).
- `sv-spawnpoints.select.spawn_evalfunc` — The port splits Base's single vector-returning spawn_evalfunc into TWO delegates: SpotEvalReject (bool veto, used by assault) + SpotEvalScore (additive prio delta, used by race). Both real Base users are covered (assault only vetoes; race only adds +50 to prio.x), but an evalfunc that rewrites the WEIGHT component or returns a fully arbitrary score vector isn't representable. No real Base user does this — minor. STILL OPEN.
- `sv-spawnpoints.relocate.move_out_of_solid` — Recorded divergence — Base relocates an in-solid spot OUT of solid once at link time via the generic omni-directional move_out_of_solid helper, then keeps it permanently. The port has no link-time relocation pass, so it does the equivalent at SELECT time (ScoreSpot, when the placement-box trace reports startsolid): RelocateSpawnOutOfSolid steps the spot's origin straight UP up to the player-box height + 16qu margin and commits the new origin onto the spot entity via SetOrigin, so the nudge persists across selections (GatherSpawnPoints re-reads the same live spot). The straight-up search (vs Base's omni-directional) is a deliberate simplification covering the common rest-in-floor case; the gate cvar, keep-on-success, and drop-on-failure semantics all match. Direction of the nudge differs from Base: the port searches only straight up, whereas Base's move_out_of_solid is omni-directional. A spot embedded in a wall/ceiling that Base would push sideways out, the port will fail to clear (and drop the spot) if a clear position isn't found by rising the box height. Low severity (the dominant embed case is a spot resting in/just below the floor). Intended divergence. UNCHANGED. Constant relocation search direction (move_out_of_solid (omni-directional generic helper) / straight-up step (rest-in-floor approximation))
- `sv-spawnpoints.fx.spawnpoint_idle_glow` — Recorded divergence — Upstream emits per render-frame via boxparticles with pcount = bound(0, frametime, 0.1); the port uses a 0.2s pulse timer passing count = PulseInterval through the EffectSystem accumulator (same per-point average rate — 37.5 count => ~37.5 particles/sec; fixed 2026-07-03, count 1 per pulse was 5x). The distance cull reads cl_spawn_point_dist_max (registered 1200; DrawDistance is a dead legacy fallback). The discovery is via the entity table (FindByClass) rather than per-spawnpoint ENT_CLIENT networking (static edicts) — both are the deliberate static-spawnpoint divergences.
- `sv-spawnpoints.net.spawnpoint_link_send` — Recorded divergence — The port discovers spawnpoints directly from the entity table (FindByClass) on both server (SpawnSystem) and client (SpawnPointParticles) rather than networking a per-spawnpoint ENT_CLIENT_SPAWNPOINT entity. Static map edicts don't move, so the SpawnPoint_Send / spawnpoint_think (0.1s move-resend) / link_spawnpoint visibility-gating machinery has no gameplay effect. The spawnpoint_use team-retag, spawnpoint_setactive toggle (Onslaught), and spawnpoint_reset ARE now ported and wired: GatherSpawnPoints lazily installs the .Use + .SetActive handlers on every spawnpoint (idempotent, never clobbers a map-wired handler), and they're reached by the generic SUB_UseTargets / relay_(de)activate dispatch — so a map targeting a spawnpoint by targetname drives the team-retag + active-toggle + some_spawn_has_been_used latch. SpawnActive is honored by ScoreSpot's ACTIVE_ACTIVE gate. FIXED vs prior baseline: spawnpoint_use (teamplay team-retag) and spawnpoint_setactive now HAVE live writers (SpawnSystem.cs:652-680), reachable via MapMover.UseTargets (MapObjectsCommon.cs:468) and the relay SetActive path (LogicGates.cs:417-418). SomeSpawnHasBeenUsed is set true by both handlers; the GameWorld.cs:3857 useallspawns count gate is now satisfiable. CAVEAT: no PORTED gametype (Onslaught/Assault) drives a control-point capture chain onto spawnpoints — the machinery is reachable on generic map-object targeting but not exercised by a gametype control-point — so it's live-but-not-gametype-driven. Confidence medium for that reason. spawnpoint_reset (active-restore on round reset) has no dedicated reset2 caller (the port lacks the QC reset infra for spawnpoints); SpawnActive is restored only by an explicit SetActive(ACTIVE_ACTIVE). Minor. STILL OPEN. SelectSpawnPoint does NOT explicitly model Base's 'if(some_spawn_has_been_used) return NULL' lockout branch — it returns null whenever zero spots survive (any cause) and the caller retries, which subsumes but doesn't distinguish the lockout. STILL OPEN. Constant spawnpoint_think interval (0.1 s (resend on move) / none) Constant link nextthink jitter (time + 0.5 + random()*2 / none)
- `sv-teamplay.join.find_best_team` — Gametype-level callers (CTF/CA/FreezeTag/Dom/KeyHunt/TDM/TeamMayhem via TeamBalance.JoinSmallestTeam) apply count only, with no score/skill tiebreak and no randomization — only the connect/moveplayer/team/selectteam AssignBestTeam path runs the full Base ladder. Base uses m_num_players_net (humans, deducts leavable bots); port approximates this as count - min(bots,1) per team rather than distributing bots_would_leave across teams (MeasureTeam joinerHuman branch).
- `sv-teamplay.join.skill_weighting` — Constant skill mu/var model (TrueSkill m_skill_mu / m_skill_var / bots = BotSkill, humans = flat unranked (server-avg); var = flat 1)
- `sv-teamplay.balance.autobalance_bots` — Constant tiebreak score (SP_SCORE / frag score (ScoreFrags)) Constant trigger (on every human SetPlayerTeam (unconditional) / 3s timer (now UNCONDITIONAL — no cvar gate) + synchronous on human team-change)
- `sv-teamplay.balance.prevent_imbalance` — Base's queue-aware clause (!g_balance_teams_queue || caller.team > 0) is absent since the join queue isn't ported; with no queue this is a no-op so behavior matches for stock servers. Admin moveplayer/shuffleteams intentionally bypass the gate (matches Base — Base's gate is only in ClientCommand_selectteam, not the admin move path).
- `sv-teamplay.forced.determine_team` — PlayerInList matches the client's PersistentId (crypto id) or NetAddress (IP) against the space-separated cvar list; Base PlayerInList (client.qc:1047) matches against crypto-id and IP the same way, so this is faithful — but it depends on PersistentId/NetAddress being populated for real clients (verify the host fills these on connect).
- `sv-teamplay.remove.excess_players` — Like Base, only handles the 2-team case (AVAILABLE_TEAMS != 2 early-out) and skips campaign — matches the QC FIXME-noted limitation (Base also doesn't avoid kicking the flag/ball/key carrier; the port shares that gap, but it's a Base limitation not a port divergence).
- `sv-teamplay.bot_vs_human.team_split` — Side selection is faithful only for the standard 2-team (red/blue) match: AllowedTeams keys off Teams.Active order (index0=first active, index1=last) rather than Base's NUM_TEAM_1..4 absolute index ladder; for a non-red/blue 2-team config the chosen side could differ. For >2 teams Base itself only TODO-handles bots (no partition), matching the port's all-allowed fallback. Count sizing (TargetBotCount = |ratio| * active humans) and the team partition live on different paths (BotPopulation count vs AssignBestTeam ban).
- `sv-teamplay.change.kill_on_teamchange` — No LogTeamChange event-log emission (:team:) and no INFO_JOIN_PLAY_TEAM join notification on a team change.
- `sv-teamplay.color.set_player_colors` — The teamplay path sets Player.ClientColors but the visible player-model re-color from clientcolors is a separate presentation concern (handled in the model/render layer); this row covers only the server-side authority encoding, which is now faithful.
- `sv-teamplay.change.team_change_hooks` — No port mutator yet SUBSCRIBES to PlayerChangeTeam / PlayerChangedTeam — the hook chain fires on every team set but currently has zero consumers (only Teamplay.cs references the hooks). The mechanism is faithful and reachable; a future mode that needs to veto/react has a seam, but no live behavior depends on it yet.
- `sv-teamplay.bot.bot_forced_team` — Bot-forced pin is suppressed under bot_vs_human on a 2-team match (BotPopulation.cs:349) so it doesn't fight the human/bot partition; Base lets bot_forced_team early-out before CheckAllowedTeams, so an edge config combining both could differ. Niche.
- `sv-world-rules.boot.worldspawn` — No ServerProgsDB load and no full support-entity spawn pass at boot (RandomSeed/PingPLReport/lightstyles covered as separate rows; DB persistence covered under misc.shutdown).
- `sv-world-rules.warmup.ready_count` — No GiveWarmupResources re-give on countdown abort (loadout re-give deferred to SpawnSystem); the abort itself fires, but the per-player warmup loadout is not re-given when reset_map already ran at countdown start.
- `sv-world-rules.warmup.ready_restart` — Per-player restart policy still not ported: no sv_spectate==2 demotion, no re-join of queued (wants_join) players, no per-player allowed_timeouts reset, no alivetime_start clear in ReadyRestart_force.
- `sv-world-rules.entity.randomseed` — Recorded divergence — The seed VALUE is rolled from a process RNG, not Base's engine random() stream, so it is not bit-exact with Base. This is harmless and deliberate: no client EFFECT currently consumes the decoded seed (the client RandomRng PrandomContext has no downstream reader — grep returns none), so only the seam (re-roll cadence + network round-trip + per-context reseed) needs to agree, and it does. The mechanism also folds the seed onto the existing SendMatchState packet rather than a dedicated ENT_CLIENT_RANDOMSEED networked entity (smallest diff that reaches every accepted peer; appended trailing so an old client truncates harmlessly). No client effect consumes the decoded shared seed yet: ClientNet.RandomRng (PrandomContext, reseeded on change at ClientNet.cs:617) has no downstream reader, so the seed produces no observable deterministic spread/effect — the seam exists but is presentationally inert until a consumer is wired. Seed value is not bit-exact with Base's engine random() stream (intended; see divergence_rationale). Constant seed_value_bit_exact (engine random() stream / process System.Random (NOT bit-exact; no client effect consumes it yet))
- `sv-world-rules.entity.pingplreport` — Constant LATENCY_THINKRATE (10 seconds for the playerstats latency record / PlayerStats.PingProvider has no live producer) Constant report_period_s (3 / maxclients / ENet stats sampled every 0.25 seconds and carried in scoreboard deltas)
- `sv-world-rules.misc.start_delay` — The dedicated-server 15s default comes from an `if_dedicated` clause in xonotic-common.cfg that the port's config layer does not replicate — an explicit `set g_start_delay 15` works, but the stock dedicated 15s join window is not auto-applied. Config-layer divergence only, not a code gap.
- `sv-world-rules.misc.redirection` — Constant quit_and_redirect_timer (defer-reconnect delay (autocvar_quit_and_redirect_timer) / none — port reconnects immediately, no defer timer)
- `sv-world-rules.eventlog.dumpstats` — The dedicated per-call score-LOG-FILE open/append (Base appends each block to sv_logscores_filename when sv_logscores_file is on) is not reproduced — the port routes the same blocks to the structured eventlog-file sink (sv_eventlog_files) instead, so a standalone scores.log is not written. Constant sv_logscores_file (console/file score-line sinks / registered (Cvars.cs:424) but per-call file open/append omitted (eventlog-file sink covers it))
- `sv-world-rules.boot.initialize_entity_queue` — Recorded divergence — The port does NOT reproduce Base's generic order-sorted linked list (InitializeEntity(e, func, order) sorted by initialize_entity_order). Instead it reproduces the same BEHAVIORAL ordering with a fixed-sequence drain: MapObjectsRegistry.RunPostSpawn() (called once at Boot after the whole BSP entity lump has spawned) runs the door-link pass FIRST (INITPRIO_LINKDOORS) then the INITPRIO_FINDTARGET pass (DynamicLight/ViewLocation/Follow/AdvancedMovers/Teleporters/InfoAutoScreenshot deferred-init queues), each family draining its own pending queue. The few entities that need it carry their own deferred-resolve list; there is no single generic priority queue. Only the families that actually need post-spawn ordering carry a deferred-init queue (doors, dynlights, view-locations, follow, advanced movers, teleporters, info_autoscreenshot). The general INITPRIO mechanism (e.g. INITPRIO_GAMETYPE_FALLBACK GameplayMode_DelayedInit, INITPRIO_DROPTOFLOOR DropToFloor, the weaponarena_available_*_update FINDTARGET passes) is not driven by a unified queue; whether every Base INITPRIO consumer has a port analogue is not exhaustively traced. The reset_map .reset2 second-pass (already tracked under warmup.reset_map) is handled by a separate two-stage Reset/Reset2 invoke, not this queue.
- `tdm.identity.registration` — tdm.qh legacydefaults "50 20 2 0" (menu create-match preset) is still not modeled, but that is menu metadata; the in-match defaults (timelimit=15, pointlimit=50, teams=2, leadlimit=0) are now all seeded correctly at boot. Constant legacydefaults (50 20 2 0 / not modeled (menu))
- `tdm.init.gamerules` — Engine-side teamplay flag (SERVERFLAG_TEAMPLAY / cvar_set teamplay 2) is handled host-side via TeamGame rather than an explicit GameRules_teams call; behavior matches. Limit seeding (GameRules_limit_score/lead) is now mirrored faithfully by Tdm.OnInit -> SeedLimit, which handles the -1 'use mapinfo default' sentinel exactly as sv_rules.qc does (skip cvar_set when limit<0, seed the gametype default).
- `tdm.team_balance.join` — Tdm.AssignTeam (and the TeamBalance.JoinSmallestTeam helper it wraps, Tdm.cs:43/264) is a DEAD duplicate with no live caller: the join path uses the richer Server-side Teamplay.AssignBestTeam (smallest team, score tiebreak, skill weighting, bot autobalance). No player-visible gap, but the redundant copy could drift. Unchanged by Wave-2.
- `tka.ball.pickup` — Constant carried_scale (12/16 = 0.75 / none (entity scale not set while carried))
- `tka.score.teamkill` — OnDeath is on the LIVE Combat.Death bus and credits the attacker's TEAM (AddTeamScore -> GS.AddToTeam slot 0). It adds killac when the attacker personally carries OR (g_tka_score_team && the team holds the ball) — matching Base PlayerDies. The Base DIFF_TEAM(attacker,target) team-kill exclusion is enforced (scoringKill = attacker!=victim && !SameTeam), so team-kills don't score. Correct cvar family: reads g_tka_score_killac (fallback 1 = Base default).
- `tka.score.scoreteam` — The DISTINGUISHING TKA rule is modeled: TeamHasBall(team) checks whether the single ball's carrier is on the attacker's team, and when ScoreTeam (g_tka_score_team, default 1) is set ANY teammate's cross-team kill scores killac for the team (OnDeath: attackerCarries || teamHasBall). The no-ball-kill warning suppression is modeled: the noncarrier warn (CENTER_KEEPAWAY_WARN) fires only when !attackerCarries && !teamHasBall && g_tka_noncarrier_warn. Single-ball simplification: TeamHasBall is BallTeam==team rather than IL_EACH(g_tkaballs, ...); correct for the one-ball case (the only case the port spawns).
- `tka.score.bckill` — OnDeath awards the bckill team bonus whenever the victim was the carrier (victimWasCarrier branch), INDEPENDENT of whether the attacker carries — matching Base. It also credits TKA_CARRIERKILLS to the killer. Reachable via the live ball/pickup path. Correct cvar: g_tka_score_bckill, fallback 1 (= Base default).
- `tka.score.bctime` — The TKA_BCTIME column is DECLARED in Activate (sorts as secondary) and Tick() accrues frametime to TKA_BCTIME (banks whole seconds, carries the fractional remainder, resets the remainder on each new pickup in GiveBall). Tick has a LIVE caller: GameWorld.cs:2583 case TeamKeepaway tka: tka.Tick(Simulation.FrameTime). So with a carrier, TKA_BCTIME accrues each frame.
- `tka.score.timepoints` — Tick() accrues g_tka_score_timepoints*dt to the carrier's TEAM score with a fractional remainder (QC float2int_decimal_fld analogue), and Tick is CALLED every frame on the live path (GameWorld.cs:2583). Default is 0 (off), so still no impact on stock servers, but the feature is connected. Residual: Scores_CountFragsRemaining suppression tied to timepoints (announce remaining frags only when timepoints off) is still unported.
- `tka.score.nofrags` — TKA subscribes MutatorHooks.GiveFragsForKill (Activate) and zeroes the per-kill frag delta (returns true), and the hook is FIRED on the live scoring path in Scores.cs (the ENEMY-FRAG branch reads the adjusted FragScore back). So DM frags are suppressed in TKA, matching Base. reset_map_global (clear float2int_decimal_fld per client) has no port analogue; moot while timepoints is off by default (and the remainder is a class field reset in Activate).
- `tka.damage.matrix` — TeamKeepaway subscribes MutatorHooks.DamageCalculate (Activate), and that hook is fired by DamageSystem.cs:263 on every player-vs-player hit, so the possession damage+force matrix affects combat. Both damage AND force are scaled: OnDamageCalculate applies DamageScale to args.Damage and DamageForceScale to args.Force. Correct cvar family + form: reads the VECTOR cvars g_tka_ballcarrier/noncarrier_damage/force and indexes the x/y/z component (self / other-carrier / noncarrier) chosen by whether the ATTACKER carries — matching Base. Default 1 1 1, so no scaling on stock servers, but it is connected.
- `tka.carrier.highspeed` — FIXED (missing -> faithful + live): OnPlayerPhysics multiplies the carrier's SpeedMultiplier by g_tka_ballcarrier_highspeed (QC STAT(MOVEVARS_HIGHSPEED, player) *= ...). Subscribed in Activate to MutatorHooks.PlayerPhysics and fired by the live physics step PlayerPhysics.cs:252, the same channel Keepaway/Nexball/Buffs/Dodging use. The pure multiply composes with powerup/buff factors (SpeedMultiplier reset to 1 each frame). Default 1 (no effect by default); the feature is now wired even though it is a no-op on stock servers.
- `tka.win.pointlimit` — CheckPointLimit is LIVE (reached via tka.Tick at GameWorld.cs:2583, which ends with CheckPointLimit()) and latches WinningTeam at the point limit via the ST_SCORE team leader; reported at GameWorld.cs:3201-2821 on MatchEnded. With the working scoring path it can trigger in play. Lead limit (g_tka_point_leadlimit / GameRules_limit_lead) is modeled: LeadLimit getter + the leader-vs-second check in CheckPointLimit. Port PointLimit fallback is literal 50; Base g_tka_point_limit default is -1 (use mapinfo 50). Resolution order (g_tka_point_limit -> fraglimit -> 50) is reasonable.
- `tka.team.framing` — Team-count clamp (2..4), the override logic (g_tka_teams_override>=2 else g_tka_teams), team-spawn gating (g_tka_team_spawns), and tie->overtime (TeamTie.TopTwoTied on ST_SCORE) are faithfully modeled. Reachable via GameWorld.cs:2096 tka.Activate().
- `tka.hud.modicon` — FIXED (missing -> faithful + live): the full server->net->client mod-icon chain is now wired. Server: BallStatusFor(viewer) bit-packs carrying/dropped/per-team-taken (QC PlayerPreThink), recipient-relative for the carrying bit; written per peer at GametypeStatusBlock.cs:120-123 (Kind.TeamKeepaway, one byte). Client: ClientNet decodes -> NetGame.cs:3772 feeds panel.TkaBallStatus + sets ModIconsMode.TeamKeepaway -> ModIconsPanel.DrawTeamKeepaway draws keepawayball_carrying / tka_taken_<color> with blink(0.85,0.15,5) + the status-change expand transition (QC HUD_Mod_TeamKeepaway). SpectateCopy mirroring is implicitly handled: BallStatusFor is computed per recipient on the server, so a spectator's panel reflects their view-entity's status through the same per-peer write (no separate copy needed). Minor presentation: QC uses a separate drawpic_aspect_skin_expanding for the prevstatus expand layer; the port folds the expand factor f into the single icon's alpha. Visual is equivalent; the distinct expanding overlay sprite is not drawn separately.
- `tka.bot.role` — Logic + values now faithful + live. FindBall (BotObjectiveRoles.cs:387) queries 'keepawayball', matching the live ball (BallEntity.cs:105). RoleKeepaway is dispatched for 'tka' (BotRoles.cs:99) and the carry link reads ball.GtCarrier (set by AttachToCarrier in the live GiveBall path). The TKA team-aware ball goalrating split is implemented (BotObjectiveRoles.cs:367-370): a loose ball or an ENEMY carrier rates 8000, a SAME-TEAM carrier rates 4000, gated on brain.GameType.TeamGame; matches havocbot_goalrating_tkaball(8000,4000) distance 2000. FIXED this pass (values partial -> faithful): the carrier/collector enemy-priority numbers are now Base-exact — the carrier branch rates enemyplayers 10000 (BotObjectiveRoles.cs:353) and the collector branch rates enemyplayers 500 (BotObjectiveRoles.cs:360), matching havocbot_role_tka_carrier(10000) / havocbot_role_tka_collector(500). Items 10000/10000 also match. The roles are still selected by the carryingBall branch each evaluation rather than as two explicit havocbot_role transitions, but the rating numbers each branch emits are now identical to Base. Bot_ForbidAttack is ported (TeamKeepaway.ForbidBotAttack) with Base-exact logic and wired LIVE — GameWorld.cs:2097-1837 installs BotBrain.ForbidAttackHook in the TKA Activate case, consumed at BotBrain.cs:797 during target eligibility.
- `tka.score.preferplayerscore` — FIXED (logic missing -> faithful, liveness dead -> live): the rejoin-clear veto is now wired AND on a live path. (1) TKA subscribes the hook in Activate (TeamKeepaway.cs:404-405 MutatorHooks.PreferPlayerScore_Clear.Add(OnPreferPlayerScoreClear)); OnPreferPlayerScoreClear returns true (TeamKeepaway.cs:423-426), matching QC MUTATOR_HOOKFUNCTION(tka, PreferPlayerScore_Clear){ return true; }. (2) The clear path is consumed live: ClientManager.Join (ClientManager.cs:294) calls GameScores.ClearPlayerOnJoin(p) on every (re)join. The veto is now consulted via the GLOBAL hook chain rather than a per-caller delegate: ClearPlayerOnJoin (GameScores.cs:636-637) ORs in MutatorHooks.FirePreferPlayerScore_Clear(p) (MutatorHooks.cs:580-583 -> PreferPlayerScore_Clear.Call), so any subscribing gametype (TKA) vetoes the -1 wipe. The prior baseline's two blockers (TKA never supplies the delegate; ClearPlayerOnJoin has no live caller) are both resolved. No-op on stock servers: the default g_score_resetonjoin is 0, so ClearPlayerOnJoin short-circuits (mode==0) BEFORE the veto matters. Faithful to Base (which also no-ops at 0). The veto only changes behavior under the admin g_score_resetonjoin -1 config, where TKA now correctly KEEPS the rejoiner's score.
- `tmayhem.score.damage_accrual` — DEATH_CAMP (campcheck) is omitted from the environmental-suicide subtraction set (MayhemScoring.IsEnvironmentalSuicide, Mayhem.cs:243-249), so a campcheck suicide would not subtract score. RECLASSIFIED 2026-06-25: the DeathTypes.Camp tag NOW EXISTS in the port (Mayhem.cs:109) — the gap is now simply 'camp not added to the set' rather than the baseline's 'port models no campcheck death'. Still non-default (no campcheck death is currently produced) and low impact; the in-code comment at Mayhem.cs:238-239/225-226 ("port has no camp deathtype yet") is now stale. Constant env_suicide_set (KILL,DROWN,HURTTRIGGER,CAMP,LAVA,SLIME,SWAMP / Kill,Drown,Void,Lava,Slime,Swamp (CAMP omitted from the set))
- `tmayhem.score.per_kill` — Recorded divergence — Port routes the per-kill recompute through the Combat.Death bus (OnDeath) and suppresses the +1 frag by running Scores in non-owning mode (OwnsScore=false) rather than subscribing to a GiveFragsForKill hook. Observable result (no ±1 frag; score == the recompute) matches Base; the kills/teamkills/suicides aux columns are still maintained by the obituary so the recompute reads them.
- `tmayhem.combat.regen_rot` — Recorded divergence — The port's PlayerRegen hook only models the boolean 'disable regen' return, not QC's separate scaling of the regen arg vs the rot arg (M_ARGV 2/3). With the stock defaults (both off) the two collapse to the same observable result (regen+rot fully disabled), so this is faithful in the default config; it would diverge only if one of regenerate/rot were enabled independently. If g_tmayhem_regenerate XOR g_tmayhem_rot were set (non-default), the port cannot independently zero one arg — it returns a single disable bool. Non-default config only. Unchanged from baseline.
- `tmayhem.loadout.weapon_arena` — QC's 'most_available' intersects weapons_most with the weapons actually present on the map (weaponarena_available_most_update via weaponsInMapAll); the port has no map-weapon set, so ExpandWeaponArena falls back to the full weapons_most — which is exactly what QC itself does when the map has no weapons, but on a weapon-bearing map the port would grant a SUPERSET of QC's 'most_available' arena. Documented divergence, low impact (tmayhem maps have pickup_items off / weapons removed, so the map-weapon set is typically empty anyway).
- `tmayhem.items.filter` — Item-class detection is by hardcoded classname string lists (item_strength/_shield/_invincible powerups; item_* ammo; weapon_* pickups) instead of the QC itemdef.instanceOfPowerup/Ammo/WeaponPickup flags — a custom/modded item could be misclassified. Unchanged from baseline.
- `turret-ewheel.identity.def` — Constant head_model (ewheel-gun1.md3 / models/turrets/ewheel-gun1.md3 (carried as identity data; NOT instantiated as a head bone entity / not rendered)) Constant spawnflags (TUR_FLAG_PLAYER|TUR_FLAG_MOVE|TUR_FLAG_ROAM / movable=true (MOVE); PLAYER select implicit; ROAM not modeled)
- `turret-ewheel.setup.tr_setup` — Initial frame=1 / tur_head.frame=1 not set (no frame handling — covered by turret-ewheel.anim.driveframes). ammo_flags ENERGY|RECHARGE|RECIEVE modeled only as an ammo pool (no energy/recharge/receive bookkeeping); the recharge rate itself is honored. Constant ammo_flags (ENERGY|RECHARGE|RECIEVE / not modeled (ammo pool only)) Constant frame/tur_head.frame (1 / none)
- `turret-ewheel.client.draw` — Constant client movetype roll (MOVETYPE_BOUNCE + gravity 1, origin += velocity*dt / none (client-render, deferred))
- `turret-flac.fire.flak_shell` — Constant missile_flags (MIF_SPLASH | MIF_PROXY / n/a (no missile_flags field; behaviour folded into the fuse think))
- `turret-framework.registry.descriptor` — Constant per-unit cvar fields (28 (TR_PROPS_COMMON via g_turrets_unit_<name>_<field>) / C# const per turret class (no per-unit cvar table))
- `turret-framework.spawn.init` — Constant tur_head child + tag_head attach (yes (new turret_head) / none (no head entity)) Constant turret_initparams clamps (yes (bound/derive every numeric) / none (raw consts))
- `turret-framework.spawn.liveness` — No Net_LinkEntity / turret_send registration (networking) and no turret_manager auto-spawn.
- `turret-framework.brain.think` — HITALLVALID / CUSTOM / VOLLYALWAYS shoot-mode branches are folded into per-turret Think rather than the shared driver (functionally covered by Tesla/Phaser/FusionReactor self-scan + the RunCombat VollyAlways path, but the generic dispatch is not in RunCombat).
- `turret-framework.acquire.target` — No separate turret_targetscore_support path (support units score like combat units). Constant support score func (turret_targetscore_support / no separate support score func)
- `turret-framework.track.head` — Recorded divergence — Port silently corrects a QC bug: the STEPMOTOR pitch clamp in turret_track (sv_turrets.qc:574-575) sets tur_head.angles.x to +aim_maxpitch even on the under-limit (< -aim_maxpitch) branch, snapping the head to max-up instead of max-down. The port uses a symmetric Bound, so a stepmotor turret at the lower pitch limit holds -aim_maxpitch. Behaviourally affects only the rare stepmotor turret driven hard past its lower pitch limit. Constant 10th-frame TNSF_ANG force (yes / n/a (no networking)) Constant STEPMOTOR negative-pitch clamp (BUGGED: sv_turrets.qc:574-575 sets angles.x=+aim_maxpitch on the < -aim_maxpitch branch / symmetric Bound(-aimMaxPitch,..,aimMaxPitch) (TurretAI.cs:544))
- `turret-framework.fire.gate` — Per-shot firecheck LOS + TEAMCHECK branches not separately re-tested at fire time — but Base itself marks TFL_FIRECHECK_LOS/TEAMCECK as un-implemented in turret_firecheck (the '-' rows in the sv_turrets.qc:856-868 legend), so the port matches Base behaviour: acquisition-time LOS/team gating is the only LOS/team test in both.
- `turret-framework.net.sync` — Constant TNSF_STATUS (active/dead) (team + scaled-health byte / TurFlags byte: bit0 .active, bit1 DEAD_DEAD (NEW this wave); objective hp/255 + state ride the separate ObjState block (Onslaught)) Constant TNSF_SETUP (registered id+origin+angles) (dedicated CSQC turret edict / none distinct — folds into the generic NetEntity stream (no ENT_CLIENT_TURRET edict, no turret_construct parse)) Constant TNSF_MOVE / TNSF_ANIM (origin+velocity+yaw / anim_start_time+frame / origin via the generic entity record; head-frame anim rides the body Entity.Frame (no distinct ANIM group))
- `turret-framework.client.presentation` — Recorded divergence — Turret debris is spawned and moved on the server and rendered as ordinary snapshot entities. No tur_head MODEL render: the head aim/avel IS now networked + decoded onto the proxy (Entity.TurHeadPitch/Yaw/AVelYaw, see net.sync), but NO client node yet reads it to rotate a head model/bone (turret_draw tur_head.angles += avelocity*dt) — the render is deferred to 'Phase-3'. The per-shot head-frame anim still rides the turret body's own Entity.Frame instead of a separate head bone. No turret_draw2d radar layer: no waypoint sprite and no over-turret healthbar. No turret_changeteam team recolor (colormap/glowmod). Native turret gibs follow the server tick and a hardcoded lifetime, with immediate removal. Base uses local cl_gibs_ticrate/sloppy and cl_gibs_lifetime, whose shipped default is 2.5 s. The generic native toss skips eWheel, Walker and Tesla. No replacement producer for their QC whole-model toss was found. Constant head rotation render (tur_head.angles += avelocity*dt (turret_draw, every turret) / none (no tur_head sub-entity; head angles are a TurretState vector, never rendered as a separate bone)) Constant head-frame fire anim (tur_head.frame cycle (plasma 1-5, flac 0-3, phaser fireflag 1-10/15 charge/discharge) / runs on the turret edict's own networked Entity.Frame: plasma kick frame=1, flac 0-3 cycle, phaser fireflag charge/discharge (PhaserTurret.TrThinkHeadAnim) — no separate head bone (NEW Wave-8e)) Constant gib toss / gib boom (turret_gibtoss / turret_gibboom (per-type client gib entities) / TurretAI.TossDeathGibs and GibBoom spawn generic-branch server bounce entities rendered from snapshots) Constant waypoint sprite + healthbar (turret_draw2d g_waypointsprite_turrets + drawhealthbar / none) Constant team recolor (turret_changeteam colormap/glowmod (Team_ColorRGB) / none)
- `turret-framework.mapents.path_trigger` — turrets_manager_think + the turret_manager auto-spawn (g_turrets_reloadcvars: re-load every turret's per-unit cvars on a think) not ported — consistent with the per-unit cvar table being absent. (Inert: g_turrets_reloadcvars defaults off and the per-unit cvar table itself is unported, so there is nothing for the manager to reload.) Constant g_turrets_reloadcvars manager (1s think re-load cvars (turret_findtarget auto-spawns turret_manager -> turrets_manager_think) / none (consts))
