- `turret-fusionreactor.identity.def` — Constant head_model (models/turrets/reactor.md3 / none (no head entity)) Constant spawnflags (TUR_FLAG_SUPPORT|TUR_FLAG_AMMOSOURCE / doc-only (no flags int; behavior hardcoded as support))
- `turret-fusionreactor.setup.tr_setup` — Constant tur_head.scale (0.75 / none) Constant tur_head.avelocity (initial) ('0 50 0' / none)
- `turret-fusionreactor.fx.attack` — Constant head avelocity scale ('0 250 0' * ammo/ammo_max / st.HeadAVelocity = '0 250*ammo/ammo_max 0' (computed, but no client head render integrates it))
- `turret-hellion.identity.def` — Constant head_model (models/turrets/hellion.md3 / none (no separate head entity)) Constant spawnflags (TUR_FLAG_SPLASH|FASTPROJ|PLAYER|MISSILE / splash via radius dmg; PLAYER/MISSILE select implicit; FASTPROJ n/a)
- `turret-hk.identity.def` — Constant head_model (models/turrets/hk.md3 / none (no head bone entity)) Constant spawnflags (TUR_FLAG_SPLASH|MEDPROJ|PLAYER|RECIEVETARGETS / SPLASH via radius damage; PLAYER implicit; RECIEVETARGETS now modeled (AddTarget hook wired in Spawn))
- `turret-hk.setup.tr_setup` — Constant ammo_flags (TFL_AMMO_ROCKETS|RECHARGE / ammo pool only (recharge))
- `turret-hk.weapon.guidance` — Re-seek iterates the port's FindInRadius spatial query rather than Base's IL_EACH(g_damagedbycontents) list; functionally equivalent valid-target scan (HkIsValidTarget mirrors hk_is_valid_target), but the candidate set is derived differently. Minor: when enemy is null, port computes angular-offset 'ad' = 0 (explicit null guard, HkThink:258-260); Base derefs null enemy.origin = '0 0 0' yielding a non-zero ad. Post-re-seek the enemy is normally re-acquired, so this branch is rarely hit.
- `turret-hk.target.recieve` — Re-seek/dispatch over the live entity table iterates Api.Entities.All (or per-class FindByClass fallback) filtered by classname 'turret_*' instead of Base's IL_EACH(g_turrets); functionally equivalent turret enumeration, candidate set derived differently.
- `turret-hk.menu.describe` — Constant describe text ('The %s fires a single powerful homing rocket ... similar to that of the %s.' (refs WEP_DEVASTATOR) / Description const string (HkTurret.cs:86) — verbatim text with names resolved literally; no live turret-describe page system)
- `turret-machinegun.spawn.identity` — Constant head_model (models/turrets/machinegun.md3 / NOT IMPLEMENTED (no tur_head entity))
- `turret-machinegun.tunables.balance` — Per-unit values are still hardcoded consts, not loaded from cvars: server overrides of g_turrets_unit_machinegun_*, the g_turrets_reloadcvars live-reload, and the per-entity turret_scale_* map multipliers (load_unit_settings) have no effect. (The turret-FRAMEWORK cvars — targetscan_*/aimidle/nofire — ARE cvar-backed via TurretAI.Cvar; only the per-unit balance block remains hardcoded.)
- `turret-machinegun.weapon.firebullet` — Muzzle flash is emitted at the muzzle ORIGIN along the shot dir, not parented to a tag_fire bone of a head sub-entity (Base setattachment(muzzle_flash, tur_head, "tag_fire"), machinegun_weapon.qc:24) — because the port has no tur_head edict. The flash effect fires; its anchoring is approximate. Minor presentation residual tied to the head-model gap, not a missing emit.
- `turret-machinegun.lifecycle.die_respawn` — Constant respawn volley reset (volly_counter = shot_volly (5) (turret_respawn sv_turrets.qc:282) / VollyCounter = ShotVollyMax>0?ShotVollyMax:1 — ShotVollyMax NEVER SET (defaults 0) -> resets to 1 not 5 (Respawn:1205))
- `turret-mlrs.identity.def` — Constant head_model (models/turrets/mlrs.md3 / models/turrets/mlrs.md3 set on descriptor (consumed by the death head-gib only; no in-world head bone entity))
- `turret-mlrs.anim.ammogauge` — Constant frame target (it.tur_head.frame (separate head entity, netted via TNSF) / e.Frame (turret edict's own frame; no separate head bone))
- `turret-phaser.identity.model_hitbox` — Constant head model (models/turrets/phaser.md3 / (not separately attached))
- `turret-phaser.fire.extra_slow_status` — Recorded divergence — Base's phaser slow is purely the velocity *= 0.75 scale per tick; the port additionally layers a brief StatusEffectsCatalog disability (0.2s) on top to make the slow feel more pronounced. Deliberate port augmentation, not a Base behavior.
- `turret-plasma.balance.cvars` — Unit balance is still hardcoded C# consts, not read from g_turrets_unit_plasma_* — the values now match the cfg, but server cvar overrides / turret_scale_* map controls / g_turrets_reloadcvars have no effect.
- `turret-plasma.damage.gate_retaliate` — PRESENTATION (tracked under track/headspin presentation rows): the headshake jitter perturbs the server-only TurretState.HeadAngles but is never networked/rendered, so the head-recoil flinch is not visible client-side.
- `turret-plasma_dual.spawn.registration` — head_model plasmad.md3 is now set on the descriptor and consumed server-side (TossDeathGibs head gib); the live tur_head/tag_head CLIENT render attachment + spin is still absent (no turret CSQC head-bone layer) — tracked under presentation.head_frame_anim.
- `turret-plasma_dual.attack.plasma_ball` — Two-barrel tag_fire alternation (plasmad.md3) not modeled — single computed ShotOrg muzzle (a head-bone render detail; the muzzleflash + projectile trail themselves are now emitted).
- `turret-plasma_dual.attack.instagib_rail` — FireBullet is a single trace; QC FireRailgunBullet penetration/distance falloff deferred (matches WeaponFiring/TurretCombat documented gaps).
- `turret-plasma_dual.damage.gating_die_respawn` — Death FX/gibs are emitted SERVER-side at the death site (rocket-impact sound + ROCKET_EXPLODE + TossDeathGibs) rather than from a CSQC turret edict — the port has no client turret draw layer, so the FX/sound network to every viewer via the temp-entity/effect feed and the gibs render as physics entities through the entity feed (the VehicleCommon.TossGib idiom). Behaviorally equivalent to cl_turrets.qc turret_die; not yet runtime-confirmed on a live plasma_dual placement (code-trace only).
- `turret-tesla.identity.def` — Constant head_model (models/turrets/tesla_head.md3 / TeslaTurret.cs:52 sets HeadModel = tesla_head.md3 (identity data only); no head-bone entity / render attach consumes it)
- `turret-walker.identity.def` — Constant head_model (walker_head_minigun.md3 / none (no head bone entity))
- `turret-walker.drive.gaits_extra` — Constant turn_strafe (5 / none (no STRAFE gait)) Constant pain defer (walker_setnoanim 0.25 / none (no PAIN gait))
- `turret-walker.weapon.minigun` — Muzzleflash + bullet tracer are emitted at the shared shot org (st.ShotOrg) rather than a dedicated head muzzle tag, since the port has no tur_head sub-entity. Both effects ARE emitted (networked, viewer-independent).
- `turret-walker.weapon.rocket` — Tag resolution is graceful-fallback: if the loaded walker_body.md3 has no tag_rocket01/02 bones, all rockets fire from the shared shot org (the port has no separate tur_head sub-entity that owns the tags).
- `turret-walker.client.draw` — Constant client movetype (MOVETYPE_BOUNCE + gravity 1 (client render copy) / none (pure-client render movetype; server drives origin via MoveType.Step + shared entity feed))
- `vehicle-bumblebee.spawn.vr_spawn` — Constant scale (1.5 / none)
- `vehicle-bumblebee.multiseat.enter` — CSQCVehicleSetup viewport/HUD networking, the temp_wepent weapon swap, and the gunner SVC_SETVIEWPORT/SETVIEWANGLES are cross-boundary / not ported (the player binding fields - takedamage/solid/movetype/alpha/vehicle - ARE set). Touch-board (g_vehicles_enter 0) path: vehic.Touch = Touch is now installed in SpawnVehicle, but it still requires the host collision to dispatch .Touch onto solids; the DEFAULT g_vehicles_enter 1 uses the +use path, so touch-board remains a flagged partial under the non-default config.
- `vehicle-bumblebee.multiseat.exit` — Pilot exit uses VehicleCommon.FindGoodExit; QC's bumblebee_gunner_findgoodexit (100 random-ring tries) is approximated by the shared FindGoodExit helper.
- `vehicle-bumblebee.board.usekey` — Bots have no vehicle path (g_vehicles_allow_bots default 0; VehicleBoarding.Enter refuses bots), so vehicles are human-only — matching Base's default (the allow-bots gameplay is a flagged out-of-scope config).
- `vehicle-framework.init.initialize` — targetname controller / ACTIVE_NOT gating / vehicle_use not ported (map-scripted activation absent, sv_vehicles.qc:1178-1201/1276-1277). STILL OPEN (logic-level deferral). The targetname branch that ASSIGNS .team is unported, but the team-CLEAR branch (below) is independent of it. This is the one remaining unported sub-feature of vehicle_initialize; it has no numeric constant, so it does not downgrade values.
- `vehicle-framework.enter.handshake` — Constant player freeze (DAMAGE_NO/SOLID_NOT/MOVETYPE) (NOCLIP + disableclientprediction / MoveType.None / Solid.Not / DAMAGE_NO)
- `vehicle-framework.enter.steal` — Constant g_vehicles_steal_show_waypoint (WP_VehicleIntruder) (1 / none (CSQC, deferred))
- `vehicle-framework.combat.heal` — func_heal map-object and the mage healing entity are not separately ported, but the .event_heal SINK is reachable on the live path via the Arc heal-beam non-player friendly branch (Combat.Heal -> vehic.GtEventHeal). The Bumblebee healray remains a separate heal SOURCE with its own inline GiveResourceWithLimit (not routed through this sink).
- `vehicle-framework.combat.crush` — weaponLocked(it) is stubbed false (no per-frame weapon-lock plumbed), so the crush fires even mid-switch (matches common in-match case). Touch-board (the !g_vehicles_enter path) depends on the host dual-dispatching .Touch onto solids — MoveTypePhysics.PushEntity does dispatch .Touch. The port default g_vehicles_enter=1 (Base default 0) means touch-board is off by default in the port (config gap). trace_dphitq3surfaceflags NOIMPACT skip in vehicles_impact is deferred (no per-touch surface flag surfaced to the headless callback). Constant g_vehicles_enter (touch-board gate) (0 (default) / 1 fallback in Touch (g_vehicles_enter==0 -> touch-board))
- `vehicle-framework.combat.impact` — NOIMPACT surface-flag skip deferred (the headless touch callback has no per-touch surface flag). Constant impact gate (Δspeed > minspeed, once / 0.25s, skip NOIMPACT / delta>minspeed + play_time<time gate; 0.25s; NOIMPACT deferred)
- `vehicle-framework.physics.force_fromtag` — Tag-less fallback computes the spring from the entity up-axis instead of a per-engine tag — a faithful approximation when tags are present.
- `vehicle-framework.respawn.return_waypoint` — Constant WP_Vehicle return waypoint (spawned (vehicle_return + vehicles_showwp) / none (deferred, CSQC))
- `vehicle-framework.impulse.mode_switch` — The shared imp 17 chase-cam toggle is consumed but does nothing (cl_eventchase_vehicle is a client cvar with no in-vehicle camera to toggle). Constant IMP_weapon_drop chase toggle (17 -> toggle cl_eventchase_vehicle / 17 consumed (no-op; client cvar))
- `vehicle-framework.hooks.init_touch` — Both hooks dispatch on the live path: VehicleTouch (suppress-on-true) from VehicleCommon.Touch:746; VehicleInit (abort-on-true) from VehicleSpawnFuncs.Spawn:73 (calls VehicleCommon.InitVehicle, deletes the edict on a true return). No vehicle-framework mutator currently REGISTERS either hook (no stock Base mutator does either) — the dispatch is live but the chains are empty in the default config, which matches Base.
- `vehicle-framework.lifecycle.reset` — The map-scripted ACTIVE_NOT gate (`if (this.active != ACTIVE_NOT) vehicles_spawn`) is not ported — the port has no per-vehicle active state, so VehiclesReset ALWAYS respawns. Matches the stock-map common case (every vehicle is ACTIVE_ACTIVE) but a map that deactivated a vehicle via a controller would see it respawn on reset where Base would leave it dormant. (Tied to the same unported targetname/ACTIVE controller as init.initialize.) vehicles_clearreturn is folded into VehiclesReset (cancels the vehicle's own scheduled return Think) rather than removing a separate return-helper entity — the port never spawns a return-helper entity (SetReturn re-arms the vehicle's own Think), so this is functionally equivalent. Constant respawn if active != ACTIVE_NOT (vehicles_spawn) (yes / yes (VehiclesReset:916 -> descriptor Spawn; ACTIVE_NOT gate absent -> always respawns))
- `vehicle-framework.death.gibs` — The 4th Base gib — the BODY tossgib (bumblebee.qc:821) — is not spawned; the port keeps the diethink/blowup/respawn cycle on the original vehicle body in place instead (functionally equivalent for the death->blowup->respawn loop, one fewer throwaway entity; documented at Bumblebee.cs:535-538). Only the 3 GUN gibs are tossed. EFFECT_EXPLOSION_SMALL at the wreck origin (gib_explode's wp00 blast) uses gib.GoalEntity as the QC .wp00 link — faithful, but the wreck may have respawned by the time a 6s-window gib explodes (minor edge case). Constant body tossgib (4th gib, bumblebee.qc:821) (tossed / NOT tossed — diethink/blowup/respawn kept on the original body (documented Bumblebee.cs:535))
- `vehicle-racer.identity.def` — Constant hud_model (wakizashi_cockpit.dpm / none (cosmetic)) Constant view_ofs ('0 0 50' / not set (cosmetic view)) Constant height (200 / not set (cosmetic view-dist))
- `vehicle-racer.spawn.lifecycle` — Constant scale (0.5 / not set (cosmetic))
- `vehicle-racer.spawnfunc.liveness` — Minor: LoadCvars uses a `value != 0 ? value : default` guard, so a server that explicitly sets a tunable to 0 (e.g. g_vehicle_racer_health_regen 0, bouncestop 0, rocket_locktarget 0) keeps the Base-default field rather than honouring the 0. For racer the meaningful tunables are all non-zero defaults, so this only affects the (rarely-tuned-to-zero) values; a cvar-presence query would close it.
- `vehicle-racer.drive.afterburn` — Boost sound plays on the vehicle entity, not a tur_head sub-entity (the port has no tur_head); audibly equivalent (same origin). Constant boost sound entity (vehic.tur_head / vehicle (no tur_head sub-entity))
- `vehicle-racer.death.blowup` — setSendEntity(func_null) stop-networking the wreck is a net-layer concern, unmodeled (Death NOTE line 669). vehicles_setreturn's CSQC return waypoint / radar icon + EFFECT_TELEPORT return flash are presentation and unported (the authoritative return SCHEDULE is set by SetReturn).
- `vehicle-racer.impact.bouncepain` — The Q3SURFACEFLAG_NOIMPACT skip (Base early-returns on a no-impact surface) is deferred: the headless touch callback has no per-touch surface-flag in the collision result. Minor fidelity detail. OldVelocity is snapshotted at Think-start (before this tick's Frame) rather than in the engine integrator just before the clip, so the measured speed-change is the across-tick delta — close to but not bit-exact with DP's .oldvelocity capture point.
- `raptor.spawn.setup` — Constant mass (1 / 1 (set but INERT — engine physics never reads .Mass))
- `raptor.enter` — Constant carried-flag cockpit offset ('-20 0 96' (raptor vr_enter) / '0 0 96' (inherits VehicleDef.FlagCarryOffset default — Raptor does NOT override; Racer DOES to '-190 0 96'))
- `raptor.cannon_aim_lock` — DRIFT FIX (2026-06-27b): the aux lock crosshair color feedback (red→yellow→green by lock strength) IS now drawn — NetGame.UpdateVehicleHud:3443 calls hud.SetAuxiliaryXhairLock(0, target.Origin, veh.VehLockStrength, 'gfx/vehicles/crosshair_lock') live each frame while veh.VehLockTarget is held. Closed the prior 'not drawn' gap. locktarget==2 alternate direct-trace lock path unimplemented (default is 1, so off the live path). AimTurret uses an Euler-subtract approximation of QC's AnglesTransform left-divide — behaviorally close but not bit-identical (the residual 'medium' caveat).
- `raptor.mode_switch` — DRIFT FIX (2026-06-27b): the CSQCVehicleSetup-equivalent client HUD reticle update on mode change IS now done — NetGame.UpdateVehicleHud->FeedRaptorReticle (NetGame.cs:3517) reads the server-authoritative veh.VehW2Mode every render frame and re-selects vCROSS_BURST (bomb) / vCROSS_RAIN (flare), so switching the secondary flips the centered reticle (and toggles the bomb dropmark) live. Closed the prior 'not done' gap.
- `raptor.painframe` — DRIFT FIX (2026-06-27b): the EFFECT_SMOKE_SMALL low-health smoke puff (origin + randomvec()*80) IS now emitted — VehicleCommon.PainFrame:760 EffectEmitter.Emit('SMOKE_SMALL', vehic.Origin + Prandom.Vec()*80, 1) (the old 'no headless emitter' deferral no longer holds; SMOKE_SMALL is a registered effect on the live EffectNetSink->client render path). Gated on myHealth<=50 exactly like QC. No remaining gap.
- `raptor.hud_crosshair` — Pilot path is the host/listen-server LOCAL Player (the live single-player + listen-server pilot case): UpdateVehicleHud feeds the panel straight from LocalServerPlayer's 0..100 vehicle stat mirror + the live veh.VehW2Mode/VehLockTarget/VehLockStrength. The cross-client VEHICLESTAT_* networking so a PURE remote client (not the host) sees the same bars/reticle is the documented follow-up (NetGame.cs:2648 comment) — liveness:live for the normal local-pilot path, partial only for a non-host remote pilot. Minor: the dropmark uses a fixed 0.05s integration step over 200 iterations (VehiclePhysicsHelpers.cs:BombDropPredict) rather than QC tracetoss's engine ballistic; impact point is close but not bit-identical.
- `raptor.cb_shellfrag_gib` — Recorded divergence — Tumble jitter advances on committed tics for consistent render-rate playback. MultiMesh uses a sink and bounds the separate fragment pool to 64. The launch lacks QC's surface force-direction backoff and initial roll offsets. Native fragments do not react to blast force. Fragment orientation and fade have not been compared in a paired Raptor gameplay capture. Constant frag lifetime / fade (nextthink time+3, fade after cnt=time+2 / lifetime 3, sink over final second) Constant avelocity tumble (avelocity = prandomvec()*vlen(vel) + randomvec()*15/draw / |vel| spin seed plus independent jitter of 15 per committed tic)
- `raptor.missile_alarm` — QC uses soundto(MSG_ONE, vehic, ...) so the alarm is heard ONLY by the pilot (msg_entity = this); the port plays it on the vehicle entity via the general sound API (heard positionally by anyone in range). Behaviorally the warning still fires on the right trigger, but the per-pilot-only routing (MSG_ONE) is not reproduced — minor audio-scope divergence.
- `vehicle-spiderbot.identity.registration` — Constant view_ofs (0 0 70 / not modeled (no seated view-offset in headless port)) Constant height (170 / not modeled)
- `vehicle-spiderbot.spawn.setup` — Port adds a MoveGround vehicle flag not present in QC vr_setup; harmless shared-helper flag, not a Base value.
- `vehicle-spiderbot.enter` — Constant flagcarried reattach offset (setattachment(flag, tur_head) + setorigin '-20 0 120' (rides the HEAD bone, engine-carried) / Enter does the same ONCE, but Ctf.Tick now re-parks it every tick at FlagCarryOffset='0 0 96' anchored to the vehicle ORIGIN (no spiderbot override) -> clobbers the head attachment)
- `vehicle-spiderbot.exit` — Port keys the eject branch on IsDead(vehicle) rather than the explicit eject flag QC passes; equivalent on the death-eject path but the +use 'normal' exit always takes the momentum branch (matches QC eject=false). antilag_clear(player) is not called on exit (antilag system cross-boundary).
- `vehicle-spiderbot.frame.controller` — Residual: the controller is invoked once per 0.1s (DefaultThinkRate) and now correctly steps slew/turn/gravity by that elapsed dt (0.1), so the aggregate per-second rate MATCHES QC (which runs every movement frame stepping by PHYS_INPUT_FRAMETIME). The only remaining divergence is granularity: response is quantized to 0.1s steps instead of per-frame, so it is coarser but no longer wrong-magnitude. Constant controller invocation cadence (PHYS_INPUT_FRAMETIME (every movement frame) / 0.1 (DefaultThinkRate))
- `vehicle-spiderbot.frame.head_aim` — Per-tag fallback offset when a tag is missing is the same hardcoded (60,0,20) for BOTH hardpoints, so the headless fallback collapses the two to one point (minor, only when the model tags are absent). With real model tags the averaged origin matches QC line 76-82/95-96. The v_forward averaging (QC lines 79-82) is used only for the aux-crosshair traceline (client/presentation, UpdateAuxiliaryXhair) and is not produced; the head-aim itself uses crosshair_trace endpos minus the averaged origin and matches.
- `vehicle-spiderbot.frame.locomotion` — Body-turn step multiplies by dt=0.1 (the elapsed think dt), matching QC's aggregate per-frame rate; response is quantized to the 0.1s think cadence (coarser granularity, not wrong-magnitude).
- `vehicle-spiderbot.weapon.modeswitch` — CSQCVehicleSetup HUD/crosshair networking on the QC mode-set is deferred; the W2MODE state itself is set + networked via the vehicle stat.
- `vehicle-spiderbot.death.blowup` — Residual: the head gib's MF_ROCKET modelflag (the QC rocket-smoke-trail render) is a client-only modelflag with no port Entity field, so it is not produced (EF_FLAME IS set on the head gib). The client VehicleVisuals ALSO does its own approximate scripted Godot-mesh gib arc (GibCount) on the alive->dead edge — a non-1:1 presentation overlap with the now-faithful server gibs, not a missing feature. Everything else (4 networked physics gib entities, headfade EXPLOSION_BIG+impact, per-burn EXPLOSION_SMALL+impact, 250-dmg blast, 45s respawn) is faithful.
- `vehicle-spiderbot.impact.bouncepain` — QC vr_impact is `if (autocvar_g_vehicle_spiderbot_bouncepain) vehicles_impact(...)`. The port's Spiderbot.Impact (Spiderbot.cs:155-165) mirrors this exactly: it guards on `bouncepain != Vector3.Zero` with bouncepain hardcoded to the stock default '0 0 0', so the guard is always false and vehicles_impact is skipped — a faithful no-op at stock cvars (QC's vector cvar is likewise falsy). A server-overridable bouncepain cvar is not exposed, so a non-default '0 0 0' override cannot be reproduced; the VehicleCommon.Impact (vehicles_impact) helper it would call IS present and used by Racer/Bumblebee.
- `warpzones.transform.algebra` — Recorded divergence — Port encodes the transform with an explicit forward/right/up basis (WarpzoneTransform) instead of QC's AnglesTransform Euler-vector algebra — same math, headless-testable. AnglesTransform_Normalize/CancelRoll view-angle canonicalisation is not reproduced (only relevant once the client view-rotate exists, currently moot). Constant transform_encoding (AnglesTransform Euler-vector (transform+shift) / explicit fwd/right/up basis)
- `warpzones.spawn.trigger` — The EF_NODEPTHTEST render flag and WarpZone_Send CSQC serialisation are unported (no client warpzone entity — shared with the missing client render rows). scale + the warpzone_isboxy box-vs-exacttrigger distinction ARE now modeled in AddMapZone (Warpzone.cs:738-777). Constant EF_NODEPTHTEST render flag + WarpZone_Send CSQC serialisation (BITSET EF_NODEPTHTEST; setSendEntity(WarpZone_Send) (server.qc:667-669) / none (no client warpzone entity))
- `warpzones.teleport.player` — The frame-back re-trace (WarpZone_Teleport f0/f1 double-touch avoidance, server.qc:80) and WarpZoneLib_MoveOutOfSolid stuck-recovery are not ported. WarpZone_Projectile_Touch (the projectile .move_touch filter that aborts a stray impact and re-teleports on the same frame, server.qc:344) and the WARPZONELIB_REMOVEHACK-gated WarpZone_CheckProjectileImpact impact-filter are absent — a rocket that touches the trigger and a wall in the same frame could detonate on the seam instead of passing through. The QC pre-gate skip for move_movetype == MOVETYPE_NONE/MOVETYPE_FOLLOW || tag_entity (server.qc:189) is not modeled (the .Touch lambda guards on Solid.Not only).
- `warpzones.teleport.usetargets` — Minor: for the THIS-zone relay the port passes trigger=trig where Base passes trigger=toucher (BIT-skipped slots are unaffected; only matters if a relayed handler reads the trigger edict). Functionally equivalent for the common cases.
- `warpzones.combat.traceline` — Recorded divergence — Port detects crossings analytically (plane + trigger AABB) rather than QC's WarpZone_MakeAllSolid + tracebox-hits-the-now-solid-zone; same outcome, deterministic and headless-testable.
- `warpzones.combat.findradius` — Recorded divergence — Radius recursion uses an explicit front-side gate (Dot(org-InOrigin, InForward) >= 0) instead of QC's per-zone LOS traceline + radius reduction to discard the wrong side (WarpzoneRadiusQuery.cs:407) — same result without the extra trace, avoids back-warp loops. Same-room LOS is still warpzone-aware via WeaponSplash.cs:161. VERIFIER: three un-flagged numeric divergences from WarpZone_FindRadius_Recurse: (1) radius reduction uses distance to the zone PLANE ORIGIN (wz.InOrigin) not Base's vlen(org_new - org0_new) (the transformed blast-to-zone distance) — slightly different falloff budget through a portal; (2) per-victim distance uses the entity CENTER not Base's WarpZoneLib_NearestPointOnBox (nearest point on the victim's AABB) — large/elongated victims at the radius edge can be mis-included/excluded; (3) is_pure() blacklist branch not modeled. Outcome-equivalent for the common small-victim, single-portal blast; edge cases diverge. Constant radius recurse clamp (bound(0, rad - vlen(org_new-org0_new), rad - 8) / Clamp(rad - dist_to_zone_plane, 0, rad - 8)) Constant blacklist classnames (is_pure + weaponentity/exteriorweaponentity/sprite_waypoint/waypoint/spawnfunc/weaponchild/chatbubbleentity/buff_model + target_*/info_* / same; Wave-2 added a partial is_pure approx (Solid.Not && empty classname) but no Entity.PureData flag yet) Constant per-victim distance (WarpZoneLib_NearestPointOnBox(it.mins,it.maxs,org0) / entity center (wz.InOrigin/org distance))
- `warpzones.porto` — Constant porto trigger bounds (weapon-defined / +/-48 box (PortoMins/Maxs, Warpzone.cs:502-503))
- `warpzones.client.render` — Constant fade by distance (warpzone_fadestart/_fadeend / none (no PVS/distance fade yet))
- `warpzones.client.fixview` — Constant r_drawexteriormodel inside (0 (except classic chase) / normal local first-person body exclusion)
- `warpzones.reconnect` — The reconnect spawnflag-1 'don't reconnect a zone a client can currently see' cosmetic skip (visible_to_some_client, server.qc:790-792) is not honoured — the port reconnects every matching zone regardless. This is a seam-pop avoidance, not a correctness gate; the conservative port may briefly pop a visible seam on a runtime reconnect. Camera targets now resolve on reconnect. The pinned visible-to-client suppression also applies to cameras and remains open. Constant reconnect spawnflag 1 (skip visible zones) (!(spawnflags&1 && visible_to_some_client(e)) (server.qc:790) / no client-visibility test (reconnects regardless; conservative))
- `warpzones.observer_warp` — The teleportable simple-teleporter half of WarpZone_StartFrame's observer pass (server.qc:768-774, Teleport_Find + Simple_TeleportPlayer for observers) is a teleporter concern, not handled here. WarpZone_StoreProjectileData per-frame projectile snapshot (server.qc:749) is still absent (only relevant to the unported client view-rotate / FixVAngle path).
- `weapon-arc.identity.attributes` — Constant muzzle effect (EFFECT_ARC_MUZZLEFLASH + flash.md3 muzzle model (carried as m_muzzleeffect/m_muzzlemodel attribs) / ARC_MUZZLEFLASH emitted on the bolt path only; not carried as a weapon attribute, no flash.md3 entity) Constant crosshair (gfx/crosshairhlac size 0.7 / none (CSQC attribute))
- `weapon-arc.beam.dps_core` — Residual (UNCHANGED): the warpzone TRANSFORM half of WarpZone_traceline_antilag is still not modeled (plain segment trace, no per-segment WarpZone_TransformOrigin re-projection); identical to Base only when no warpzones lie on the beam path. The multi-segment bezier body itself is now traced (the prior 'single straight trace' gap is closed — see notes). LagComp.Begin/End bracket around the swept damage trace (Arc.cs:423-442) mirrors Base WarpZone_traceline_antilag at ANTILAG_LATENCY. Provider IS installed live (ServerNet ctor assigns LagComp.Provider = new LagCompProvider). Antilag rewind is live; only the warpzone transform is absent.
- `weapon-arc.beam.heal_teammates` — Minor residual: Base routes the health heal through Heal()->PlayerHeal which also early-outs on game_stopped/frozen/dead/spectator. The port calls GiveResourceWithLimit(Health) directly for player/creature targets (gated only by the beam's own same-team/live-damageable check) — so a frozen/just-dying teammate could in principle still receive a tick of healing. Non-player OBJECTIVES route through Combat.Heal -> GtEventHeal (Arc.cs:425-426). Edge-case only; clamping behavior matches.
- `weapon-arc.beam.heat_overheat` — Constant cooldown_release (0 / 0 (still unused at stock))
- `weapon-arc.bolt.secondary_burst` — Constant bolt_refire2 (0.16667 / 0.16667 (no separate tail enforcement))
- `weapon-arc.bolt.explode_bounce` — Bounce path (bolt_bounce_count > 0) does not clear owner, set HITTYPE_BOUNCE, or emit EFFECT_BALL_SPARKS like Base (arc.qc:120-124); dormant at stock bolt_bounce_count 0. UNCHANGED.
- `weapon-arc.checkammo` — Minor: Base wr_checkammo2 bolt branch also counts reloaded `weapon_load` (cells >= bolt_ammo from the reserve) as satisfying; the port has no per-weapon reload reserve, so this OR-term is absent. Irrelevant without the reload system (g_balance_arc_reload_ammo 0 at stock). UNCHANGED.
- `weapon-arc.beam.visual` — Constant beam render (Draw_CylindricLine straight beam (cl_arcbeam_simple default) + arc_beam trailparticles ALONGSIDE / BeamRenderer.Beam cylinder (ARC_BEAM_LINE/_HEAL/_BURST) + arc_beam spark trail (ARC_BEAM) — both emitted)
- `weapon-arc.smoke_overheat_fx` — Minor: the muzzle origin is approximated from the eye + view forward (Arc.cs:272-274) rather than the weapon movedir tag (Base arc.qc:516-520) — headless, no weapon tag available; particle spawn point is slightly off but cue/probability logic is exact.
- `weapon-arc.bot_aim` — The non-stock beam_botaimspeed>0 lead-tuning branch (bot_aim(actor, ent, beam_botaimspeed, 0, beam_botaimlifetime, ...) arc.qc:558-559) is not separately modeled per-weapon — it would need a per-weapon bot-aim-speed seam the brain doesn't expose for a hitscan weapon. Dormant at stock beam_botaimspeed 0 (the only live branch is the 1000000/0/0.001 hitscan fallback, which the generic path reproduces). Coverage of the non-stock branch only.
- `weapon-blaster.primary.attack` — fire sound is on the port's single SoundChannel.Weapon; QC uses CH_WEAPON_B specifically (cosmetic channel-routing nuance, same cue).
- `weapon-blaster.shot.velocity` — g_projectiles_newton_style velocity inheritance not modeled; matches the stock default 0 (no inheritance), so no observable divergence in stock play.
- `weapon-blaster.bolt.touch_radiusdamage` — Recorded divergence — QC plays the impact sound + impact particle CLIENT-side in CSQC wr_impacteffect (at w_org along w_backoff); the port emits both server-side from OnTouch (project-wide pattern, see WeaponSplash.ImpactSound docs). Cue (laserimpact) and blast position match; the exact w_backoff plane-normal is approximated by -normalize(velocity). impact particle uses -normalize(velocity) as the w_backoff plane normal (approximation, not the exact surface normal); CSQCProjectile networking of the impact is server-emitted instead of CSQC-rendered.
- `weapon-blaster.secondary.lastweapon` — QC gates the switch on m_switchweapon == WEP_BLASTER (the weapon being switched TO); the port gates on ActiveWeaponId == RegistryId (the currently active weapon). Functionally equivalent in steady state, but during a switch-in-progress the precise guard window may differ (minor edge case). fallback when no previous weapon recorded picks best-owned-by-impulse; QC W_LastWeapon relies on the tracked previous selection (cnt) — minor edge-case behavior on a first-ever secondary press. Constant gate (only if m_switchweapon == WEP_BLASTER / only if ActiveWeaponId == RegistryId)
- `weapon-blaster.bot.aim_dodge` — lead is a GENERIC engine-level projectile lead (BotBrain.ShotLead using the weapon's speed cvar), not the per-weapon wr_aim hook; faithful in effect for the blaster (leads at speed 6000), but the wr_aim/bot_aim weapon-dispatch seam itself is not modeled. dodge is SUPERBOT-gated (Skill>100) exactly as in Base (havocbot_dodge is disabled for non-SUPERBOT bots there too), so under stock skill levels bots do not specifically swerve from bolts; this matches Base behaviour, it is not a port limitation.
- `crylink.secondary.attack` — spreadtype 0 (linear horizontal fan) IS implemented (Crylink.cs:239-243) but unreachable at the stock default spreadtype 1. Not a defect — full parity coverage.
- `crylink.linkjoin.converge_on_release` — Port keys the group on the C# group[0] list head; QC re-heads the queuenext/queueprev ring to a live spike when the head dies. If the port's head spike dies before release the group can't converge (WrThink notes this and still clears wait-release so the player isn't stranded, Crylink.cs:166-175), whereas Base would converge the survivors. Edge case. WarpZone reference-frame transforms (WarpZone_RefSys_Transform*) in W_Crylink_LinkJoin are omitted — no-op without warpzones.
- `crylink.linkjoin.joinexplode_bonus` — Convergence-count test uses the C# group list rather than the QC queuenext ring; if the LinkJoin head dangled (head spike died pre-release) the group never schedules the think, so the survivors' convergence sparkle is missed in that edge case (same head-dangle limitation as crylink.linkjoin.converge_on_release).
- `crylink.ammo.checkammo` — Clip-load fallback term (weapon_load[m_id] >= ammo) omitted; moot while reload_ammo defaults 0 (clips disabled).
- `crylink.fx.trail_muzzle_impact` — Recorded divergence — Muzzleflash/impact particle/trail are server-side-emitted-then-networked rather than from CSQC wr_impacteffect — the port-wide presentation convention, visually equivalent. The big/small impact-particle mapping (primary=impactbig, secondary=impact) matches Base exactly (EffectsList.cs:75-76 vs all.inc:48-49, re-verified line-for-line).
- `crylink.bot.wr_aim` — Secondary lead speed: QC's wr_aim leads the secondary shot at WEP_CVAR_SEC(speed) (3000); the port's BotAimShotSpeed returns a single speed (Primary.Speed=2000) regardless of which mode the 10/90 pick routes to. This is the shared port-wide wr_aim lead-speed simplification (the brain keys lead off one per-weapon BotAimShotSpeed, not per-fire-mode), tracked at bot-ai.aim.lead — not a crylink-specific logic defect. The mode-PICK and the primary lead are faithful. Constant secondary lead speed (bot_aim(secondary_speed,...) / BotAimShotSpeed => Primary.Speed (single-speed lead, shared simplification))
- `weapon-devastator.fire.launch` — W_DecreaseAmmo's clip path is not modeled (reload_ammo 0, so identical for stock); port uses a plain TakeResource.
- `weapon-devastator.flight.laser_guide` — WarpZone RefSys transforms on the aim ray (desireddir/desiredorigin) are dropped — guiding through a warpzone aims in the unwarped frame. Dual-wield movedir offset (dv) is omitted — but QC also zeroes dv unless dual-wielding, so faithful on the live single-slot path.
- `weapon-devastator.explode.remote` — QC passes the rocket-jump target (head) as the RadiusDamage forceintersect arg (force-application target) for the plain remote blast; the port's RadiusDamage has no forceintersect param. Inert at point-blank rocket-jump range (no LOS reduction engages).
- `weapon-devastator.firer.transparency` — QC clipgroup (same-owner projectiles not colliding with each other) and g_projectiles_interact variants are deferred (do not affect firer self-hit).
- `weapon-devastator.ammo.checkammo_reload` — wr_suicidemessage (WEAPON_DEVASTATOR_SUICIDE) not separately verified in the port.
- `weapon-devastator.presentation.flight_fx` — Constant muzzle flash model (models/flash.md3 (MDL_DEVASTATOR_MUZZLEFLASH) / not attached (effect only)) Constant crosshair (gfx/crosshairrocketlauncher size 0.7 / unaudited)
- `weapon-devastator.bot.aim` — BotWantsDetonate iterates the brain's Players() as the target set rather than QC's g_bot_targets intrusive list (close approximation; both filter to damageable bot targets, but a non-player damageable target would be missed). The 'don't fire a new shot while detonating' interlock is enforced by the brain (BotBrain:368-369 sets wantAttack=false when BotWantsDetonate is true), not inside the weapon — equivalent effect.
- `weapon-devastator.presentation.impact_fx` — Recorded divergence — QC fires wr_impacteffect from CSQC on the networked projectile-impact event (w_org/w_backoff backoff offset, w_issilent gating); the port emits the same ROCKET_EXPLODE particle + rocket_impact.wav cue directly from the server-side Explode/DoRemoteExplode at the detonation origin (the port's effect/sound sinks are host-swapped for networking). Cues match; the w_backoff surface-normal offset and w_issilent suppression are not separately modeled. w_backoff surface-normal offset (org2 = w_org + w_backoff*2) on the particle origin is not applied — effect spawns at the rocket origin, not nudged off the impacted surface. w_issilent gating (suppress the impact sound for a silenced detonation) is not modeled.
- `weapon-devastator.menu.describe` — The trailing W_Guide_Keybinds + W_Guide_DPS_onlyOne_unnamed helper lines (per-binding keybind hints + DPS figure) are not reproduced — those QC helpers are unported across the whole weapon-guide. The narrative paragraphs (remote-controlled rocket, hold-to-guide, ammo drain / alternate-with-Vortex, high-damage role, rocket-flying) ARE present.
- `electro.primary.bolt_projectile` — Minor: the trail/glow are the port's procedural ProjectileCatalog visual (blue plasma TR_NEXUIZPLASMA + saturated blue glow light + elaser model via the host ModelFactory), not a 1:1 CSQC particle replay; the trail spacing rides DP's faithful per-segment sim. Visual identity matches; exact particle counts are presentation-tuned.
- `electro.combo.trigger_chain` — IS_INDEPENDENT_PLAYER owner-gating in W_Electro_TriggerCombo (port TriggerCombo, Electro.cs:567-598) is not ported here (it IS ported in the midair path); dead-OFF-equivalent since Entity.IsIndependentPlayer is hardwired false and default modes have no independent players.
- `electro.secondary.orb_projectile` — Minor: in-flight visual is the port's procedural ProjectileCatalog body (glow sprite + ebomb model via host ModelFactory) with the TR_NEXUIZPLASMA blue plasma trail + dynamic light; the CSQC electro_orb_draw scale-pulse is not reproduced (cosmetic).
- `electro.secondary.orb_touch_explode` — Sticky contact and moving-player follow have their own registry row.
- `electro.ammo_reload` — No Electro-specific wr_reload override (Base's is W_Reload(min(pri,sec) ammo, SND_RELOAD)); relies on the generic WeaponFireGate.WrReload. Acceptable since reload_ammo defaults 0; the reload amount min(4,2)=2 differs from the generic path only on a reload-enabled server (harmless at stock defaults).
- `electro.orb.csqc_netlink_draw` — Constant orb_setup_scale_factor (combo_radius*0.05 * bound(0,(ltime-time)*4,1) / none (procedural catalog glow, no shrink-on-death pulse)) Constant orb_avelocity ('7 0 11' (spin) / none) Constant orb_alpha (0.7 / catalog-tuned)
- `electro.secondary.sticky_orb` — Recorded divergence — A freed native carrier no longer identifies a live attachment. Native consistently removes the orb, matching stock's null-reference outcome and avoiding the measured stock stale-slot world-origin drop on some disconnects.
- `electro.combo.explode_over_time` — Recorded divergence — g_balance_electro_combo_duration defaults 0 in bal-wep-xonotic.cfg (only 1.5 in bal-wep-mario / testing.cfg), so the explode-over-time path is dead in stock play; intentionally omitted. When combo_duration>0 the combo detonation spawns a persistent field (W_Electro_Orb_ExplodeOverTime) that re-RadiusDamages every think (combo_damage*PHYS_INPUT_TIMELENGTH) until ltime, ORing HITTYPE_SPAM to suppress repeat effects. ExplodeCombo (Electro.cs:601-613) always does the single instantaneous RadiusDamage; the combo_duration branch (electro.qc:172-178) is absent.
- `weapon-fireball.identity` — Crosshair gfx (gfx/crosshairfireball) not set here (separate crosshair table); model render fidelity unverified at render time. Unchanged from baseline.
- `weapon-fireball.primary.chargeup` — Render fidelity of EFFECT_FIREBALL_PRE_MUZZLEFLASH (the actual swirling particle visual) is the same deferred CSQC-effect-layer concern as every weapon; the gameplay-side Emit IS now wired (effect registered in EffectsList).
- `weapon-fireball.primary.lifetime` — Port sets cnt=1 on timeout (so the BFG sweep is now correctly suppressed) but does NOT OR HITTYPE_SPLASH into the projectile's deathtype — a timed-out kill is tagged as the plain BLAST rather than carrying the SPLASH hittype. Cosmetic/obituary-only (the obituary BLAST/FIREMINE split keys on HITTYPE_SECONDARY, not SPLASH, so the displayed line is unaffected). Constant timeout cnt + HITTYPE_SPLASH (cnt=1; projectiledeathtype|=HITTYPE_SPLASH / self.Cnt=1 (suppresses BFG sweep))
- `weapon-fireball.primary.shootable` — Constant explode timing + mutator hook (deferred to NEXT FRAME (nextthink=time; setthink) + MUTATOR_CALLHOOK(PrepareExplosionByDamage) / synchronous Explode in the damage call; no PrepareExplosionByDamage hook)
- `weapon-fireball.secondary.firemine.lifecycle` — FIXED (Wave-4 fix-up): the owner-decouple attribution bug is resolved. The firer is now captured into a closure-local `firer` at Attack2 spawn and threaded through FiremineThink/FiremineTouch (and as the `creditOwner` arg of LaserPlay), so burn/obituary credit survives the Owner=null decouple even though the port's RealOwner aliases Owner. The same-team / firer-skip gates in LaserPlay also use the threaded firer so they stay correct post-decouple. Touch silent + bounce-on-fail now match Base (no impact sound; Fire_AddDamage<0 keeps bouncing).
- `weapon-fireball.burn.model` — Fire transfer iteration uses FindInRadius + a takedamage check rather than QC's exact g_damagedbycontents list (no such list in the port) — a faithful approximation of the same overlap set; and FireApplyDamage's owner-resolve is FireOwner??Source rather than QC's owner-chain walk (functionally credits the same fire_owner).
- `hagar.secondary.bounce` — Reachable only at g_balance_hagar_secondary_load=0 (non-stock) — this matches Base, where stock secondary_load=1 also routes the alt-fire to the load machine and never reaches W_Hagar_Attack2. SecondaryLoad is cvar-seeded from g_balance_hagar_secondary_load, so a load=0 config genuinely runs AttackBounce/BounceTouch; at stock load=1 it is correctly unreachable in BOTH Base and port.
- `hagar.secondary.load_release` — load_animtime (0.2) post-release anim time is folded into the secondary refire gate (AnimtimeFor returns 0; release schedules become-READY after Secondary.Refire) — cosmetic anim-only. Salvo W_SetupShot accuracy/recoil pass uses sec.damage*shots in Base (cosmetic accuracy denominator); the port's SetupShot does not scale the recoil arg by shots. Constant secondary_load_animtime (0.2 / folded into secondary refire gate)
- `hagar.secondary.load_charge_machine` — load_animtime (0.2) post-release anim time is folded into the secondary refire gate (no separate animtime cvar in the port; release schedules become-READY after Secondary.Refire) — cosmetic anim-only. Re-verified 2026-06-25: the load==0 out-of-ammo branch (hagar.qc:357-363 wr_checkammo1+2 -> W_SwitchToOtherWeapon when neither has ammo) is NOT ported — the port's AttackLoad else-branch only resets the beep/warning flags. Minor: out-of-ammo auto-switch from the empty-hand upkeep tick is dropped (the player keeps the empty Hagar instead of auto-switching).
- `hagar.damage.shootdown` — exception value hardcoded to 1f on all three spawn paths; Base W_Hagar_Damage computes a DYNAMIC is_linkexplode (load_linkexplode=0 for same-owner secondary-load link, else -1). MOOT at stock g_projectiles_damage=-2 (both Base and port reject ALL projectile damage, so no rocket is shootable). Diverges only at non-stock modes -1/0/1. this.angles = vectoangles(this.velocity) re-orient on taking (non-lethal) damage is not ported (cosmetic; the rocket already self-aligns its angles to velocity only on bounce). damageforcescale (knockback applied to the rocket when shot) not modeled; moot at default 0. Constant load_linkexplode (0 / exception hardcoded 1 (not the dynamic is_linkexplode)) Constant damageforcescale (0 / unused)
- `hagar.explosion.radiusdamage` — Damage_DamageInfo blast-effect networking deferred (shared WeaponSplash gap, render-only).
- `hagar.fx.muzzle_explode_trail` — Recorded divergence — Muzzleflash, fire sound and explosion are emitted server-side and networked rather than in CSQC wr_impacteffect; this is the port-wide presentation convention and is visually/aurally equivalent.
- `hagar.load_lifecycle` — WrPlayerDeath / WrResetPlayer dispatch only on the currently-active weapon (Inventory.CurrentWeapon), whereas Base wr_resetplayer iterates ALL weaponslots clearing hagar_load on each. Equivalent in the single-active-weapon port (load only exists on the held Hagar) but narrower than Base's all-slot sweep — latent once dual-wield lands.
- `weapon-hlac.secondary.burst` — Base decrements ammo AFTER W_SetupShot; port decrements before the loop. No observable difference (single actor, single tick).
- `weapon-hlac.bolt.impact` — Base damages only on touch (SUB_Remove at lifetime does no damage); port think=Remove also does no damage — matches.
- `weapon-hlac.projectile.velocity` — Recorded divergence — VERIFIED 2026-06-22 against tracing.qc:184-213 (re-confirmed post-Wave-2; ProjectileVelocity body unchanged — WeaponFiring.cs:702-704 still `QMath.Normalize(dir)*speed` -> |v|==speed exactly). QC `W_SetupProjVelocity_Explicit` calls `W_CalculateSpread(dir, spread, style, false)` (NOT normalized) then `proj.velocity = W_CalculateProjectileVelocity(owner, owner.velocity, pSpeed*dir, false)`; with g_projectiles_newton_style 0 that returns `pSpeed*dir` unchanged, so |velocity| = pSpeed*|normalize(dir_folded)+randomvec*spread|, i.e. Base bolts gain a small speed bump. Magnitude: at spread_max 0.03125 the per-axis randomvec()*spread is in [-0.03125,0.03125), so |v| is within ~1-3% of speed in typical draws (worst case ~5% on a fully axis-aligned draw). Aim DIRECTION is effectively identical (port normalizes the same vector QC builds). Flagged so it is not re-reported; fix only if exact projectile-speed parity is required.
- `weapon-hook.primary.reel_pull` — Base applies the rubber-band velocity to pull_entity only `if (!frozen_pulling && !(aiment.flags & FL_PROJECTILE))`; the port guards the projectile-aiment case (Hook.cs:475-478) but not the frozen_pulling case (modeled as always-false since g_balance_grapplehook_pull_frozen is 0). Differs only when pull_frozen is enabled (edge case). Mutator tarzan/pull_entity/velocity_multiplier rewrite from GrappleHookThink not honoured (port's GrappleHookThinkArgs carries no such args); pull_frozen (g_balance_grapplehook_pull_frozen) and the frozen-pull early-remove are not ported; no WarpZone refsys transforms. pusher/pushltime/istypefrag frag attribution on the PULLED aiment (tarzan>=2 reel-to-victim, server/hook.qc:223-225) not set (the shoot-down frag credit IS set, but the reel-pull credit window is separate; out of scope).
- `weapon-hook.primary.remove_reset` — Shoot-down frag attribution sets pusher/pushltime + istypefrag=actor.ButtonChat (Hook.cs:314-318), which is faithful to Base's PHYS_INPUT_BUTTON_CHAT classification (server/hook.qc:303-305).
- `weapon-hook.secondary.gravity_bomb` — Recorded divergence — Base gates the secondary via weapon_prepareattack on sec.refire; the ammo-free port bomb is routed through Weapon.RefireFor(Secondary)=sec.refire so holding ATK2 can't spawn one bomb per tick. Net 3 s cadence matches Base. No Newton-style owner-velocity add to the bomb (g_projectiles_newton_style). No bot_dodge/bot_dodgerating, missile_flags (MIF_SPLASH|MIF_ARC), or damagedbycontents on the bomb (bot AI + lava/contents-detonation; out of scope here). Constant newton_style velocity add (if g_projectiles_newton_style / not modelled)
- `weapon-hook.offhand.mutator` — No useammo ammo_factor toggle on the Hook weapon (handled via fuel top-up instead) — behaviourally equivalent; only matters with g_grappling_hook_useammo on. weaponUseForbidden side of the QC gate (weaponsystem.qc:609-611) is not modelled (WeaponUseForbidden is a stub returning false); the vehicle case IS now gated (GameWorld.cs:2227 `hookPressed && Vehicle is null`).
- `weapon-hook.mutator.vampirehook` — hitsound_damage_dealt HUD/stats accumulator on the drain is omitted (cosmetic; port doesn't carry it). Drains only on a DIRECT hook hit onto an enemy player; with tarzan reel-to-victim a hooked player can also be pulled, which works, but the drain still requires the chain's aiment to be that player (matches Base, since Base also keys on thehook.aiment).
- `weapon-hook.presentation.rope_line` — Constant team textures (particles/hook_red/blue/yellow/pink/white / ModelTint.TeamColor tint (no per-team texture asset))
- `machinegun.audio.impact_ricochet` — Minor: Base plays the ric client-side in CSQC wr_impacteffect; the port plays it server-side on the actor (broadcast to clients). Audibly equivalent. Base's w_issilent is set per-surface (e.g. nodamage/sky); the port approximates it as sky-surface-hit OR trace-miss only, so a few special non-sky 'silent' surfaces would still ping. Cosmetic.
- `minelayer.mine.shootdown` — QC re-faces the mine (angles = vectoangles(velocity)) AFTER the g_projectiles_damage gate + TakeResource; the port performs the re-face inside onHit, BEFORE the gate. With stock g_projectiles_damage -2 + exception=1 the gate always passes for a mine, so behaviorally equivalent — ordering nuance only. Re-grounding a bouncing knocked-loose mine relies on the next BSP touch (OnTouch re-stick) rather than the QC IS_ONGROUND/UNSET_ONGROUND ground-flag toggle in W_MineLayer_Think — the port's movement model has no per-tick onground flag to clear, so this is a model difference, not a behavioral gap.
- `minelayer.obituary` — Resolved via the centralized DeathMessages netname switch rather than per-weapon wr_killmessage/wr_suicidemessage methods, but the resulting notification ids (WEAPON_MINELAYER_MURDER / WEAPON_MINELAYER_SUICIDE) and templates match Base. Liveness is live (the obituary pipeline runs in every match regardless of the MutatorBlocked flag — it fires whenever someone dies to a mine, which requires the weapon to have been granted).
- `minelayer.bot.aim` — The skill<=9 distance/skill random gate uses an approximate per-think frametime constant (0.05f) rather than the true bot-think frametime, so the low-skill long-range detonation probability is approximate (QC random()/distance*300 > frametime*bound(0,(10-skill)*0.2,1)). Behaviorally close; exact probability differs slightly. Over-limit primary-fire suppression (QC wr_aim:382-383 clears BUTTON_ATCK when at the mine limit) is not done inside BotWantsDetonate; it is handled instead by WrThink's over-limit gate (NotifyOverLimit returns before PrepareAttack). Net effect on the live path is equivalent — a bot at the limit cannot lay another mine — but the suppression site differs. Liveness moot in practice while the weapon stays MutatorBlocked, but the dispatch (BotBrain.BotWantsDetonate) IS on the live bot decision path when the weapon is granted.
- `minelayer.player.reset` — Recorded divergence — QC wr_resetplayer zeroes the cached .minelayer_mines HUD counter on each slot at respawn/reset (W_MineLayer_Count is recomputed into it in wr_think). The port has no cached mine counter — CountMines (Minelayer.cs:544) enumerates the live g_mines analogue (FindByClass('mine')) on every read — so there is no stale per-player field to reset. The Minelayer does not override WrResetPlayer (the base no-op is correct). Behaviorally equivalent: the displayed/used mine count is always current.
- `mortar.secondary.attack` — Post-bounce fuse uses a `fuse < deathTime` guard where Base sets nextthink unconditionally; sub-threshold at defaults (0.5 << 20), no observable difference.
- `mortar.explosion.radiusdamage` — Damage_DamageInfo blast-effect networking deferred (shared WeaponSplash gap, render-only). Local-listen play sees the GRENADE_EXPLODE effect + impact sound; what is missing is the dedicated damage-info networked decal/effect packet a dedicated-server client would receive.
- `weapon-porto.fire.launch` — gren.Flags set to EntFlags.Item (256) but commented 'QC FL_PROJECTILE'; the port has no FL_PROJECTILE flag (32768). Mostly inert for porto but the projectile is not flagged as a projectile. gren.dphitcontentsmask (SOLID|BODY|PLAYERCLIP) is not set on the spawned projectile, so the engine bouncemissile move uses its default contents mask rather than Base's; the OnTouch ProbeImpact re-trace recovers the PLAYERCLIP flag independently, so this is mostly cosmetic for the placement decision. right_vector (portal roll axis) carried in AVelocity and reflected on each placement/slick bounce; now reflected about the TRUE impact plane normal recovered by ProbeImpact, so orientation is closer to Base but still derived from a re-trace rather than the original collision plane. Constant dphitcontentsmask (DPCONTENTS_SOLID|BODY|PLAYERCLIP / not set on the projectile) Constant FL_PROJECTILE flag (FL_PROJECTILE (32768) / EntFlags.Item (256) (mislabeled))
- `weapon-porto.touch.portal_spawn` — Recorded divergence — The port realises porto portals as the warpzone subsystem (linked warpzone pair via Warpzones.PlacePortoPortal) rather than a dedicated portal entity with MDL_PORTAL/health/SOLID_BSP. The teleport transform is shared with warpzones. Portal health/damage/lifetime/expire-explode behavior is delegated to (and audited by) the warpzone unit, not this weapon unit. Portal health (200, damageable) / 15 s lifetime / expire (porto/expire) + explode (porto/explode + ROCKET_EXPLODE) and MDL_PORTAL visuals are not realised here (warpzone-unit scope). Constant portal bbox ('-48 -48 -48' / '48 48 48' / n/a (warpzone unit)) Constant g_balance_portal_health (200 / n/a (warpzone unit)) Constant g_balance_portal_lifetime (15 s (refreshed per use) / n/a (warpzone unit))
- `weapon-porto.lifecycle.lifetime_self_destruct` — Throwable-drop nudgeoutofsolid_OrFallback is approximated as always-succeed (the engine extrication isn't reachable from this layer; after a free-flight timeout the origin is in open air) — a minor divergence vs Base which can abort the drop when stuck in solid.
- `weapon-porto.bot.aim` — The port reuses the generic bot-aim path (lead-by-speed + press-ATCK-in-cone) rather than porto's specific bot_aim(speed, 0, lifetime, false, true) call; functionally equivalent for the non-secondary fire decision but not a line-for-line port of the lifetime/teamcheck args.
- `rifle.reload` — MINOR (single-slot OK): CheckAmmoPrimary/Secondary read the clip term from WeaponState(WeaponSlot(0)) hardcoded, while the firing path uses the actual slot — for the standard single-slot case (slot 0) this is correct and live, but a non-zero weapon slot would read the wrong clip. Edge case, not exercised in stock play.
- `rifle.casings` — MINOR transport divergence (functionally equivalent, not byte-faithful): Base uses a dedicated REGISTER_NET_TEMP(casings) (casingtype byte + compressed velocity + 2 angle bytes, with per-client cl_casings/PVS/silent gating and the cl_gunoffset first-person bit); the port instead carries the casing on the shared effect channel as a registered Effect (origin + full velocity, no angle/silent/gunoffset bits). The shell ejects, networks, and bounces on the live path; only the angle/silent/first-person-offset nuances and per-client cvar gating are not reproduced.
- `rifle.bot_aim` — MINOR: QC's wr_aim itself calls bot_aim(1000000,0,0.001) to perform the aim; the port splits responsibility — the generic BotBrain.AimAndDecideFire does the aim/lead/deviation, and BotWantsSecondary only chooses pri vs sec. Equivalent outcome (the rifle's bot_aim tuning is effectively 'always in range, tiny think'), but the exact (1000000,0,0.001) tuning is not threaded into the aim solver per-weapon. Cosmetic w.r.t. fire-mode selection, which is what wr_aim's rifle-specific behavior is about.
- `rifle.resetplayer` — Recorded divergence — QC's wr_resetplayer resets rifle_accumulator across ALL weapon slots on respawn; the port has no per-weapon respawn-reset hook, so — like Vortex/OkNex/Tuba's setup seed — the reset is attached to the weapon switch-in path (WrSetup, dispatched by WeaponFireDriver.cs:281) and seeds only the equipped slot. Inert at stock bursttime 0 (time - 0 already bounds the accumulator), and the accumulator self-corrects via the Bound() at the top of WrThink within one tick, so the per-slot-on-switch-in placement is behaviorally faithful. A switch-away-and-back re-seeds (benign, same documented convention as Vortex).
- `weapon-seeker.wr_think_dispatch` — Forced-reload top-of-wr_think branch (g_balance_seeker_reload_ammo) not ported; dormant since reload_ammo defaults 0.
- `weapon-seeker.missile_homing` — Trace-end selection has a minor literal divergence: Base's `vdist(this.origin + olddir*this.wait, <, dist)` compares the absolute world-position length to dist; the port (Seeker.cs:286) compares the DELTA length (==waitCell[0] < dist). The port's reading is the sensible one and the adaptive-trace + trace_plane_normal blend (1-fraction) are otherwise bit-faithful — practically equivalent steering. Proxy detonation (missile_proxy/_delay/_maxrange) not ported — default OFF, so no observable divergence at stock balance (dormant).
- `weapon-seeker.volley_controller` — Switch-away gate keys on owner.ActiveWeaponId (the currently-equipped weapon) where Base keys on m_switchweapon (the QUEUED switch weapon); these differ only during the one-tick weapon-switch transition, so a port volley may fire one extra missile vs Base at the exact moment a switch is initiated. Negligible in practice.
- `weapon-seeker.type1_attack` — Reachable only when g_balance_seeker_type=1 (non-default layout); under the default type 0 this path is unused (matches Base).
- `weapon-shotgun.primary.attack_fan` — Antilag takeback is still per-pellet (inside FireBullet/LagComp.Begin) rather than ONCE for the whole volley as Base does outside the loop (shotgun.qc:23-26 antilag_takeback_all); functionally-equivalent hit-reg but redundant rewind work per pellet.
- `weapon-shotgun.primary.setupshot_recoil` — Antilag takeback at the SetupShot level is bracketed by LagComp.Begin/End around the trueaim + muzzle-nudge traces (g_antilag==2); warpzones in the trueaim are partially handled (direction kept straight on a portal cross). Accuracy FIRED credit IS wired (WeaponAccuracyEvents.Fired).
- `weapon-shotgun.ammo.checkammo_reload` — wr_checkammo1/2 in Base also OR the clip load (weapon_load[m_id] >= ammo) (shotgun.qc:357,370); the port's CheckAmmoPrimary/Secondary DO include the GetWeaponLoad OR-term (Shotgun.cs:366-367,374-375), so the clip-load term is now present. reload_ammo defaults 0 so no clip is used by default.
- `weapon-shotgun.fx.muzzle_impact` — Recorded divergence — Impact effect is emitted server-side per pellet (an extra WorldOnly trace in Shotgun.Attack finds the surface, then WeaponFiring.BulletImpactFx emits SHOTGUN_IMPACT) rather than from a CSQC wr_impacteffect, because the port has no CSQC layer. The effect sink is live on both ends (ServerNet EffectNetSink networks it; ClientWorld RenderSink renders it). SHOTGUN_IMPACT is still emitted per pellet server-side (~12/shot) via a second WorldOnly trace, not the single CSQC w_org + w_backoff*2 impact; it emits on the world hit point even when the pellet struck a player (the extra trace is WorldOnly, ignoring entities). Constant impact effect emission (EFFECT_SHOTGUN_IMPACT in CSQC wr_impacteffect, once per networked impact / SHOTGUN_IMPACT emitted server-side per pellet via an EXTRA WorldOnly trace (~12/shot))
- `weapon-shotgun.menu.describe` — The two dynamic trailing lines (W_Guide_Keybinds player keybinds + W_Guide_DPS_primaryMultishot DPS metric, shotgun.qc:427) are not part of GuideDescription — omitted by design, exactly as the Electro/Tuba guides omit them (those helpers belong to the guide page, not the static prose).
- `weapon-tuba.deathtype.instrument_bits` — The Accordion/Klein Bottle obituary branches are reachable only once the player has cycled instruments via reload (now live, see weapon-tuba.reload.instrument_cycle) — no longer doubly-dead.
- `weapon-tuba.reload.instrument_cycle` — Constant weaponname tuba/akordeon/kleinbottle (set in wr_reload (tuba.qc:370,374,378) / not set (drives CSQC viewmodel only; render-only, see notes))
- `weapon-tuba.pitch.getnote` — Recorded divergence — Movement-key bits and crouch/jump/team tuning aren't carried to the headless weapon entity, so the port derives the movestate from horizontal velocity vs facing and applies only the +7 secondary shift. Pitch feeds ONLY the (still mostly-unported/silent) note sound, so the divergence has no gameplay effect today; flagged so it isn't re-reported. Would need real input bits to be faithful once audio lands. Chosen note differs from Base (velocity proxy, missing crouch/jump/team shifts). Now AUDIBLE since weapon-tuba.audio.note_sound plays a sustained pitched loop (PitchStepBaseNote snaps to a real sample + shifts the residual pitch), so this divergence changes which pitch is heard — but still never which file resolves (always a real sample) and has no gameplay effect (pitch feeds only the sound). Constant movestate 3x3 note table (from movement.x/.y keys / from horizontal velocity vs facing) Constant crouch shift (-12 / none) Constant jump shift (+12 / none) Constant team/player tuning (+3 (team 2/4 or clientcolors&1) / none)
- `weapon-tuba.note.sustain_lifetime` — Constant keep-alive teleport_time (time + refire*2*W_WeaponRateFactor / time + refire*2 (MaxHealth, no consumer))
- `weapon-tuba.fx.smoke_ring` — Constant ring origin (gettaginfo(weapon, 0) (weapon tag) / actor.Origin + actor.ViewOfs (eye approximation))
- `weapon-tuba.audio.note_sound` — Constant cl_tuba_fadetime (0.25 (Ent_TubaNote_Think ramps volume down on release) / not honored — NoteOff Stops the loop cleanly, no fade-out ramp)
- `weapon-tuba.describe.guide` — The two dynamic trailing paragraphs (W_Guide_Keybinds player keybinds + W_Guide_DPS damage-per-second line) are not part of GuideDescription — omitted exactly as the Electro guide omits them (they belong to the guide page, not the static prose). Constant dynamic Keybinds/DPS trailing PARs (W_Guide_Keybinds + W_Guide_DPS_onlyOne_unnamed (tuba.qc:609-610) / omitted by design (owned by the guide page, matching the Electro guide precedent))
- `weapon-vaporizer.primary.beam_particle` — Constant beam_tex (particles/gauntletbeam (hit) / particles/lgbeam / VAPORIZER_BEAM_HIT (TE_TEI_G3_HIT) / VAPORIZER_BEAM (TE_TEI_G3)) Constant cl_vaporizerbeam_lifetime (0.8 / engine trail default)
- `weapon-vaporizer.primary.impact_effect` — Impact is emitted server-side at the railgun world-trace endpoint rather than via the CSQC wr_impacteffect (w_org + w_backoff*2 offset) path — visually equivalent, minor positional nuance; w_issilent gate not modeled (port always plays the sound).
- `weapon-vaporizer.rm.laser_barrage` — RM-laser rapid stream omits the cosmetic weapon_thinkf WFRAME_FIRE2 rapid_animtime view-model frame (vaporizer.qc:334, commented out in Base too); purely a first-person anim frame, no gameplay/visual-bolt effect.
- `weapon-vaporizer.menu.describe` — The trailing W_Guide_Keybinds(this) + W_Guide_DPS_onlyOne lines of describe() (keybind hints + a computed DPS figure) are not reproduced — those helpers aren't ported. The four prose paragraphs ARE present. Cosmetic/menu-only. Constant W_Guide_Keybinds / W_Guide_DPS_onlyOne (appended by describe() / not reproduced (guide-page helpers unported))
- `weapon-vortex.fx.beam` — Constant beam alpha/spacing scale (sqrt(charge) / none (constant))
- `weapon-vortex.ammo.reload_checkammo` — Recorded divergence — Charge / chargepool seed runs on weapon switch-in (WrSetup) instead of respawn (QC wr_resetplayer): the port has no per-weapon respawn-reset hook, so the seed is attached to the switch-in path. Documented in Vortex.cs:300-311. Effect: switching away and back re-seeds vortex_charge to charge_start (0.5); generally favorable (lowers, never raises, a full charge) and not exploitable.
- `weapon-vortex.achievements` — Multi-pierce divergence (minor): QC's ++impressive_hits runs PER pierced cross-team victim inside the Damage loop, so a single rail that pierces two enemies counts as 2; the port re-derives impressive from only the FIRST pierced victim (Vortex.cs Announce uses FireRailgunBullet's returned `first`). For the impressive every-other STREAK this is observationally identical (impressive_hits != 0 is all that matters); the only edge case where it differs is a yoda where the first pierced enemy is grounded but a later-pierced one is airborne — extremely rare. Not a stock-balance concern.
